Please help me to diagnose and fix problem with this proxy and use of the `agy-cli-wrapper` backend. During each longer session which involves multiple Agy wrapper uses this Python proxy eventually freezes. I mean either wrapper freezes or proxy itself due to some inter-op glitch. Initial turns, sometimes even hundreds of them seem working perfectly fine. But then suddenly clients stall when they try to init new session using this connector. This may be an important clue as this does not seem to happen mid-session, rather when they try to spawn new subagents using `agy-cli-wrapper` as the subagen'ts backend. You may use the most recent log file of the proxy and CBOR wire capture (if needed, not sure if needed here, your call) to diagnose it. Below I paste tail of the server console. 29: ACP_PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1
30: # Match go-agy-acp-wrapper's 4-hour per-turn ceiling. This is both the ACP
31: # JSON-RPC wait and --timeout-seconds forwarded to the wrapper; agy's own
32: # --print-timeout default is only 5 minutes.
33: DEFAULT_AGY_PROCESS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 14400.0
34: CANONICAL_MODEL_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(
35:     r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]*/[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._-]*$"
36: )
37: 
38: 
39: def canonicalize_agy_model_id(native_id: str) -> str | None:
40:     """Collapse an agy-native effort variant into a canonical provider model ID."""
41:     model = native_id.strip()
42:     if not model or any(char.isspace() for char in model):
43:         return None
44:     for suffix in ("-low", "-medium", "-high"):
45:         if model.endswith(suffix):
46:             model = model[: -len(suffix)]
47:             break
48:     if model.endswith("-thinking"):
49:         model = model[: -len("-thinking")]
50: 
51:     if model.startswith("gemini-"):
52:         return f"google/{model}"
53:     if model.startswith("claude-"):
54:         parts = model.split("-")
55:         for index in range(1, len(parts) - 1):
56:             if parts[index].isdigit() and parts[index + 1].isdigit():
57:                 parts[index] = f"{parts[index]}.{parts[index + 1]}"
58:                 del parts[index + 1]
59:                 break
60:         return f"anthropic/{'-'.join(parts)}"
61:     if model.startswith("gpt-"):
62:         return f"openai/{model}"
63:     return None
64: 
65: 
66: def parse_agy_models_catalog(stdout: str) -> list[str]:
67:     """Parse and deduplicate canonical identities from ``agy models`` output."""
68:     models: list[str] = []
69:     seen: set[str] = set()
70:     for line in stdout.splitlines():
71:         canonical = canonicalize_agy_model_id(line)
72:         if canonical is None or canonical in seen:
73:             continue
74:         seen.add(canonical)
75:         models.append(canonical)
76:     return models
77: 
78: 
79: def parse_wrapper_model_catalog(stdout: str) -> list[str]:
80:     """Parse canonical model IDs emitted by the wrapper."""
81:     models: list[str] = []
82:     seen: set[str] = set()
83:     for line in stdout.splitlines():
84:         model = line.strip()
85:         if not CANONICAL_MODEL_ID_PATTERN.fullmatch(model) or model in seen:
86:             continue
87:         seen.add(model)
88:         models.append(model)
89:     return models
90: 
91: 
92: def resolve_agy_executable(configured: str | None) -> str | None:
93:     for candidate in (
94:         (configured or "").strip(),
95:         os.environ.get("AGY_BINARY", "").strip(),
96:         "agy",
97:         "agy.exe",
98:         "agy.cmd",
99:     ):
100:         if not candidate:
101:             continue
102:         path = Path(candidate)
103:         if path.is_file():
104:             return str(path.resolve())
105:         resolved = shutil.which(candidate)
106:         if resolved:
107:             return resolved
108:     return None
109: 
110: 
111: def resolve_agy_acp_wrapper_executable(configured: str | None) -> str | None:
112:     """Resolve go-agy-acp-wrapper binary suitable for subprocess.Popen."""
113:     for candidate in (
114:         (configured or "").strip(),
115:         os.environ.get("AGY_ACP_WRAPPER_BIN", "").strip(),
116:     ):
117:         if not candidate:
118:             continue
119:         p = Path(candidate)
120:         if p.is_file():
121:             return str(p.resolve())
122:         resolved = shutil.which(candidate)
123:         if resolved:
124:             return resolved
125:     for name in ("go-agy-acp-wrapper", "go-agy-acp-wrapper.exe"):
126:         resolved = shutil.which(name)
127:         if resolved:
128:             return resolved
129:     for dev_path in (
130:         Path("C:/Users/Mateusz/source/repos/go-agy-acp-wrapper/go-agy-acp-wrapper.exe"),
131:         Path.home() / "source/repos/go-agy-acp-wrapper/go-agy-acp-wrapper.exe",
132:     ):
133:         if dev_path.is_file():
134:             return str(dev_path.resolve())
135:     return None
136: 
137: 
138: def build_agy_acp_wrapper_command(
139:     executable: str,
140:     *,
141:     agy_binary: str | None,
142:     model: str | None,
143:     timeout_seconds: int | None,
144:     skip_permissions: bool,
145:     extra_args: Sequence[str] | None,
146: ) -> list[str]:
147:     cmd = [executable]
148:     if agy_binary:
149:         cmd.extend(["--agy-binary", agy_binary])
150:     if model and model != "auto":
151:         cmd.extend(["--model", model])
152:     if timeout_seconds is not None and timeout_seconds > 0:
153:         cmd.extend(["--timeout-seconds", str(timeout_seconds)])
154:     cmd.append("--skip-permissions" if skip_permissions else "--no-skip-permissions")
155:     if extra_args:
156:         cmd.extend(list(extra_args))
157:     return cmd
158:

161:     executable: str,
162:     *,
163:     agy_binary: str | None,
164: ) -> list[str]:
165:     """Build the wrapper's non-interactive canonical catalog command."""
166:     cmd = [executable, "--list-models"]
167:     if agy_binary:

169:     return cmd

171: 
172: async def run_agy_model_catalog_probe(
173:     command: list[str], *, timeout: float
174: ) -> tuple[int, bytes, bytes]:
175:     """Run a wrapper probe without requiring asyncio subprocess support.
176: 
177:     The proxy uses ``WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy`` to avoid shutdown hangs,
178:     and that loop cannot create subprocess transports. Keep short-lived wrapper
179:     probes on the same ``subprocess.Popen`` path used by ACP runtimes.
180:     """
181:     process = subprocess.Popen(
182:         command,
183:         stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
184:         stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
185:         shell=False,
186:     )
187:     communicate_task = asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(process.communicate))
188:     try:
189:         stdout, stderr = await asyncio.wait_for(
190:             asyncio.shield(communicate_task), timeout
191:         )
192:     except BaseException:
193:         if process.poll() is None:
194:             process.kill()
195:         await communicate_task
196:         raise
197:     return process.returncode or 0, stdout, stderr
198:

212:         )
213:         auto_download = bool(extra.get("wrapper_auto_download", True))
214:         if os.environ.get("AGY_ACP_WRAPPER_AUTO_DOWNLOAD", "").strip().lower() in {
215:             "0",
216:             "false",
217:             "no",
218:             "off",
219:         }:
220:             auto_download = False
221:         if executable is None and auto_download:
222:             cache_dir = extra.get("wrapper_cache_dir")
223:             async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
224:                 executable = await install_latest_wrapper(
225:                     client,
226:                     cache_dir=(
227:                         Path(str(cache_dir)).expanduser() if cache_dir else None
228:                     ),
229:                 )
230:         if executable is None:
231:             return BackendModelEnumeration.unavailable(
232:                 instance_name=instance_name,
233:                 connector="agy-cli-acp",
234:                 source="agy_wrapper",
235:                 error_code="wrapper_not_found",
236:                 instance_pinned=True,
237:             )
238: 
239:         agy_binary = extra.get("agy_binary") or os.environ.get("AGY_BINARY")
240:         command = build_agy_model_catalog_command(
241:             executable,
242:             agy_binary=str(agy_binary).strip() if agy_binary else None,
243:         )
244:         timeout = float(extra.get("model_discovery_timeout_seconds", 15.0))
245:         try:
246:             returncode, stdout, _ = await run_agy_model_catalog_probe(
247:                 command, timeout=timeout
248:             )
249:         except (TimeoutError, FileNotFoundError, OSError):
250:             return BackendModelEnumeration.unavailable(
251:                 instance_name=instance_name,
252:                 connector="agy-cli-acp",
253:                 source="agy_wrapper",
254:                 error_code="catalog_probe_failed",
255:                 instance_pinned=True,
256:             )
257:         models = (
258:             parse_wrapper_model_catalog(stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
259:             if returncode == 0
260:             else []
261:         )
262:         if not models:
263:             return BackendModelEnumeration.unavailable(
264:                 instance_name=instance_name,
265:                 connector="agy-cli-acp",
266:                 source="agy_wrapper",
267:                 error_code="catalog_unavailable",
268:                 instance_pinned=True,
269:             )
270:         return BackendModelEnumeration.available(
271:             instance_name=instance_name,
272:             connector="agy-cli-acp",
273:             models=models,
274:             source="agy_wrapper",
275:             instance_pinned=True,
276:         )

279: class AgyCliAcpConnector(BaseAcpConnector[ACPProcessRuntime]):
280:     """Experimental Antigravity CLI backend through go-agy-acp-wrapper."""
281: 
282:     backend_type: str = "agy-cli-acp"

284:     requires_explicit_workspace: bool = True

286:     def __init__(
287:         self,
288:         client: httpx.AsyncClient,
289:         config: AppConfig,
290:         translation_service: TranslationService,
291:         **_: Any,
292:     ) -> None:
293:         super().__init__(config, translation_service=translation_service)
294:         self.client = client
295:         self.name = "agy-cli-acp"
296:         self._wrapper_executable = "go-agy-acp-wrapper"
297:         self._agy_binary: str | None = None
298:         self._model = "google/gemini-3.5-flash"
299:         self._configured_models: list[str] = []
300:         self._skip_permissions = True
301:         self._mcp_servers: list[Any] = []
302:         self._extra_wrapper_args: list[str] = []
303:         self._process_timeout = DEFAULT_AGY_PROCESS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS

305:     async def initialize(self, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
306:         try:
307:             workspace, cfg_err = resolve_backend_init_acp_workspace(
308:                 project_dir=kwargs.get("project_dir"),
309:                 workspace_path=kwargs.get("workspace_path"),
310:                 env_workspace=os.getenv("AGY_CLI_WORKSPACE"),
311:                 env_source_label="AGY_CLI_WORKSPACE",
312:                 is_usable=self._is_usable_directory,
313:             )
314:             if cfg_err:
315:                 raise ConfigurationError(
316:                     message=cfg_err,
317:                     details={"error_code": "agy_cli_acp_workspace_invalid"},
318:                 )
319:             self._default_project_dir = workspace
320: 
321:             wrapper = kwargs.get("wrapper_executable") or kwargs.get(
322:                 "agy_acp_wrapper_executable"
323:             )
324:             self._wrapper_executable = str(wrapper or self._wrapper_executable)
325:             agy_binary = kwargs.get("agy_binary") or os.environ.get("AGY_BINARY")
326:             self._agy_binary = str(agy_binary).strip() if agy_binary else None
327:             configured_model = str(kwargs.get("model") or self._model)
328:             self._model = strip_vendor_prefix(configured_model, self.VENDOR_PREFIX)
329:             configured_models = kwargs.get("models")
330:             self._configured_models = (
331:                 [
332:                     str(model).strip()
333:                     for model in configured_models
334:                     if str(model).strip()
335:                 ]
336:                 if isinstance(configured_models, list)
337:                 else []
338:             )
339:             self._skip_permissions = bool(
340:                 kwargs.get("skip_permissions", self._skip_permissions)
341:             )
342:             self._process_timeout = float(
343:                 kwargs.get("process_timeout", self._process_timeout)
344:             )
345:             self._idle_timeout = float(kwargs.get("idle_timeout", self._idle_timeout))
346:             mcp = kwargs.get("mcp_servers", [])
347:             self._mcp_servers = mcp if isinstance(mcp, list) else []
348:             extra = kwargs.get("wrapper_extra_args") or kwargs.get(
349:                 "agy_acp_wrapper_extra_args"
350:             )
351:             if isinstance(extra, list):
352:                 self._extra_wrapper_args = [str(x) for x in extra]
353:             elif isinstance(extra, str) and extra.strip():
354:                 self._extra_wrapper_args = [extra.strip()]
355:             else:
356:                 self._extra_wrapper_args = []
357: 
358:             resolved = resolve_agy_acp_wrapper_executable(self._wrapper_executable)
359:             auto_download = bool(kwargs.get("wrapper_auto_download", True))
360:             if os.environ.get("AGY_ACP_WRAPPER_AUTO_DOWNLOAD", "").strip().lower() in {
361:                 "0",
362:                 "false",
363:                 "no",
364:                 "off",
365:             }:
366:                 auto_download = False
367:             if resolved is None and auto_download:
368:                 cache_dir = kwargs.get("wrapper_cache_dir")
369:                 resolved = await install_latest_wrapper(
370:                     self.client,
371:                     cache_dir=Path(str(cache_dir)).expanduser() if cache_dir else None,
372:                 )
373:             if resolved is None:
374:                 raise ConfigurationError(
375:                     message="go-agy-acp-wrapper executable not found",
376:                     details={
377:                         "configured": self._wrapper_executable,
378:                         "hint": "Enable wrapper_auto_download, put the wrapper on PATH, "
379:                         "or set AGY_ACP_WRAPPER_BIN / wrapper_executable.",
380:                     },
381:                 )
382:             self._wrapper_executable = resolved
383: 
384:             if not await self._check_wrapper_available():
385:                 raise ConfigurationError(
386:                     message=f"go-agy-acp-wrapper not callable: {self._wrapper_executable}",
387:                     details={"executable": self._wrapper_executable},
388:                 )
389: 
390:             if not self._configured_models:
391:                 self._configured_models = await self._discover_models()
392: 
393:             self._validation_errors = []
394:             self._initialization_failed = False
395:             self.is_functional = True
396:         except Exception:
397:             self._initialization_failed = True
398:             self.is_functional = False
399:             self._validation_errors = ["agy-cli-acp initialization failed"]
400:             raise
401: 
402:     async def _run_probe(
403:         self, command: list[str], *, timeout: float
404:     ) -> tuple[int, bytes, bytes]:
405:         """Run a short-lived probe without leaking executor threads on cancellation."""
406:         return await run_agy_model_catalog_probe(command, timeout=timeout)
407: 
408:     async def _discover_models(self) -> list[str]:
409:         command = build_agy_model_catalog_command(
410:             self._wrapper_executable,
411:             agy_binary=self._agy_binary,
412:         )
413:         try:
414:             returncode, stdout, stderr = await self._run_probe(command, timeout=15)
415:         except (TimeoutError, FileNotFoundError, OSError):
416:             logger.warning("agy model discovery failed", exc_info=True)
417:             return []
418:         if returncode != 0:
419:             logger.warning(
420:                 "agy wrapper model discovery exited with code %s: %s",
421:                 returncode,
422:                 stderr.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip(),
423:             )
424:             return []
425:         return parse_wrapper_model_catalog(stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
426: 
427:     async def _check_wrapper_available(self) -> bool:
428:         try:
429:             returncode, _, _ = await self._run_probe(
430:                 [self._wrapper_executable, "--version"], timeout=10
431:             )
432:             return returncode == 0
433:         except (TimeoutError, FileNotFoundError, OSError):
434:             return False

436:     async def _build_subprocess_command(self, runtime: ACPProcessRuntime) -> list[str]:
437:         timeout = int(self._process_timeout) if self._process_timeout > 0 else None
438:         return build_agy_acp_wrapper_command(
439:             self._wrapper_executable,
440:             agy_binary=self._agy_binary,
441:             model=runtime.model,
442:             timeout_seconds=timeout,
443:             skip_permissions=self._skip_permissions,
444:             extra_args=self._extra_wrapper_args,
445:         )

447:     def _create_runtime(
448:         self, project_dir: Path, model: str, client_session_id: str = "default"
449:     ) -> ACPProcessRuntime:
450:         return ACPProcessRuntime(
451:             project_dir=project_dir,
452:             model=model,
453:             client_session_id=client_session_id,
454:             process_lock=asyncio.Lock(),
455:             request_lock=asyncio.Lock(),
456:             cancellation_lock=asyncio.Lock(),
457:             cancellation_event=asyncio.Event(),

460:     async def _perform_handshake(self, runtime: ACPProcessRuntime) -> None:
461:         initialize_id = await self._send_jsonrpc_message(
462:             runtime,
463:             "initialize",
464:             {
465:                 "protocolVersion": ACP_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
466:                 "clientCapabilities": {
467:                     "fs": {"readTextFile": False, "writeTextFile": False},
468:                     "terminal": False,
469:                 },
470:                 "clientInfo": {
471:                     "name": "llm-interactive-proxy",
472:                     "version": "1",
473:                 },
474:             },
475:         )
476:         initialize_response = await self._await_response(runtime, initialize_id)
477:         if initialize_response.is_error and initialize_response.error is not None:
478:             raise BackendError(
479:                 message=f"agy ACP initialize failed: {initialize_response.error.message}",
480:                 details=initialize_response.error.model_dump(),
481:             )
482: 
483:         auth_id = await self._send_jsonrpc_message(
484:             runtime,
485:             "authenticate",
486:             {"methodId": "agy"},
487:         )
488:         auth_response = await self._await_response(runtime, auth_id)
489:         if auth_response.is_error and auth_response.error is not None:
490:             raise BackendError(
491:                 message=f"agy ACP authenticate failed: {auth_response.error.message}",
492:                 details=auth_response.error.model_dump(),
493:             )
494: 
495:         session_new_id = await self._send_jsonrpc_message(
496:             runtime,
497:             "session/new",
498:             {
499:                 "cwd": str(runtime.project_dir),
500:                 "mcpServers": list(self._mcp_servers),
501:             },
502:         )
503:         session_new_response = await self._await_response(runtime, session_new_id)
504:         if session_new_response.is_error and session_new_response.error is not None:
505:             raise BackendError(
506:                 message=f"agy ACP session/new failed: {session_new_response.error.message}",
507:                 details=session_new_response.error.model_dump(),
508:             )
509: 
510:         session_result = session_new_response.result or {}
511:         session_id = session_result.get("sessionId")
512:         if not isinstance(session_id, str) or not session_id.strip():
513:             raise BackendError(
514:                 message="agy ACP session/new did not return a sessionId",
515:                 details={"result": session_result},
516:             )
517: 
518:         runtime.session_id = session_id
519:         runtime.initialized = True
520: 
521:     async def _prepare_turn_request_locked(
522:         self,
523:         runtime: ACPProcessRuntime,
524:         request: Any,
525:     ) -> tuple[int, str]:
526:         # The effort option is session-scoped, so establish the ACP session before
527:         # sending session/set_config_option. The base implementation's repeated
528:         # spawn/initialize calls are idempotent.
529:         await self._spawn_process(runtime)
530:         await self._initialize_runtime(runtime)
531: 
532:         effort = getattr(request.request, "reasoning_effort", None)
533:         if not effort:
534:             extra_body = getattr(request.request, "extra_body", None)
535:             if isinstance(extra_body, dict):
536:                 effort = extra_body.get("reasoning_effort")
537:         if isinstance(effort, str) and effort.strip():
538:             config_id = await self._send_jsonrpc_message(
539:                 runtime,
540:                 "session/set_config_option",
541:                 {
542:                     "sessionId": runtime.session_id,
543:                     "configId": "reasoning_effort",
544:                     "value": effort.strip().lower(),
545:                 },
546:             )
547:             response = await self._await_response(runtime, config_id)
548:             if response.is_error and response.error is not None:
549:                 raise BackendError(
550:                     message=f"agy ACP reasoning effort failed: {response.error.message}",
551:                     details=response.error.model_dump(),
552:                 )
553:         return await super()._prepare_turn_request_locked(runtime, request)
554: 
555:     async def _handle_server_request(
556:         self, runtime: ACPProcessRuntime, msg: ACPNotification
557:     ) -> None:
558:         assert msg.id is not None
559:         method = msg.method or ""
560:         if method.startswith("agy/"):
561:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.WARNING):
562:                 logger.warning(
563:                     "Unhandled agy ACP extension %s; replying empty result",
564:                     method,
565:                 )
566:             await self._send_jsonrpc_result(runtime, msg.id, {})
567:             return
568:         await self._write_json_line(
569:             runtime,
570:             {
571:                 "jsonrpc": "2.0",
572:                 "id": msg.id,
573:                 "error": {"code": -32601, "message": f"Method not handled: {method}"},
574:             },
575:         )
576: 
577:     def get_available_models(self) -> list[str]:
578:         return list(self._configured_models)

96: def _format_acp_error(error: ACPError) -> str:
97:     """Include JSON-RPC error data that generic ACP messages otherwise hide."""
98:     message = error.message.strip() or "Unknown ACP error"
99:     detail: str | None = None
100:     if isinstance(error.data, str):
101:         detail = error.data.strip()
102:     elif isinstance(error.data, dict):
103:         for key in ("error", "message", "details"):
104:             value = error.data.get(key)
105:             if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
106:                 detail = value.strip()
107:                 break
108: 
109:     if detail and detail.casefold() not in message.casefold():
110:         return f"{message}: {detail}"
111:     return message
112:

114: _STALE_ACP_KILL_DELAY_MAX_SECONDS = 604800.0  # 7 days
115: # Increment when the canonicalization used for ACP history prefix hashes changes.
116: HISTORY_PREFIX_HASH_VERSION = 2
117: 
118: #: The runtime type a connector manages. Bound to :class:`ACPProcessRuntime` so
119: #: base-class code only touches common fields, while subclasses parameterize it
120: #: with a protocol-specific runtime (e.g. :class:`CodexAppServerRuntime`) and
121: #: get type-safe access to the extra fields inside their overrides.
122: RuntimeT = TypeVar("RuntimeT", bound=ACPProcessRuntime)
123: 
124: 
125: def _canonical_chat_message_for_history_hash(message: ChatMessage) -> dict[str, Any]:
126:     """Return stable identity fields for divergence detection.
127: 
128:     Uses :meth:`ChatMessage.to_dict` so fields such as ``metadata`` that are not
129:     part of the visible transcript do not spuriously invalidate the prefix hash.
130:     """
131: 
132:     return message.to_dict()
133: 
134: 
135: def _hash_chat_messages_prefix_stable(
136:     messages: Sequence[ChatMessage], end_exclusive: int
137: ) -> str:
138:     """SHA-256 hex digest of the first ``end_exclusive`` messages (conversation prefix)."""
139: 
140:     if end_exclusive <= 0:
141:         return hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest()
142:     slice_msgs = messages[:end_exclusive]
143:     payload = [_canonical_chat_message_for_history_hash(m) for m in slice_msgs]
144:     canonical = json.dumps(
145:         {"m": payload, "v": HISTORY_PREFIX_HASH_VERSION},
146:         sort_keys=True,
147:         separators=(",", ":"),
148:     )
149:     return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
150:

152: class _RuntimeCancellable(Generic[RuntimeT]):
153:     __slots__ = ("_connector", "_runtime", "_prompt_request_id")
154: 
155:     def __init__(
156:         self,
157:         connector: BaseAcpConnector[RuntimeT],
158:         runtime: RuntimeT,
159:         prompt_request_id: int,
160:     ) -> None:
161:         self._connector = connector
162:         self._runtime = runtime
163:         self._prompt_request_id = prompt_request_id
164: 
165:     def cancel(self) -> None:
166:         if self._runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
167:             self._runtime.cancellation_event.set()
168:         if self._runtime.cancellation_lock is None:
169:             return
170:         try:
171:             loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
172:         except RuntimeError:
173:             return
174:         connector = cast(Any, self._connector)
175:         task = loop.create_task(
176:             connector._cancel_active_request(self._runtime, self._prompt_request_id)
177:         )
178:         task.add_done_callback(lambda t: t.exception() if not t.cancelled() else None)
179:

181: class BaseAcpConnector(LLMBackend, UsageCalculationMixin, ABC, Generic[RuntimeT]):
182:     """Base class for ACP-based connectors."""
183: 
184:     VENDOR_PREFIX: str
185:     requires_explicit_workspace: bool = False
186: 
187:     def __init__(
188:         self, config: Any, translation_service: Any = None, **kwargs: Any
189:     ) -> None:
190:         super().__init__(config)
191:         self.translation_service = translation_service
192:         self.is_functional = False
193:         self._initialization_failed = False
194:         self._validation_errors: list[str] = []
195:         self._default_project_dir: Path | None = None
196:         self._model = "auto"
197:         self._process_timeout = DEFAULT_PROCESS_TIMEOUT
198:         self._idle_timeout = DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT
199:         self._acp_tool_heartbeat_seconds = DEFAULT_ACP_TOOL_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS
200:         self._runtime_pool_lock = asyncio.Lock()
201:         self._runtimes: dict[tuple[str, str, str], RuntimeT] = {}
202: 
203:     @property
204:     def has_static_credentials(self) -> bool:
205:         return False
206: 
207:     def is_backend_functional(self) -> bool:
208:         return (
209:             self.is_functional
210:             and not self._initialization_failed
211:             and len(self._validation_errors) == 0
212:         )
213: 
214:     def _is_backend_functional_internal(self) -> bool:
215:         return self.is_backend_functional()
216: 
217:     def get_validation_errors(self) -> list[str]:
218:         return self._validation_errors.copy()
219: 
220:     def _resolve_stream_keepalive_interval(self) -> float:
221:         """Seconds between idle keepalive chunks while waiting for ACP subprocess output."""
222:         cfg = getattr(self, "config", None)
223:         failure_handling = getattr(cfg, "failure_handling", None)
224:         interval = getattr(failure_handling, "keepalive_interval", None)
225:         if isinstance(interval, int | float) and float(interval) > 0:
226:             return float(interval)
227:         return 12.0
228: 
229:     def _stale_acp_kill_enabled(self) -> bool:
230:         return not bool(getattr(self.config, "disable_stale_acp_agent_kills", False))
231: 
232:     def _stale_acp_kill_delay_seconds(self) -> float:
233:         raw = getattr(
234:             self.config,
235:             "stale_acp_agent_kill_idle_seconds",
236:             DEFAULT_STALE_ACP_AGENT_KILL_IDLE_SECONDS,
237:         )
238:         if isinstance(raw, bool) or not isinstance(raw, int | float):
239:             return DEFAULT_STALE_ACP_AGENT_KILL_IDLE_SECONDS
240:         v = float(raw)
241:         if v != v:  # NaN
242:             return DEFAULT_STALE_ACP_AGENT_KILL_IDLE_SECONDS
243:         return max(1.0, min(v, _STALE_ACP_KILL_DELAY_MAX_SECONDS))
244: 
245:     async def _cancel_stale_kill_timer(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
246:         task = runtime.stale_kill_task
247:         if task is None:
248:             return
249:         current = asyncio.current_task()
250:         if task is current:
251:             runtime.stale_kill_task = None
252:             return
253:         if not task.done():
254:             task.cancel()
255:             with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
256:                 await task
257:         runtime.stale_kill_task = None
258: 
259:     async def _schedule_stale_kill_after_turn(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
260:         await self._cancel_stale_kill_timer(runtime)
261:         if not self._stale_acp_kill_enabled():
262:             return
263:         delay = self._stale_acp_kill_delay_seconds()
264:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
265:             logger.info(
266:                 "Scheduling stale ACP agent kill in %.0fs (backend=%s project=%s "
267:                 "model=%s client_session=%s)",
268:                 delay,
269:                 self.backend_type,
270:                 runtime.project_dir,
271:                 runtime.model,
272:                 runtime.client_session_id,
273:             )
274:         connector = self
275:         runtime_ref = runtime
276: 
277:         async def _run() -> None:
278:             try:
279:                 try:
280:                     await asyncio.sleep(delay)
281:                 except asyncio.CancelledError:
282:                     return
283:                 if not connector._stale_acp_kill_enabled():
284:                     return
285:                 proc = runtime_ref.process
286:                 if proc is None or proc.poll() is not None:
287:                     return
288:                 ident = runtime_ref.acp_subprocess_identity
289:                 if ident is not None and not stale_kill_still_same_os_process(
290:                     proc, ident
291:                 ):
292:                     if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
293:                         logger.info(
294:                             "Stale ACP kill skipped: OS process identity mismatch "
295:                             "(backend=%s pid=%s project=%s)",
296:                             connector.backend_type,
297:                             getattr(proc, "pid", None),
298:                             runtime_ref.project_dir,
299:                         )
300:                     return
301:                 if ident is None and logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
302:                     logger.debug(
303:                         "ACP stale kill has no subprocess identity fingerprint "
304:                         "(backend=%s pid=%s); relying on subprocess handle state only",
305:                         connector.backend_type,
306:                         getattr(proc, "pid", None),
307:                     )
308:                 req_lock = runtime_ref.request_lock
309:                 if req_lock is not None and req_lock.locked():
310:                     if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
311:                         logger.info(
312:                             "Stale ACP kill skipped: request in progress "
313:                             "(backend=%s pid=%s)",
314:                             connector.backend_type,
315:                             getattr(proc, "pid", None),
316:                         )
317:                     return
318:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
319:                     logger.info(
320:                         "Stale ACP agent idle timeout: terminating process (backend=%s "
321:                         "pid=%s project=%s model=%s client_session=%s)",
322:                         connector.backend_type,
323:                         getattr(proc, "pid", None),
324:                         runtime_ref.project_dir,
325:                         runtime_ref.model,
326:                         runtime_ref.client_session_id,
327:                     )
328:                 await connector._kill_runtime(runtime_ref)
329:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
330:                 raise
331:             except Exception:
332:                 logger.exception(
333:                     "Stale ACP agent kill task failed (backend=%s project=%s model=%s "
334:                     "client_session=%s)",
335:                     connector.backend_type,
336:                     runtime_ref.project_dir,
337:                     runtime_ref.model,
338:                     runtime_ref.client_session_id,
339:                 )
340: 
341:         runtime.stale_kill_task = asyncio.create_task(_run())
342: 
343:     @abstractmethod
344:     async def _build_subprocess_command(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> list[str]:
345:         """Build the command to spawn the backend subprocess."""
346: 
347:     @abstractmethod
348:     async def _perform_handshake(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
349:         """Perform the protocol handshake (initialize, authenticate, session/new)."""
350: 
351:     @abstractmethod
352:     async def _handle_server_request(
353:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, msg: ACPNotification
354:     ) -> None:
355:         """Handle server-initiated JSON-RPC requests (e.g., permissions)."""
356: 
357:     @abstractmethod
358:     def _create_runtime(
359:         self, project_dir: Path, model: str, client_session_id: str = "default"
360:     ) -> RuntimeT:
361:         """Construct a fresh runtime instance with its own locks."""
362: 
363:     def _reset_protocol_runtime_state(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
364:         """Reset protocol-specific runtime state before a (re)spawn/teardown.
365: 
366:         Called from :meth:`_spawn_process` and :meth:`_cleanup_runtime_state`
367:         instead of inline resets. The default clears the ACP ``session_id``;
368:         Codex overrides to clear ``thread_id`` / ``turn_id`` /
369:         ``pending_history_state`` (and must NOT touch ``session_id``). Common
370:         resets (``process``, ``initialized``, ``message_id``, ``last_activity``,
371:         ``history_state``, ``acp_subprocess_identity``) stay inline in the base.
372:         """
373: 
374:         runtime.session_id = None
375: 
376:     @staticmethod
377:     def _is_usable_directory(path: Path) -> bool:
378:         return is_usable_workspace_directory(path)
379: 
380:     def _build_runtime_key(
381:         self,
382:         project_dir: Path,
383:         model: str,
384:         client_session_id: str,
385:         *,
386:         responses_text_only: bool = False,
387:     ) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
388:         return (str(project_dir), model, client_session_id)
389: 
390:     def _is_responses_text_only_request(
391:         self, request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest
392:     ) -> bool:
393:         return False
394: 
395:     def _resolve_client_session_id(
396:         self, request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest
397:     ) -> str:
398:         """Resolve the logical client session used to key ACP subprocess pools.
399: 
400:         When neither ``ConnectorRequestContext.session_id`` nor
401:         ``request.request.session_id`` is set, callers share the pool key
402:         ``"default"`` (one ACP runtime per ``(project_dir, model)`` for all
403:         such traffic). Upstream layers should set a stable session id when
404:         isolation between clients or tabs is required.
405:         """
406: 
407:         sid: str | None = None
408:         if request.context is not None and request.context.session_id:
409:             sid = request.context.session_id
410:         if not sid:
411:             raw = getattr(request.request, "session_id", None)
412:             if isinstance(raw, str) and raw.strip():
413:                 sid = raw
414:         if isinstance(sid, str) and sid.strip():
415:             return sid.strip()
416:         return "default"
417: 
418:     @staticmethod
419:     def _hash_messages_prefix(
420:         messages: Sequence[ChatMessage], end_exclusive: int
421:     ) -> str:
422:         return _hash_chat_messages_prefix_stable(messages, end_exclusive)
423: 
424:     async def _acquire_runtime(
425:         self, request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest
426:     ) -> RuntimeT:
427:         project_dir = self._resolve_project_dir_for_request(request)
428:         requested_model = strip_vendor_prefix(
429:             request.effective_model or self._model, self.VENDOR_PREFIX
430:         )
431:         client_session_id = self._resolve_client_session_id(request)
432:         runtime_key = self._build_runtime_key(
433:             project_dir,
434:             requested_model,
435:             client_session_id,
436:             responses_text_only=self._is_responses_text_only_request(request),
437:         )
438:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
439:             logger.debug(
440:                 "ACP runtime lookup: key=%s project=%s model=%s client_session=%s",
441:                 runtime_key,
442:                 project_dir,
443:                 requested_model,
444:                 client_session_id,
445:             )
446: 
447:         async with self._runtime_pool_lock:
448:             runtime = self._runtimes.get(runtime_key)
449:             if runtime is None:
450:                 runtime = self._create_runtime(
451:                     project_dir, requested_model, client_session_id
452:                 )
453:                 self._runtimes[runtime_key] = runtime
454: 
455:         return await self._reap_idle_runtime(runtime_key, runtime)
456: 
457:     def _resolve_project_dir_for_request(
458:         self, request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest
459:     ) -> Path:
460:         extra_body: object = getattr(request.request, "extra_body", None)
461:         extra_dict = cast(dict[str, Any] | None, extra_body)
462:         options = cast(dict[str, Any] | None, request.options)
463: 
464:         usable = first_usable_workspace_dir(
465:             extra_dict, options, is_usable=is_usable_workspace_directory
466:         )
467:         if usable is not None:
468:             return usable
469: 
470:         if self.requires_explicit_workspace:
471:             hint = first_workspace_hint_str(extra_dict, options)
472:             if hint is not None:
473:                 raise BackendError(
474:                     message=f"Unusable ACP workspace directory: {hint}",
475:                     details={"code": ACP_MISSING_PROJECT_WORKSPACE_CODE, "hint": hint},
476:                 )
477:             raise BackendError(
478:                 message=(
479:                     "ACP backend requires an explicit workspace directory "
480:                     "(session project_dir or request project_dir/workspace_path/cwd/project)."
481:                 ),
482:                 details={"code": ACP_MISSING_PROJECT_WORKSPACE_CODE},
483:             )
484: 
485:         hint = first_workspace_hint_str(extra_dict, options)
486:         if hint is not None and logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
487:             logger.debug("Ignoring unusable ACP project_dir override: %s", hint)
488: 
489:         if self._default_project_dir is None:
490:             raise ConfigurationError(
491:                 message=f"{self.backend_type} backend has no default project directory configured"
492:             )
493: 
494:         return self._default_project_dir
495: 
496:     async def _reap_idle_runtime(
497:         self, runtime_key: tuple[str, str, str], runtime: RuntimeT
498:     ) -> RuntimeT:
499:         """Drop idle subprocesses and swap in a fresh :class:`ACPProcessRuntime` slot.
500: 
501:         Replacing the pool entry (instead of only clearing ``runtime.process``)
502:         avoids unbounded growth of dead :class:`ACPProcessRuntime` objects while
503:         ensuring concurrent acquirers always resolve to the canonical instance
504:         currently registered for ``runtime_key``.
505:         """
506: 
507:         if self._idle_timeout <= 0:
508:             return runtime
509:         if runtime.request_lock is None or runtime.request_lock.locked():
510:             return runtime
511:         if runtime.process is None:
512:             # Another concurrent acquirer may have already idle-reaped this
513:             # key and swapped in a fresh runtime. Re-read the pool to return
514:             # the canonical instance; otherwise we would spawn a duplicate
515:             # child on a detached runtime (duplicate agents + divergent
516:             # history for one workspace/session/model tuple).
517:             async with self._runtime_pool_lock:
518:                 canonical = self._runtimes.get(runtime_key)
519:             if canonical is not None and canonical is not runtime:
520:                 return canonical
521:             return runtime
522:         if runtime.last_activity <= 0:
523:             return runtime
524:         if (time.monotonic() - runtime.last_activity) < self._idle_timeout:
525:             return runtime
526: 
527:         await self._kill_runtime(runtime)
528: 
529:         async with self._runtime_pool_lock:
530:             current = self._runtimes.get(runtime_key)
531:             if current is runtime:
532:                 replacement = self._create_runtime(
533:                     runtime.project_dir, runtime.model, runtime.client_session_id
534:                 )
535:                 self._runtimes[runtime_key] = replacement
536:                 return replacement
537:             if current is not None:
538:                 return current
539:         return runtime
540: 
541:     def _subprocess_env(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> dict[str, str]:
542:         """Environment for the ACP child process. Subclasses may specialize."""
543:         del runtime
544:         return os.environ.copy()
545: 
546:     async def _spawn_process(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
547:         assert runtime.process_lock is not None
548:         async with runtime.process_lock:
549:             process = runtime.process
550:             if process is not None and process.poll() is None:
551:                 return
552: 
553:             cmd = await self._build_subprocess_command(runtime)
554: 
555:             new_process: subprocess.Popen[bytes] | None = None
556:             try:
557:                 new_process = subprocess.Popen(
558:                     cmd,
559:                     stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
560:                     stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
561:                     stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
562:                     cwd=str(runtime.project_dir),
563:                     shell=False,
564:                     env=self._subprocess_env(runtime),
565:                     creationflags=(
566:                         subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP if os.name == "nt" else 0
567:                     ),
568:                 )
569:                 runtime.process = new_process
570:                 runtime.stderr_drain_stop_event.clear()
571:                 # Clear diagnostics before the reader starts so bytes emitted
572:                 # immediately during process startup cannot be erased by a
573:                 # late reset.
574:                 with runtime.stderr_tail_lock:
575:                     runtime.stderr_tail.clear()
576:                 runtime.stderr_drain_thread = threading.Thread(
577:                     target=self._drain_stderr_thread,
578:                     args=(new_process, runtime),
579:                     name=f"acp-stderr-{new_process.pid}",
580:                     daemon=True,
581:                 )
582:                 runtime.stderr_drain_thread.start()
583:                 await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
584:                 if new_process.poll() is not None:
585:                     stderr = await self._read_stderr(new_process, runtime)
586:                     raise BackendError(
587:                         message="ACP process failed to start",
588:                         details={
589:                             "stderr": stderr,
590:                             "project_dir": str(runtime.project_dir),
591:                             "model": runtime.model,
592:                             "command": cmd,
593:                         },
594:                     )
595:                 runtime.last_activity = time.monotonic()
596:                 runtime.initialized = False
597:                 self._reset_protocol_runtime_state(runtime)
598:                 runtime.message_id = 0
599:                 runtime.history_state = None
600:                 runtime.acp_subprocess_identity = capture_acp_subprocess_identity(
601:                     new_process, cmd
602:                 )
603:             except Exception as exc:
604:                 if new_process is not None:
605:                     self._stop_stderr_drain(runtime)
606:                     self._cleanup_process(new_process)
607:                     self._join_stderr_drain_thread(runtime)
608:                 runtime.process = None
609:                 runtime.initialized = False
610:                 self._reset_protocol_runtime_state(runtime)
611:                 runtime.history_state = None
612:                 runtime.acp_subprocess_identity = None
613:                 with runtime.stderr_tail_lock:
614:                     runtime.stderr_tail.clear()
615:                 raise APIConnectionError(
616:                     message=f"Failed to start ACP process: {exc}",
617:                     details={
618:                         "project_dir": str(runtime.project_dir),
619:                         "model": runtime.model,
620:                     },
621:                 ) from exc
622: 
623:     async def _kill_runtime(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
624:         await self._cancel_stale_kill_timer(runtime)
625:         assert runtime.process_lock is not None
626:         async with runtime.process_lock:
627:             process = runtime.process
628:             if process is None:
629:                 return
630: 
631:             try:
632:                 await self._terminate_process(process)
633:             finally:
634:                 self._cleanup_runtime_state(runtime, process)
635: 
636:     async def _kill_all_runtimes(self) -> None:
637:         async with self._runtime_pool_lock:
638:             runtimes = list(self._runtimes.values())
639:             self._runtimes.clear()
640: 
641:         for runtime in runtimes:
642:             await self._kill_runtime(runtime)
643: 
644:     def _cleanup_runtime_state(
645:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes] | None = None
646:     ) -> None:
647:         self._stop_stderr_drain(runtime)
648:         self._cleanup_process(process or runtime.process)
649:         self._join_stderr_drain_thread(runtime)
650:         runtime.process = None
651:         runtime.initialized = False
652:         self._reset_protocol_runtime_state(runtime)
653:         runtime.message_id = 0
654:         runtime.last_activity = 0.0
655:         runtime.history_state = None
656:         runtime.acp_subprocess_identity = None
657:         with runtime.stderr_tail_lock:
658:             runtime.stderr_tail.clear()
659: 
660:     def _cleanup_process(self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes] | None = None) -> None:
661:         if process is None:
662:             return
663: 
664:         for stream_name in ("stdin", "stdout", "stderr"):
665:             stream = getattr(process, stream_name, None)
666:             if stream is None:
667:                 continue
668:             with contextlib.suppress(OSError, ValueError):
669:                 stream.close()
670: 
671:     def _stop_stderr_drain(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
672:         runtime.stderr_drain_stop_event.set()
673: 
674:     def _join_stderr_drain_thread(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
675:         """Detach the stderr reader without blocking the event loop.
676: 
677:         Runtime cleanup is invoked from async request/cancellation paths.  The
678:         reader is a daemon thread and its pipe is closed by
679:         :meth:`_cleanup_process` immediately before this helper is called, so
680:         it will exit promptly.  A zero-timeout join lets an already-finished
681:         reader be reclaimed while avoiding a synchronous wait on the event
682:         loop.  If the reader is still unwinding, it remains detached and will
683:         terminate once the closed pipe is observed.
684:         """
685:         thread = runtime.stderr_drain_thread
686:         if thread is None:
687:             return
688:         runtime.stderr_drain_thread = None
689:         if thread is not threading.current_thread():
690:             thread.join(timeout=0)
691: 
692:     def _drain_stderr_thread(
693:         self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes], runtime: RuntimeT
694:     ) -> None:
695:         if process.stderr is None:
696:             return
697: 
698:         def _read_chunk() -> bytes:
699:             stream = cast(Any, process.stderr)
700:             return bytes(stream.read1(4096))
701: 
702:         try:
703:             while not runtime.stderr_drain_stop_event.is_set():
704:                 chunk = _read_chunk()
705:                 if not chunk:
706:                     return
707:                 with runtime.stderr_tail_lock:
708:                     runtime.stderr_tail.extend(chunk)
709:                     if len(runtime.stderr_tail) > MAX_STDERR_TAIL_SIZE:
710:                         del runtime.stderr_tail[:-MAX_STDERR_TAIL_SIZE]
711:         except (OSError, ValueError):
712:             return
713: 
714:     async def _read_stderr(
715:         self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes], runtime: RuntimeT | None = None
716:     ) -> str:
717:         if runtime is not None:
718:             thread = runtime.stderr_drain_thread
719:             deadline = time.monotonic() + 1.0
720:             while (
721:                 thread is not None and thread.is_alive() and time.monotonic() < deadline
722:             ):
723:                 await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
724:             with runtime.stderr_tail_lock:
725:                 return bytes(runtime.stderr_tail).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
726:         if process.stderr is None:
727:             return ""
728:         stderr_bytes: list[bytes] = []
729: 
730:         def _read_all() -> None:
731:             stream = cast(Any, process.stderr)
732:             with contextlib.suppress(OSError, ValueError):
733:                 stderr_bytes.append(bytes(stream.read()))
734: 
735:         reader = threading.Thread(
736:             target=_read_all, name=f"acp-stderr-fallback-{process.pid}", daemon=True
737:         )
738:         reader.start()
739:         while reader.is_alive():
740:             await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
741:         reader.join()
742:         return (stderr_bytes[0] if stderr_bytes else b"").decode(
743:             "utf-8", errors="replace"
744:         )
745: 
746:     async def _terminate_process(self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes]) -> None:
747:         if process.poll() is not None:
748:             return
749: 
750:         if os.name == "nt":
751:             try:
752:                 result = await asyncio.to_thread(
753:                     subprocess.run,
754:                     ["taskkill", "/PID", str(process.pid), "/T", "/F"],
755:                     capture_output=True,
756:                     check=False,
757:                     shell=False,
758:                 )
759:                 if getattr(result, "returncode", 1) != 0 and process.poll() is None:
760:                     with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
761:                         process.terminate()
762:             finally:
763:                 with contextlib.suppress(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
764:                     await asyncio.to_thread(lambda: process.wait(timeout=5))
765:             return
766: 
767:         process.terminate()
768:         try:
769:             await asyncio.to_thread(lambda: process.wait(timeout=5))
770:         except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
771:             process.kill()
772:             with contextlib.suppress(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
773:                 await asyncio.to_thread(lambda: process.wait(timeout=5))
774: 
775:     def _get_next_message_id(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> int:
776:         runtime.message_id += 1
777:         return runtime.message_id
778: 
779:     async def _write_json_line(
780:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, payload: dict[str, Any]
781:     ) -> None:
782:         process = runtime.process
783:         if process is None or process.stdin is None:
784:             raise BackendError(message="ACP process not running")
785:         encoded = (json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
786: 
787:         def _write() -> None:
788:             assert process.stdin is not None
789:             process.stdin.write(encoded)
790:             process.stdin.flush()
791: 
792:         try:
793:             await asyncio.wait_for(
794:                 asyncio.to_thread(_write), timeout=self._process_timeout
795:             )
796:         except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:
797:             # A blocked pipe means the child is no longer making progress.  Tear
798:             # it down before the caller releases the request lock so a later
799:             # turn cannot inherit a half-written ACP conversation.
800:             await self._kill_runtime(runtime)
801:             raise APITimeoutError(
802:                 message="Timeout writing to ACP process",
803:                 details={"timeout": self._process_timeout, "model": runtime.model},
804:             ) from exc
805:         runtime.last_activity = time.monotonic()
806: 
807:     async def _send_jsonrpc_message(
808:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, method: str, params: dict[str, Any]
809:     ) -> int:
810:         message_id = self._get_next_message_id(runtime)
811:         payload = {
812:             "jsonrpc": "2.0",
813:             "method": method,
814:             "params": params,
815:             "id": message_id,
816:         }
817:         try:
818:             await self._write_json_line(runtime, payload)
819:             return message_id
820:         except APITimeoutError:
821:             raise
822:         except Exception as exc:
823:             raise APIConnectionError(
824:                 message=f"Failed to communicate with ACP process: {exc}"
825:             ) from exc
826: 
827:     async def _send_jsonrpc_result(
828:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, request_id: int, result: dict[str, Any]
829:     ) -> None:
830:         await self._write_json_line(
831:             runtime, {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request_id, "result": result}
832:         )
833: 
834:     async def _read_jsonrpc_message(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> ACPNotification | None:
835:         process = runtime.process
836:         if process is None or process.stdout is None:
837:             raise BackendError(message="ACP process not running")
838: 
839:         def _read_limited() -> bytes:
840:             assert process.stdout is not None
841:             return bytes(process.stdout.readline(MAX_RESPONSE_LINE_SIZE + 1))
842: 
843:         try:
844:             line = await asyncio.to_thread(_read_limited)
845:             if not line:
846:                 if process.poll() is not None:
847:                     stderr = await self._read_stderr(process, runtime)
848:                     self._cleanup_runtime_state(runtime, process)
849:                     raise BackendError(
850:                         message="ACP process exited unexpectedly",
851:                         details={
852:                             "stderr": stderr,
853:                             "project_dir": str(runtime.project_dir),
854:                             "model": runtime.model,
855:                         },
856:                     )
857:                 return None
858:             if len(line) > MAX_RESPONSE_LINE_SIZE:
859:                 raise BackendError(message="Response too large from ACP process")
860:             runtime.last_activity = time.monotonic()
861:             data = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
862:             if not isinstance(data, dict):
863:                 raise BackendError(message="Invalid non-object JSON response")
864:             return ACPNotification(**data)
865:         except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
866:             raise BackendError(
867:                 message="Invalid JSON response from ACP process",
868:                 details={"error": str(exc)},
869:             ) from exc
870:         except BackendError:
871:             raise
872:         except Exception as exc:
873:             raise APIConnectionError(
874:                 message=f"Failed to read from ACP process: {exc}"
875:             ) from exc
876: 
877:     async def _await_response(
878:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, request_id: int
879:     ) -> ACPNotification:
880:         deadline = time.monotonic() + self._process_timeout
881:         while True:
882:             remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
883:             if remaining <= 0:
884:                 raise APITimeoutError(
885:                     message="Timeout waiting for ACP response",
886:                     details={"timeout": self._process_timeout, "model": runtime.model},
887:                 )
888: 
889:             response = await asyncio.wait_for(
890:                 self._read_jsonrpc_message(runtime), timeout=remaining
891:             )
892:             if response is None:
893:                 continue
894:             if response.is_server_request:
895:                 await self._handle_server_request(runtime, response)
896:                 continue
897:             if response.id != request_id:
898:                 continue
899:             return response
900: 
901:     async def _initialize_runtime(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
902:         if runtime.initialized and runtime.session_id:
903:             return
904:         await self._perform_handshake(runtime)
905: 
906:     def _extract_user_message_as_string(
907:         self, processed_messages: Sequence[ChatMessage | dict[str, Any] | str | Any]
908:     ) -> str:
909:         last_user_message = ""
910: 
911:         for message in processed_messages:
912:             role = ""
913:             content: Any = ""
914: 
915:             if isinstance(message, ChatMessage):
916:                 role = message.role
917:                 content = message.content
918:             elif isinstance(message, dict):
919:                 role = str(message.get("role", ""))
920:                 content = message.get("content")
921:                 if content in (None, "") and "parts" in message:
922:                     content = message.get("parts")
923:             elif isinstance(message, str):
924:                 role = "user"
925:                 content = message
926:             else:
927:                 role = str(getattr(message, "role", ""))
928:                 content = getattr(message, "content", "")
929: 
930:             normalized = self._stringify_message_content(content)
931:             if role == "user" and normalized:
932:                 last_user_message = normalized
933: 
934:         return last_user_message
935: 
936:     def _stringify_message_content(self, content: Any) -> str:
937:         if content is None:
938:             return ""
939:         if isinstance(content, str):
940:             return content
941:         if isinstance(content, Sequence) and not isinstance(
942:             content, str | bytes | bytearray
943:         ):
944:             parts: list[str] = []
945:             for item in content:
946:                 if isinstance(item, str):
947:                     parts.append(item)
948:                     continue
949:                 if isinstance(item, dict):
950:                     text = item.get("text")
951:                     nested = item.get("content")
952:                     if isinstance(text, str):
953:                         parts.append(text)
954:                     elif isinstance(nested, str):
955:                         parts.append(nested)
956:                 else:
957:                     item_text = getattr(item, "text", None)
958:                     if isinstance(item_text, str):
959:                         parts.append(item_text)
960:             return " ".join(part for part in parts if part)
961:         return str(content)
962: 
963:     @staticmethod
964:     def _text_from_acp_content_block(content: Any) -> str | None:
965:         """Extract human-readable text from a session/update ``content`` block."""
966:         if isinstance(content, dict):
967:             td = content.get("textDelta")
968:             if isinstance(td, str) and td:
969:                 return td
970:             raw_text = content.get("text")
971:             if content.get("type") == "text" and isinstance(raw_text, str):
972:                 return raw_text
973:             try:
974:                 normalized = ACPUpdateContent(**content)
975:             except Exception:
976:                 return None
977:             if normalized.type == "text" and isinstance(normalized.text, str):
978:                 return normalized.text
979:         return None
980: 
981:     @staticmethod
982:     def _open_thinking_block(text: str) -> str:
983:         """Open a visible thinking block for clients that cannot segment reasoning."""
984: 
985:         return f"Thinking:\n{text}"
986: 
987:     @staticmethod
988:     def _append_thinking_block(text: str) -> str:
989:         """Append incremental text inside an already-open thinking block."""
990: 
991:         return text
992: 
993:     @staticmethod
994:     def _close_thinking_block() -> str:
995:         """Close a visible thinking block before ordinary content resumes."""
996: 
997:         return "\n\n"
998: 
999:     def _thinking_content_piece(self, runtime: RuntimeT, text: str) -> AcpStreamPiece:
1000:         if runtime.acp_thinking_block_open:
1001:             return AcpStreamPiece(content=self._append_thinking_block(text))
1002:         runtime.acp_thinking_block_open = True
1003:         return AcpStreamPiece(content=self._open_thinking_block(text))
1004: 
1005:     def _prepend_thinking_close_if_needed(
1006:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, pieces: list[AcpStreamPiece]
1007:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1008:         if not runtime.acp_thinking_block_open:
1009:             return pieces
1010:         for index, piece in enumerate(pieces):
1011:             if piece.content is None:
1012:                 continue
1013:             pieces[index] = AcpStreamPiece(
1014:                 content=f"{self._close_thinking_block()}{piece.content}",
1015:                 reasoning_content=piece.reasoning_content,
1016:             )
1017:             runtime.acp_thinking_block_open = False
1018:             return pieces
1019:         return pieces
1020: 
1021:     def _acp_progress_reasoning_line(
1022:         self, session_update_kind: str, update: dict[str, Any]
1023:     ) -> str | None:
1024:         """Build a short progress line for plan/mode style updates (reasoning channel)."""
1025:         if session_update_kind == "plan":
1026:             title = update.get("title")
1027:             if isinstance(title, str) and title.strip():
1028:                 return f"[plan] {title.strip()}\n"
1029:             return "[plan]\n"
1030:         if session_update_kind == "current_mode_update":
1031:             mode = update.get("modeId") or update.get("mode")
1032:             if isinstance(mode, str) and mode:
1033:                 return f"[mode] {mode}\n"
1034:             return "[mode]\n"
1035:         return None
1036: 
1037:     def _resolve_tool_stream_key(
1038:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, tc: dict[str, Any], *, for_new_invocation: bool
1039:     ) -> str:
1040:         ck = extract_tool_correlation_key(tc)
1041:         if ck:
1042:             return ck
1043:         if for_new_invocation:
1044:             runtime.acp_anon_tool_seq += 1
1045:             key = f"__anon__:{runtime.acp_anon_tool_seq}"
1046:             runtime.acp_last_anon_stream_key = key
1047:             return key
1048:         if runtime.acp_last_anon_stream_key:
1049:             return runtime.acp_last_anon_stream_key
1050:         runtime.acp_anon_tool_seq += 1
1051:         key = f"__anon__:{runtime.acp_anon_tool_seq}"
1052:         runtime.acp_last_anon_stream_key = key
1053:         return key
1054: 
1055:     @staticmethod
1056:     def _acp_update_tool_sizes_from_merged(
1057:         acc: AcpToolStreamAccum, merged: dict[str, Any]
1058:     ) -> None:
1059:         inp = extract_tool_input(merged)
1060:         input_bytes = payload_utf8_byte_length(inp)
1061:         if inp is not None and input_bytes >= acc.last_input_bytes:
1062:             acc.last_input = inp
1063:             acc.last_input_bytes = input_bytes
1064:         out = extract_tool_output(merged)
1065:         if out is not None:
1066:             acc.last_output_bytes = max(
1067:                 acc.last_output_bytes, payload_utf8_byte_length(out)
1068:             )
1069: 
1070:     def _ensure_acp_tool_accum(
1071:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, key: str, tool_name: str
1072:     ) -> AcpToolStreamAccum:
1073:         acc = runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum.get(key)
1074:         if acc is None:
1075:             acc = AcpToolStreamAccum(tool_name=tool_name)
1076:             acc.started_wall_iso = utc_now_iso()
1077:             acc.started_perf = time.perf_counter()
1078:             runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum[key] = acc
1079:         elif tool_name and tool_name != "tool":
1080:             acc.tool_name = tool_name
1081:         return acc
1082: 
1083:     @staticmethod
1084:     def _acp_mark_tool_ended_now(acc: AcpToolStreamAccum) -> None:
1085:         if acc.ended_wall_iso is None:
1086:             acc.ended_wall_iso = utc_now_iso()
1087:         if acc.ended_perf is None:
1088:             acc.ended_perf = time.perf_counter()
1089: 
1090:     def _acp_try_emit_tool_summary(
1091:         self, acc: AcpToolStreamAccum
1092:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1093:         if acc.summary_emitted or not acc.started_wall_iso:
1094:             return []
1095:         self._acp_mark_tool_ended_now(acc)
1096:         end_wall = acc.ended_wall_iso or utc_now_iso()
1097:         end_perf = acc.ended_perf if acc.ended_perf is not None else time.perf_counter()
1098:         started_perf = acc.started_perf if acc.started_perf > 0 else end_perf
1099:         elapsed = max(0.0, end_perf - started_perf)
1100:         text = format_acp_tool_completion_summary(
1101:             acc.tool_name,
1102:             input_payload=acc.last_input,
1103:             input_bytes=acc.last_input_bytes,
1104:             output_bytes=acc.last_output_bytes,
1105:             started_iso=acc.started_wall_iso,
1106:             ended_iso=end_wall,
1107:             elapsed_s=elapsed,
1108:         )
1109:         acc.summary_emitted = True
1110:         acc.pending_terminal_summary = False
1111:         return [AcpStreamPiece(content=text)]
1112: 
1113:     def _acp_start_summary_pieces(
1114:         self, acc: AcpToolStreamAccum
1115:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1116:         if acc.start_emitted or acc.summary_emitted or not acc.started_wall_iso:
1117:             return []
1118:         text = format_acp_tool_started_summary(
1119:             acc.tool_name,
1120:             input_payload=acc.last_input,
1121:             input_bytes=acc.last_input_bytes,
1122:             started_iso=acc.started_wall_iso,
1123:         )
1124:         acc.start_emitted = True
1125:         acc.last_heartbeat_perf = (
1126:             acc.started_perf if acc.started_perf > 0 else time.perf_counter()
1127:         )
1128:         return [AcpStreamPiece(content=text)]
1129: 
1130:     def _acp_in_progress_heartbeat_pieces(
1131:         self, runtime: RuntimeT
1132:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1133:         interval = self._acp_tool_heartbeat_seconds
1134:         if interval <= 0:
1135:             return []
1136:         now = time.perf_counter()
1137:         out: list[AcpStreamPiece] = []
1138:         for acc in runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum.values():
1139:             if acc.summary_emitted or not acc.start_emitted:
1140:                 continue
1141:             last = acc.last_heartbeat_perf or acc.started_perf
1142:             if last > 0 and (now - last) < interval:
1143:                 continue
1144:             started = acc.started_perf if acc.started_perf > 0 else now
1145:             elapsed = max(0.0, now - started)
1146:             out.append(
1147:                 AcpStreamPiece(
1148:                     content=format_acp_tool_heartbeat_line(acc.tool_name, elapsed)
1149:                 )
1150:             )
1151:             acc.last_heartbeat_perf = now
1152:         return out
1153: 
1154:     def _acp_has_in_progress_tools(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> bool:
1155:         return any(
1156:             acc.start_emitted and not acc.summary_emitted
1157:             for acc in runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum.values()
1158:         )
1159: 
1160:     def _acp_terminal_summary_pieces(
1161:         self,
1162:         acc: AcpToolStreamAccum,
1163:         merged: dict[str, Any],
1164:         status_str: str | None,
1165:         *,
1166:         allow_defer: bool,
1167:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1168:         pieces: list[AcpStreamPiece] = []
1169:         if (
1170:             acc.pending_terminal_summary
1171:             and not acc.summary_emitted
1172:             and (
1173:                 extract_tool_output(merged) is not None
1174:                 or acc.last_output_bytes > 0
1175:                 or acc.last_input_bytes > 0
1176:             )
1177:         ):
1178:             pieces.extend(self._acp_try_emit_tool_summary(acc))
1179:         if not acc.summary_emitted and is_terminal_tool_status(status_str):
1180:             no_output = (
1181:                 extract_tool_output(merged) is None and acc.last_output_bytes == 0
1182:             )
1183:             no_input = extract_tool_input(merged) is None and acc.last_input_bytes == 0
1184:             correlated = extract_tool_correlation_key(merged) is not None
1185:             if allow_defer and no_output and no_input and correlated:
1186:                 acc.pending_terminal_summary = True
1187:             else:
1188:                 pieces.extend(self._acp_try_emit_tool_summary(acc))
1189:         return pieces
1190: 
1191:     @staticmethod
1192:     def _acp_tool_call_payload_is_multi_dict_list(upd: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
1193:         nested = (
1194:             upd.get("toolCall") or upd.get("tool_call") or upd.get("toolInvocation")
1195:         )
1196:         if not isinstance(nested, list):
1197:             return False
1198:         return sum(1 for x in nested if isinstance(x, dict)) > 1
1199: 
1200:     def _acp_pieces_for_tool_call(
1201:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, upd: dict[str, Any]
1202:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1203:         out: list[AcpStreamPiece] = []
1204:         batch_multi = self._acp_tool_call_payload_is_multi_dict_list(upd)
1205:         for merged in iter_coalesced_acp_tool_session_dicts(upd):
1206:             if not merged or not acp_tool_payload_should_emit(merged):
1207:                 continue
1208:             key = self._resolve_tool_stream_key(
1209:                 runtime, merged, for_new_invocation=True
1210:             )
1211:             name = extract_tool_name(merged)
1212:             acc = self._ensure_acp_tool_accum(runtime, key, name)
1213:             self._acp_update_tool_sizes_from_merged(acc, merged)
1214:             status_raw = merged.get("status") or merged.get("state")
1215:             status_str = status_raw.strip() if isinstance(status_raw, str) else None
1216:             pieces = self._acp_terminal_summary_pieces(
1217:                 # Batched multi-tool payloads lack a stable follow-up edge per item, so
1218:                 # empty terminal entries emit immediately instead of waiting for an update.
1219:                 acc,
1220:                 merged,
1221:                 status_str,
1222:                 allow_defer=not batch_multi,
1223:             )
1224:             if not pieces and not is_terminal_tool_status(status_str):
1225:                 pieces = self._acp_start_summary_pieces(acc)
1226:             out.extend(pieces)
1227:         return out
1228: 
1229:     def _acp_pieces_for_tool_call_update(
1230:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, upd: dict[str, Any]
1231:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1232:         merged = coalesce_acp_tool_call_update_session_dict(upd)
1233:         if not merged:
1234:             return []
1235:         key = self._resolve_tool_stream_key(runtime, merged, for_new_invocation=False)
1236:         if (
1237:             key not in runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum
1238:             and not acp_tool_payload_should_emit(merged)
1239:         ):
1240:             return []
1241:         name = extract_tool_name(merged)
1242:         acc = self._ensure_acp_tool_accum(runtime, key, name)
1243:         self._acp_update_tool_sizes_from_merged(acc, merged)
1244:         status_raw = merged.get("status") or merged.get("state")
1245:         status_str = status_raw.strip() if isinstance(status_raw, str) else None
1246:         pieces = self._acp_terminal_summary_pieces(
1247:             acc, merged, status_str, allow_defer=True
1248:         )
1249:         if pieces or is_terminal_tool_status(status_str):
1250:             return pieces
1251:         return self._acp_start_summary_pieces(acc)
1252: 
1253:     def _flush_incomplete_acp_tool_streams(
1254:         self, runtime: RuntimeT
1255:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1256:         """Emit summaries for tools still missing a final summary (incl. deferred terminal)."""
1257:         out: list[AcpStreamPiece] = []
1258:         for _key, acc in list(runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum.items()):
1259:             if acc.summary_emitted or not acc.started_wall_iso:
1260:                 continue
1261:             self._acp_mark_tool_ended_now(acc)
1262:             out.extend(self._acp_try_emit_tool_summary(acc))
1263:         return out
1264: 
1265:     def _session_update_to_stream_pieces(
1266:         self, response: ACPNotification, runtime: RuntimeT
1267:     ) -> list[AcpStreamPiece]:
1268:         """Map a ``session/update`` JSON-RPC notification to zero or more stream pieces."""
1269:         if response.method != ACP_UPDATE_METHOD or response.params is None:
1270:             return []
1271:         try:
1272:             envelope = ACPSessionUpdate(**response.params)
1273:         except Exception:
1274:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1275:                 logger.debug(
1276:                     "ACP session/update params could not be parsed", exc_info=True
1277:                 )
1278:             return []
1279:         upd = envelope.update
1280:         kind = upd.get("sessionUpdate")
1281:         if not isinstance(kind, str):
1282:             return []
1283: 
1284:         text = self._text_from_acp_content_block(upd.get("content"))
1285: 
1286:         if kind == ACP_AGENT_MESSAGE_CHUNK:
1287:             if text:
1288:                 return self._prepend_thinking_close_if_needed(
1289:                     runtime, [AcpStreamPiece(content=text)]
1290:                 )
1291:             return []
1292:         if kind == ACP_AGENT_THOUGHT_CHUNK:
1293:             if text:
1294:                 return [self._thinking_content_piece(runtime, text)]
1295:             return []
1296:         if kind == "tool_call":
1297:             return self._prepend_thinking_close_if_needed(
1298:                 runtime, self._acp_pieces_for_tool_call(runtime, upd)
1299:             )
1300:         if kind == "tool_call_update":
1301:             return self._prepend_thinking_close_if_needed(
1302:                 runtime, self._acp_pieces_for_tool_call_update(runtime, upd)
1303:             )
1304: 
1305:         progress = self._acp_progress_reasoning_line(kind, upd)
1306:         if progress:
1307:             return [self._thinking_content_piece(runtime, progress)]
1308:         return []
1309: 
1310:     def _session_update_to_stream_piece(
1311:         self, response: ACPNotification, runtime: RuntimeT
1312:     ) -> AcpStreamPiece | None:
1313:         """Back-compat helper merging multiple ``content`` / ``reasoning`` pieces."""
1314:         pieces = self._session_update_to_stream_pieces(response, runtime)
1315:         if not pieces:
1316:             return None
1317:         if len(pieces) == 1:
1318:             return pieces[0]
1319:         content_parts = [p.content for p in pieces if p.content is not None]
1320:         reasoning_parts = [
1321:             p.reasoning_content for p in pieces if p.reasoning_content is not None
1322:         ]
1323:         return AcpStreamPiece(
1324:             content="".join(content_parts) if content_parts else None,
1325:             reasoning_content="".join(reasoning_parts) if reasoning_parts else None,
1326:         )
1327: 
1328:     async def _iter_acp_stream_pieces(
1329:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, prompt_request_id: int, response_model: str
1330:     ) -> AsyncGenerator[AcpStreamPiece, None]:
1331:         runtime.acp_tool_stream_accum.clear()
1332:         runtime.acp_anon_tool_seq = 0
1333:         runtime.acp_last_anon_stream_key = None
