1334:         runtime.acp_thinking_block_open = False
1335:         deadline = time.monotonic() + self._process_timeout
1336:         read_task: asyncio.Task[ACPNotification | None] | None = None
1337:         try:
1338:             while True:
1339:                 remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
1340:                 if remaining <= 0:
1341:                     raise asyncio.TimeoutError()
1342:                 interval = self._acp_tool_heartbeat_seconds
1343:                 slice_for_heartbeat = interval > 0 and self._acp_has_in_progress_tools(
1344:                     runtime
1345:                 )
1346:                 wait_timeout = (
1347:                     min(interval, remaining) if slice_for_heartbeat else remaining
1348:                 )
1349: 
1350:                 if runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
1351:                     if read_task is None:
1352:                         read_task = asyncio.create_task(
1353:                             self._read_jsonrpc_message(runtime)
1354:                         )
1355:                     cancel_task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.cancellation_event.wait())
1356:                     try:
1357:                         done, pending = await asyncio.wait(
1358:                             {read_task, cancel_task},
1359:                             return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED,
1360:                             timeout=wait_timeout,
1361:                         )
1362:                     except asyncio.CancelledError:
1363:                         read_task.cancel()
1364:                         cancel_task.cancel()
1365:                         with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1366:                             await read_task
1367:                         with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1368:                             await cancel_task
1369:                         read_task = None
1370:                         raise
1371:                     if cancel_task not in done:
1372:                         cancel_task.cancel()
1373:                         with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1374:                             await cancel_task
1375:                     if not done:
1376:                         for piece in self._acp_in_progress_heartbeat_pieces(runtime):
1377:                             if piece.content or piece.reasoning_content:
1378:                                 yield piece
1379:                         continue
1380:                     for t in pending:
1381:                         if t is read_task:
1382:                             continue
1383:                         t.cancel()
1384:                         with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1385:                             await t
1386: 
1387:                     if cancel_task in done:
1388:                         if read_task is not None:
1389:                             read_task.cancel()
1390:                             with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1391:                                 await read_task
1392:                             read_task = None
1393:                         return
1394:                     if read_task is None:
1395:                         continue
1396:                     response = read_task.result()
1397:                     read_task = None
1398:                 else:
1399:                     if read_task is None:
1400:                         read_task = asyncio.create_task(
1401:                             self._read_jsonrpc_message(runtime)
1402:                         )
1403:                     try:
1404:                         response = await asyncio.wait_for(
1405:                             asyncio.shield(read_task), timeout=wait_timeout
1406:                         )
1407:                         read_task = None
1408:                     except asyncio.TimeoutError:
1409:                         if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
1410:                             pending_read = read_task
1411:                             if pending_read is not None:
1412:                                 pending_read.cancel()
1413:                                 with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1414:                                     await pending_read
1415:                             read_task = None
1416:                             raise
1417:                         for piece in self._acp_in_progress_heartbeat_pieces(runtime):
1418:                             if piece.content or piece.reasoning_content:
1419:                                 yield piece
1420:                         continue
1421: 
1422:                 if response is None:
1423:                     continue
1424: 
1425:                 if response.is_server_request:
1426:                     await self._handle_server_request(runtime, response)
1427:                     continue
1428: 
1429:                 if response.id == prompt_request_id:
1430:                     if response.is_error and response.error is not None:
1431:                         raise BackendError(
1432:                             message=(
1433:                                 "ACP process error: "
1434:                                 f"{_format_acp_error(response.error)}"
1435:                             ),
1436:                             details=response.error.model_dump(),
1437:                         )
1438:                     if runtime.acp_thinking_block_open:
1439:                         runtime.acp_thinking_block_open = False
1440:                         yield AcpStreamPiece(content=self._close_thinking_block())
1441:                     for flush_piece in self._flush_incomplete_acp_tool_streams(runtime):
1442:                         if flush_piece.content or flush_piece.reasoning_content:
1443:                             yield flush_piece
1444:                     break
1445: 
1446:                 for piece in self._session_update_to_stream_pieces(response, runtime):
1447:                     if piece.content is not None or piece.reasoning_content is not None:
1448:                         yield piece
1449:         except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:
1450:             raise APITimeoutError(
1451:                 message="Timeout waiting for ACP response",
1452:                 details={"timeout": self._process_timeout, "model": response_model},
1453:             ) from exc
1454:         finally:
1455:             if read_task is not None and not read_task.done():
1456:                 read_task.cancel()
1457:                 with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
1458:                     await read_task
1459: 
1460:     async def _iter_stream_pieces(
1461:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, request_id: int, response_model: str
1462:     ) -> AsyncGenerator[AcpStreamPiece, None]:
1463:         """Dispatch to the protocol-specific stream-piece iterator.
1464: 
1465:         The default delegates to the ACP loop (:meth:`_iter_acp_stream_pieces`).
1466:         Codex overrides to feed :meth:`_iter_codex_stream_pieces` so the shared
1467:         streaming/non-streaming scaffolding (``_stream_response`` /
1468:         ``_collect_non_streaming_response``) works for both protocols without
1469:         re-implementing either.
1470:         """
1471: 
1472:         async for piece in self._iter_acp_stream_pieces(
1473:             runtime, request_id, response_model
1474:         ):
1475:             yield piece
1476: 
1477:     async def _collect_non_streaming_response(
1478:         self,
1479:         runtime: RuntimeT,
1480:         requested_model: str,
1481:         turn_request_id: int,
1482:         request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest,
1483:     ) -> ResponseEnvelope:
1484:         """Accumulate a non-streaming turn into a :class:`ResponseEnvelope`.
1485: 
1486:         ACP default: iterate :meth:`_iter_stream_pieces`, join ``content`` /
1487:         ``reasoning_content`` fragments, build a :class:`CanonicalChatResponse`
1488:         with ``finish_reason="stop"``, and attach usage. Codex overrides to map
1489:         the Codex turn ``finish_reason`` (raising on a failed turn).
1490:         """
1491: 
1492:         fragments: list[str] = []
1493:         reasoning_fragments: list[str] = []
1494:         async for piece in self._iter_stream_pieces(
1495:             runtime, turn_request_id, requested_model
1496:         ):
1497:             if piece.content:
1498:                 fragments.append(piece.content)
1499:             if piece.reasoning_content:
1500:                 reasoning_fragments.append(piece.reasoning_content)
1501:         full_response = "".join(fragments)
1502:         full_reasoning = "".join(reasoning_fragments) if reasoning_fragments else None
1503:         response = CanonicalChatResponse(
1504:             id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
1505:             object="chat.completion",
1506:             created=int(time.time()),
1507:             model=requested_model,
1508:             choices=[
1509:                 ChatCompletionChoice(
1510:                     index=0,
1511:                     message=ChatCompletionChoiceMessage(
1512:                         role="assistant",
1513:                         content=full_response,
1514:                         reasoning_content=full_reasoning,
1515:                     ),
1516:                     finish_reason="stop",
1517:                 )
1518:             ],
1519:         )
1520:         envelope = ResponseEnvelope(
1521:             content=response.model_dump(exclude_none=True), headers={}, status_code=200
1522:         )
1523:         return self.ensure_usage_in_response(
1524:             envelope, list(request.processed_messages), requested_model
1525:         )
1526: 
1527:     def _create_sse_chunk_from_piece(
1528:         self, piece: AcpStreamPiece, model: str, chunk_id: str
1529:     ) -> str | None:
1530:         delta: dict[str, Any] = {}
1531:         if piece.content:
1532:             delta["content"] = piece.content
1533:         if piece.reasoning_content:
1534:             delta["reasoning_content"] = piece.reasoning_content
1535:         if not delta:
1536:             return None
1537:         payload = {
1538:             "id": chunk_id,
1539:             "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
1540:             "created": int(time.time()),
1541:             "model": model,
1542:             "choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": delta, "finish_reason": None}],
1543:         }
1544:         return f"data: {json.dumps(payload)}\n\n"
1545: 
1546:     @staticmethod
1547:     def _create_sse_done_chunk() -> str:
1548:         return "data: [DONE]\n\n"
1549: 
1550:     @staticmethod
1551:     def _is_terminal_finish_reason(value: Any) -> bool:
1552:         """Return whether a finish reason terminates an OpenAI stream."""
1553: 
1554:         return value in {"stop", "length", "content_filter", "tool_calls", "error"}
1555: 
1556:     @classmethod
1557:     def _stream_chunk_is_terminal(cls, chunk: ProcessedResponse) -> bool:
1558:         """Detect terminal markers before exposing a stream chunk downstream.
1559: 
1560:         Most providers send a separate ``data: [DONE]`` sentinel, but Codex
1561:         emits its final OpenAI JSON chunk with ``finish_reason="stop"`` and
1562:         downstream converters stop consuming at that chunk.  Detect both
1563:         representations here so the lock-owning wrapper can finish teardown
1564:         before yielding a terminal chunk to the client.
1565:         """
1566: 
1567:         metadata = chunk.metadata
1568:         if isinstance(metadata, dict):
1569:             if metadata.get("is_done") is True:
1570:                 return True
1571:             if cls._is_terminal_finish_reason(metadata.get("finish_reason")):
1572:                 return True
1573: 
1574:         content = chunk.content
1575:         if content == cls._create_sse_done_chunk():
1576:             return True
1577: 
1578:         payloads: list[Any] = []
1579:         if isinstance(content, dict):
1580:             payloads.append(content)
1581:         else:
1582:             if isinstance(content, bytes | bytearray):
1583:                 with contextlib.suppress(UnicodeDecodeError):
1584:                     content = bytes(content).decode("utf-8")
1585:             if isinstance(content, str):
1586:                 # Accept both SSE data lines and raw JSON payloads.  Ignore
1587:                 # comments/keepalives and malformed data lines; a later
1588:                 # well-formed terminal payload still has to release the lock.
1589:                 for line in content.splitlines():
1590:                     line = line.strip()
1591:                     if line.startswith("data:"):
1592:                         line = line[5:].strip()
1593:                         if line == "[DONE]":
1594:                             return True
1595:                     elif not line:
1596:                         continue
1597:                     else:
1598:                         continue
1599:                     if not line:
1600:                         continue
1601:                     with contextlib.suppress(json.JSONDecodeError):
1602:                         payloads.append(json.loads(line))
1603:                 if not payloads:
1604:                     with contextlib.suppress(json.JSONDecodeError):
1605:                         payloads.append(json.loads(content))
1606: 
1607:         for payload in payloads:
1608:             if not isinstance(payload, dict):
1609:                 continue
1610:             if cls._is_terminal_finish_reason(payload.get("finish_reason")):
1611:                 return True
1612:             choices = payload.get("choices")
1613:             if isinstance(choices, list):
1614:                 for choice in choices:
1615:                     if isinstance(choice, dict) and cls._is_terminal_finish_reason(
1616:                         choice.get("finish_reason")
1617:                     ):
1618:                         return True
1619:         return False
1620: 
1621:     async def _stream_response(
1622:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, requested_model: str, prompt_request_id: int
1623:     ) -> AsyncGenerator[ProcessedResponse, None]:
1624:         chunk_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
1625:         async for piece in self._iter_stream_pieces(
1626:             runtime, prompt_request_id, requested_model
1627:         ):
1628:             sse = self._create_sse_chunk_from_piece(piece, requested_model, chunk_id)
1629:             if sse is not None:
1630:                 yield ProcessedResponse(content=sse)
1631: 
1632:         # Emit the terminal chunk only after the protocol iterator has ended.
1633:         # Keeping this out of ``finally`` is important: downstream adapters may
1634:         # stop consuming as soon as they see [DONE].  The lock-owning wrapper
1635:         # must be able to finish its own cleanup before exposing that chunk.
1636:         yield ProcessedResponse(content=self._create_sse_done_chunk())
1637: 
1638:     async def _compute_history_and_user_message(
1639:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, messages: Sequence[ChatMessage]
1640:     ) -> tuple[str, HistoryState]:
1641:         """Compute the user-message text and resulting history state for a turn.
1642: 
1643:         Shared divergence-detection body used by both ACP (``session/prompt``)
1644:         and Codex (``turn/start``). On the first turn (``history_state is
1645:         None``) the full Markdown transcript is sent. On detected prefix
1646:         divergence (edit, branch switch, or truncated history) the agent
1647:         subprocess is killed, respawned, and re-handshaked before resending the
1648:         full transcript. On an idempotent retry (same message list as the last
1649:         successful turn) only the last user line is sent. Otherwise an
1650:         append-only tail is shipped. A failed post-respawn handshake kills the
1651:         runtime so the next request respawns a fresh child instead of reusing a
1652:         half-initialized stdio session.
1653:         """
1654: 
1655:         state = runtime.history_state
1656:         if state is None:
1657:             user_message = ACPTranscriptSerializer.serialize(messages)
1658:             new_history_state = HistoryState(
1659:                 message_count=len(messages),
1660:                 prefix_hash=self._hash_messages_prefix(messages, len(messages)),
1661:             )
1662:             return user_message, new_history_state
1663: 
1664:         n = state.message_count
1665:         prefix_hash = state.prefix_hash
1666:         diverged = (
1667:             len(messages) < n or self._hash_messages_prefix(messages, n) != prefix_hash
1668:         )
1669: 
1670:         # Prefix edit, branch switch, or truncated history vs. what the agent saw.
1671:         if diverged:
1672:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
1673:                 logger.info(
1674:                     "History diverged or shrank; resetting agent process "
1675:                     "(project=%s model=%s client_session=%s)",
1676:                     runtime.project_dir,
1677:                     runtime.model,
1678:                     runtime.client_session_id,
1679:                 )
1680:             await self._kill_runtime(runtime)
1681:             await self._spawn_process(runtime)
1682:             try:
1683:                 await self._initialize_runtime(runtime)
1684:             except Exception:
1685:                 # A failed post-respawn handshake leaves a broken child; kill
1686:                 # it so the next request respawns a fresh process instead of
1687:                 # reusing the half-initialized stdio session.
1688:                 await self._kill_runtime(runtime)
1689:                 raise
1690:             user_message = ACPTranscriptSerializer.serialize(messages)
1691:             new_history_state = HistoryState(
1692:                 message_count=len(messages),
1693:                 prefix_hash=self._hash_messages_prefix(messages, len(messages)),
1694:             )
1695:             return user_message, new_history_state
1696: 
1697:         # Same message list as the last successful turn (e.g. client retry).
1698:         if len(messages) == n:
1699:             user_message = self._extract_user_message_as_string(messages)
1700:             return user_message, state
1701: 
1702:         # Append-only: the agent already saw messages[:n]; ship incremental context.
1703:         user_message = ACPTranscriptSerializer.serialize_tail(messages, n)
1704:         if not user_message.strip():
1705:             user_message = self._extract_user_message_as_string(messages)
1706:         new_history_state = HistoryState(
1707:             message_count=len(messages),
1708:             prefix_hash=self._hash_messages_prefix(messages, len(messages)),
1709:         )
1710:         return user_message, new_history_state
1711: 
1712:     async def _prepare_turn_request_locked(
1713:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest
1714:     ) -> tuple[int, str]:
1715:         """Build ``session/prompt`` text and JSON-RPC id under ``runtime.request_lock``.
1716: 
1717:         ACP default: spawn + handshake, compute the user message and new history
1718:         state via :meth:`_compute_history_and_user_message`, send
1719:         ``session/prompt``, and commit ``history_state`` immediately. Codex
1720:         overrides to send ``turn/start`` and stage ``pending_history_state``
1721:         instead (committed only on a successful ``turn/completed``).
1722:         """
1723: 
1724:         await self._cancel_stale_kill_timer(runtime)
1725:         await self._spawn_process(runtime)
1726:         await self._initialize_runtime(runtime)
1727: 
1728:         messages = list(request.processed_messages)
1729:         if not messages:
1730:             raise BackendError(message="No messages found in request")
1731: 
1732:         user_message, new_history_state = await self._compute_history_and_user_message(
1733:             runtime, messages
1734:         )
1735:         if not user_message:
1736:             raise BackendError(message="No user message found in request")
1737: 
1738:         requested_model = request.effective_model or add_vendor_prefix(
1739:             runtime.model, self.VENDOR_PREFIX
1740:         )
1741:         prompt_params: dict[str, Any] = {
1742:             "sessionId": runtime.session_id,
1743:             "prompt": [{"type": "text", "text": user_message}],
1744:             "messageId": str(uuid.uuid4()),
1745:         }
1746:         prompt_request_id = await self._send_jsonrpc_message(
1747:             runtime, "session/prompt", prompt_params
1748:         )
1749:         runtime.history_state = new_history_state
1750:         return prompt_request_id, requested_model
1751: 
1752:     async def _stream_response_with_lock(
1753:         self,
1754:         runtime: RuntimeT,
1755:         requested_model: str,
1756:         prompt_request_id: int,
1757:         request_generation: int | None = None,
1758:     ) -> AsyncGenerator[ProcessedResponse, None]:
1759:         stream_completed = False
1760:         natural_cleanup_done = False
1761:         inner = self._stream_response(runtime, requested_model, prompt_request_id)
1762:         try:
1763:             # Closing the inner generator is essential when a downstream
1764:             # adapter stops at the terminal [DONE] chunk.  Without this,
1765:             # ``_stream_response`` remains suspended immediately after its
1766:             # final yield and this wrapper never reaches teardown reliably.
1767:             async with contextlib.aclosing(inner):
1768:                 async for chunk in inner:
1769:                     if self._stream_chunk_is_terminal(chunk):
1770:                         stream_completed = True
1771:                         # A provider terminal JSON chunk (for example Codex's
1772:                         # ``finish_reason="stop"``) is followed by the
1773:                         # canonical ``[DONE]`` sentinel from
1774:                         # ``_stream_response``.  Keep the inner iterator alive
1775:                         # in that case so direct consumers still receive the
1776:                         # sentinel; converter consumers may close the nested
1777:                         # generators immediately after this terminal chunk.
1778:                         if chunk.content == self._create_sse_done_chunk():
1779:                             await inner.aclose()
1780:                         if not natural_cleanup_done:
1781:                             await self._invalidate_active_request_generation(
1782:                                 runtime, request_generation
1783:                             )
1784:                             cancellation_in_progress = (
1785:                                 runtime.cancellation_event is not None
1786:                                 and runtime.cancellation_event.is_set()
1787:                             )
1788:                             if not cancellation_in_progress:
1789:                                 if runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
1790:                                     runtime.cancellation_event.clear()
1791:                                 await self._schedule_stale_kill_after_turn(runtime)
1792:                                 await self._release_runtime_request_lock(runtime)
1793:                             natural_cleanup_done = True
1794:                     yield chunk
1795:                 stream_completed = True
1796:         finally:
1797:             if not stream_completed:
1798:                 # A downstream consumer can close this generator without the
1799:                 # session cancellation coordinator seeing the disconnect. Own
1800:                 # teardown here as well; otherwise the per-runtime lock can
1801:                 # remain held behind a blocked stdout read forever.
1802:                 cancellation_in_progress = (
1803:                     runtime.cancellation_event is not None
1804:                     and runtime.cancellation_event.is_set()
1805:                 )
1806:                 if not cancellation_in_progress:
1807:                     await self._cancel_active_request(
1808:                         runtime,
1809:                         prompt_request_id,
1810:                         expected_generation=request_generation,
1811:                     )
1812:             elif not natural_cleanup_done:
1813:                 # Natural stream end: clear the event, schedule idle stale-kill,
1814:                 # and release the request lock so the next turn can acquire it.
1815:                 cancellation_in_progress = (
1816:                     runtime.cancellation_event is not None
1817:                     and runtime.cancellation_event.is_set()
1818:                 )
1819:                 if not cancellation_in_progress:
1820:                     await self._invalidate_active_request_generation(
1821:                         runtime, request_generation
1822:                     )
1823:                     if runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
1824:                         runtime.cancellation_event.clear()
1825:                     await self._schedule_stale_kill_after_turn(runtime)
1826:                     await self._release_runtime_request_lock(runtime)
1827: 
1828:     async def _invalidate_active_request_generation(
1829:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, request_generation: int | None
1830:     ) -> None:
1831:         """Make a completed stream's cancel callback a no-op before unlock.
1832: 
1833:         Streaming envelopes can report a client disconnect after the terminal
1834:         ``[DONE]`` chunk has already been delivered.  The callback belongs to
1835:         the completed turn, while the pooled runtime may already be serving a
1836:         newer turn by the time it runs.  Serialize invalidation with active
1837:         cancellation so the old callback cannot kill or unlock the newer turn.
1838:         """
1839: 
1840:         if request_generation is None:
1841:             return
1842:         cancellation_lock = runtime.cancellation_lock
1843:         if cancellation_lock is None:
1844:             if runtime.active_request_generation == request_generation:
1845:                 runtime.active_request_generation = None
1846:             return
1847:         async with cancellation_lock:
1848:             if runtime.active_request_generation == request_generation:
1849:                 runtime.active_request_generation = None
1850: 
1851:     async def _release_runtime_request_lock(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
1852:         if runtime.request_lock is not None and runtime.request_lock.locked():
1853:             runtime.request_lock.release()
1854:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1855:                 logger.debug(
1856:                     "ACP request lock released: project=%s model=%s client_session=%s",
1857:                     runtime.project_dir,
1858:                     runtime.model,
1859:                     runtime.client_session_id,
1860:                 )
1861: 
1862:     async def _acquire_runtime_request_lock(self, runtime: RuntimeT) -> None:
1863:         if runtime.request_lock is None:
1864:             raise BackendError(message="ACP runtime is missing request lock")
1865:         started = time.monotonic()
1866:         await runtime.request_lock.acquire()
1867:         waited = time.monotonic() - started
1868:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1869:             logger.debug(
1870:                 "ACP request lock acquired: waited=%.3fs project=%s model=%s "
1871:                 "client_session=%s",
1872:                 waited,
1873:                 runtime.project_dir,
1874:                 runtime.model,
1875:                 runtime.client_session_id,
1876:             )
1877:         if waited >= 1.0:
1878:             logger.warning(
1879:                 "ACP request lock waited %.3fs: project=%s model=%s client_session=%s",
1880:                 waited,
1881:                 runtime.project_dir,
1882:                 runtime.model,
1883:                 runtime.client_session_id,
1884:             )
1885: 
1886:     async def _wait_for_process_exit(
1887:         self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes], timeout_s: float
1888:     ) -> bool:
1889:         if process.poll() is not None:
1890:             return True
1891:         try:
1892:             await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.to_thread(process.wait), timeout=timeout_s)
1893:             return True
1894:         except asyncio.TimeoutError:
1895:             return False
1896:         except Exception:
1897:             return process.poll() is not None
1898: 
1899:     async def _attempt_graceful_cancel(
1900:         self, runtime: RuntimeT, request_id: int, total_timeout_s: float
1901:     ) -> bool:
1902:         process = runtime.process
1903:         if process is None or process.poll() is not None:
1904:             return True
1905: 
1906:         deadline = time.monotonic() + total_timeout_s
1907:         for method in ACP_CANCEL_METHODS:
1908:             remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
1909:             if remaining <= 0:
1910:                 return False
1911:             try:
1912:                 await self._send_jsonrpc_message(
1913:                     runtime,
1914:                     method,
1915:                     {
1916:                         "sessionId": runtime.session_id,
1917:                         "requestId": request_id,
1918:                         "messageId": str(request_id),
1919:                     },
1920:                 )
1921:             except Exception:
1922:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1923:                     logger.debug("ACP cancel method %s failed or not supported", method)
1924:                 continue
1925: 
1926:             exited = await self._wait_for_process_exit(
1927:                 process, timeout_s=min(remaining, 1.5)
1928:             )
1929:             if exited:
1930:                 return True
1931: 
1932:         if process.stdin is not None:
1933:             with contextlib.suppress(OSError, ValueError):
1934:                 process.stdin.close()
1935:             remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
1936:             if remaining > 0 and await self._wait_for_process_exit(
1937:                 process, timeout_s=min(remaining, 3.0)
1938:             ):
1939:                 return True
1940: 
1941:         return process.poll() is not None
1942: 
1943:     async def _cancel_active_request(
1944:         self,
1945:         runtime: RuntimeT,
1946:         prompt_request_id: int,
1947:         expected_generation: int | None = None,
1948:     ) -> bool:
1949:         """Cancel the request iff it still owns the pooled runtime.
1950: 
1951:         ``expected_generation`` is supplied by streaming envelopes.  A stale
1952:         callback from a completed stream must not tear down a newer request or
1953:         release its request lock.  Calls without a generation retain the
1954:         unconditional cancellation behavior used by non-streaming requests.
1955:         """
1956: 
1957:         cancellation_lock = runtime.cancellation_lock
1958:         if cancellation_lock is None:
1959:             if (
1960:                 expected_generation is not None
1961:                 and runtime.active_request_generation != expected_generation
1962:             ):
1963:                 return False
1964:             if runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
1965:                 runtime.cancellation_event.set()
1966:             try:
1967:                 await self._kill_runtime(runtime)
1968:             finally:
1969:                 if (
1970:                     expected_generation is None
1971:                     or runtime.active_request_generation == expected_generation
1972:                 ):
1973:                     runtime.active_request_generation = None
1974:                 if runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
1975:                     runtime.cancellation_event.clear()
1976:                 await self._release_runtime_request_lock(runtime)
1977:             return True
1978: 
1979:         async with cancellation_lock:
1980:             if (
1981:                 expected_generation is not None
1982:                 and runtime.active_request_generation != expected_generation
1983:             ):
1984:                 return False
1985:             if runtime.cancellation_event is not None:
1986:                 runtime.cancellation_event.set()
1987:             try:
1988:                 process = runtime.process
1989:                 if process is None or process.poll() is not None:
1990:                     # Process already gone (or never attached): still stop the
1991:                     # stderr drain and close any leftover pipe handles so a
1992:                     # later respawn cannot leak FDs or mix diagnostics.
1993:                     self._cleanup_runtime_state(runtime, process)
1994:                     return True
1995: 
1996:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
1997:                     logger.info(
1998:                         "Cancelling active ACP request (pid=%s), "
1999:                         "attempting graceful cancellation then process kill",
2000:                         process.pid,

That new-session stall after many turns smells like a pool lock leak — I'm pulling the latest logs to confirm.

90: func (a *AgyAgent) NewSession(ctx context.Context, params acp.NewSessionRequest) (acp.NewSessionResponse, error) {

121:

124: 		ConfigOptions: a.buildConfigOptions(sess),

129: func (a *AgyAgent) Prompt(ctx context.Context, params acp.PromptRequest) (acp.PromptResponse, error) {
130: 	sid := string(params.SessionId)
131: 	sess, ok := a.store.Get(sid)
132: 	if !ok {
133: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s not found", sid)
134: 	}
135: 
136: 	promptText := extractPromptText(params.Prompt)
137: 	if promptText == "" {
138: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("empty prompt")
139: 	}
140: 
141: 	promptCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
142: 	token, _, ok := a.startPrompt(sid, cancel)
143: 	if !ok {
144: 		cancel()
145: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s already has an active prompt", sid)
146: 	}
147: 	defer a.finishPrompt(sid, token)
148: 
149: 	unlock, err := a.coordinator.Lock(promptCtx, sess.Cwd)
150: 	if err != nil {
151: 		if promptCtx.Err() == context.Canceled {
152: 			return acp.PromptResponse{StopReason: acp.StopReasonCancelled}, nil
153: 		}
154: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("coordinate repository prompt: %w", err)
155: 	}
156: 	defer func() {
157: 		if err := unlock(); err != nil {
158: 			slog.Warn("unlock repository prompt failed", "sessionId", sid, "error", err)
159: 		}
160: 	}()
161: 
162: 	if sess.IsClosed() {
163: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s is closed", sid)
164: 	}
165: 	if a.cfg.InjectExecutionEnvNote {
166: 		promptText = applyExecutionEnvNote(promptText, sess.EnvNoteInjected())
167: 		sess.MarkEnvNoteInjected()
168: 	}
169: 	sess.AddUserMessage(promptText)
170: 
171: 	var streamedMu sync.Mutex
172: 	var streamedBuf strings.Builder
173: 	response, err := a.executeTurn(promptCtx, sess, promptText, func(event agy.StreamEvent) {
174: 		update, ok := streamEventUpdate(event)
175: 		if !ok {
176: 			return
177: 		}
178: 		if a.conn == nil {
179: 			return
180: 		}
181: 		if err := a.conn.SessionUpdate(promptCtx, acp.SessionNotification{
182: 			SessionId: params.SessionId,
183: 			Update:    update,
184: 		}); err != nil {
185: 			slog.Warn("send streamed session update failed", "sessionId", sid, "error", err)
186: 			return
187: 		}
188: 
189: 		if event.Kind == agy.StreamEventText {
190: 			streamedMu.Lock()
191: 			streamedBuf.WriteString(event.Text)
192: 			streamedMu.Unlock()
193: 		}
194: 	})
195: 	if err != nil {
196: 		if promptCtx.Err() == context.Canceled {
197: 			return acp.PromptResponse{StopReason: acp.StopReasonCancelled}, nil
198: 		}
199: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, promptRequestError(err)
200: 	}
201: 
202: 	sess.AddAssistantMessage(response)
203: 
204: 	streamedMu.Lock()
205: 	streamedText := streamedBuf.String()
206: 	streamedMu.Unlock()
207: 
208: 	tail := response
209: 	if streamedText != "" && strings.HasPrefix(response, streamedText) {
210: 		tail = response[len(streamedText):]
211: 	}
212: 
213: 	if tail != "" && a.conn != nil {
214: 		if err := a.conn.SessionUpdate(ctx, acp.SessionNotification{
215: 			SessionId: params.SessionId,
216: 			Update:    acp.UpdateAgentMessageText(tail),
217: 		}); err != nil {
218: 			return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("send session update: %w", err)
219: 		}
220: 	}
221: 
222: 	return acp.PromptResponse{StopReason: acp.StopReasonEndTurn}, nil
223: }
224: 
225: func (a *AgyAgent) executeTurn(ctx context.Context, sess *session.Context, promptText string, onEvent func(agy.StreamEvent)) (string, error) {
226: 	attempts := a.quotaRetryAttempts()
227: 	turnDeadline := time.Now().Add(time.Duration(a.cfg.TimeoutSeconds) * time.Second)
228: 	if deadline, ok := ctx.Deadline(); ok && deadline.Before(turnDeadline) {
229: 		turnDeadline = deadline
230: 	}
231: 
232: 	prompt := promptText
233: 	var native nativeAttemptState
234: 	var lastErr error
235: 	for attempt := 0; attempt < attempts; attempt++ {
236: 		remaining := time.Until(turnDeadline)
237: 		if remaining < 0 {
238: 			remaining = 0
239: 		}
240: 		timeout := remaining
241: 		configured := time.Duration(a.cfg.TimeoutSeconds) * time.Second
242: 		if configured > 0 && (timeout == 0 || timeout > configured) {
243: 			timeout = configured
244: 		}
245: 		out, err := a.executeTurnOnce(ctx, sess, prompt, timeout, onEvent, &native)
246: 		if err == nil {
247: 			return out, nil
248: 		}
249: 		lastErr = err
250: 		if ctx.Err() != nil {
251: 			return "", err
252: 		}
253: 		if !agy.IsProviderLimitError(err) || attempt+1 >= attempts {
254: 			return "", err
255: 		}
256: 		wait, ok := agy.NextProviderLimitWait(attempt, err, remaining, a.quotaRetryMaxWait())
257: 		if !ok {
258: 			return "", err
259: 		}
260: 		notice := fmt.Sprintf("\nProvider rate limit; waiting %s before retry %d/%d.\n", wait.Round(time.Second), attempt+2, attempts)
261: 		if onEvent != nil {
262: 			onEvent(agy.StreamEvent{Kind: agy.StreamEventText, Text: notice})
263: 		}
264: 		slog.Warn("provider rate limit, backing off before native continue",
265: 			"sessionId", sess.ID,
266: 			"wait", wait,
267: 			"attempt", attempt+2,
268: 			"attempts", attempts,
269: 			"error", err,
270: 		)
271: 		if sleepErr := a.sleep(ctx, wait); sleepErr != nil {
272: 			return "", sleepErr
273: 		}
274: 		if sess.GetConversationID() != "" {
275: 			prompt = providerLimitContinuePrompt
276: 		}
277: 	}
278: 	return "", lastErr
279: }
280: 
281: const providerLimitContinuePrompt = "Continue from where you left off. Do not restart the task."
282: 
283: type nativeAttemptState struct {
284: 	previousID     string
285: 	startedAt      time.Time
286: 	snapshotDone   bool
287: 	snapshotFailed bool
288: }
289: 
290: func (a *AgyAgent) quotaRetryAttempts() int {
291: 	if a.cfg == nil || a.cfg.QuotaRetryAttempts < 1 {
292: 		return config.DefaultQuotaRetryAttempts
293: 	}
294: 	return a.cfg.QuotaRetryAttempts
295: }
296: 
297: func (a *AgyAgent) quotaRetryMaxWait() time.Duration {
298: 	if a.cfg == nil || a.cfg.QuotaRetryMaxWaitSeconds <= 0 {
299: 		return 0
300: 	}
301: 	return time.Duration(a.cfg.QuotaRetryMaxWaitSeconds) * time.Second
302: }
303: 
304: func sleepContext(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
305: 	if d <= 0 {
306: 		return ctx.Err()
307: 	}
308: 	timer := time.NewTimer(d)
309: 	defer timer.Stop()
310: 	select {
311: 	case <-ctx.Done():
312: 		return ctx.Err()
313: 	case <-timer.C:
314: 		return nil
315: 	}
316: }
317: 
318: func (a *AgyAgent) executeTurnOnce(ctx context.Context, sess *session.Context, promptText string, timeout time.Duration, onEvent func(agy.StreamEvent), native *nativeAttemptState) (string, error) {
319: 	mode := sess.GetMode()
320: 	convID := sess.GetConversationID()
321: 	turnCount := sess.GetTurnCount()
322: 
323: 	nativeModel, err := a.modelCatalog.ResolveNative(sess.GetModel(), sess.GetReasoningEffort())
324: 	if err != nil {
325: 		return "", err
326: 	}
327: 
328: 	opts := agy.ExecuteOpts{
329: 		Cwd:       sess.Cwd,
330: 		Model:     nativeModel,
331: 		Timeout:   timeout,
332: 		SkipPerms: a.cfg.SkipPerms,
333: 	}
334: 
335: 	switch {
336: 	case mode == session.ModeFallbackContext:
337: 		return a.executeFallbackTurn(ctx, sess, opts, promptText, onEvent)
338: 
339: 	case convID != "":
340: 		opts.ConversationID = convID
341: 		opts.Prompt = promptText
342: 		if a.promptWriter.NeedsFile(promptText) {
343: 			path, err := a.promptWriter.WritePromptFile(sess.Cwd, sess.ID, turnCount, promptText)
344: 			if err != nil {
345: 				return "", fmt.Errorf("write prompt file: %w", err)
346: 			}
347: 			opts.PromptFilePath = path
348: 			opts.Prompt = ""
349: 		}
350: 
351: 		resp, err := a.runner.ExecuteStream(ctx, opts, onEvent)
352: 		captureConversationID(sess, resp)
353: 		out, err := turnOutput(resp, err, opts)
354: 		if err != nil {
355: 			if !agy.ShouldFallback(err) {
356: 				return "", err
357: 			}
358: 			slog.Warn("native conversation failed, switching to fallback", "error", err, "sessionId", sess.ID)
359: 			sess.SwitchToFallback()
360: 			return a.executeFallbackTurn(ctx, sess, opts, promptText, onEvent)
361: 		}
362: 		return out, nil
363: 
364: 	default:
365: 		if native.snapshotFailed {
366: 			sess.SwitchToFallback()
367: 			return a.executeFallbackTurn(ctx, sess, opts, promptText, onEvent)
368: 		}
369: 		if !native.snapshotDone {
370: 			previousID, snapErr := a.discoverer.SnapshotConversationID(ctx, sess.Cwd)
371: 			native.snapshotDone = true
372: 			if snapErr != nil {
373: 				slog.Warn("conversation cache snapshot failed, using fallback context", "error", snapErr, "sessionId", sess.ID)
374: 				native.snapshotFailed = true
375: 				sess.SwitchToFallback()
376: 				return a.executeFallbackTurn(ctx, sess, opts, promptText, onEvent)
377: 			}
378: 			native.previousID = previousID
379: 			native.startedAt = time.Now()
380: 		}
381: 		opts.Prompt = promptText
382: 		if a.promptWriter.NeedsFile(promptText) {
383: 			path, err := a.promptWriter.WritePromptFile(sess.Cwd, sess.ID, turnCount, promptText)
384: 			if err != nil {
385: 				return "", fmt.Errorf("write prompt file: %w", err)
386: 			}
387: 			opts.PromptFilePath = path
388: 			opts.Prompt = ""
389: 		}
390: 
391: 		resp, err := a.runner.ExecuteStream(ctx, opts, onEvent)
392: 		captureConversationID(sess, resp)
393: 		out, err := turnOutput(resp, err, opts)
394: 		if err != nil {
395: 			if sess.GetConversationID() == "" && !native.startedAt.IsZero() {
396: 				_ = a.discoverAndSetConversationID(ctx, sess, native.previousID, native.startedAt)
397: 			}
398: 			return "", err
399: 		}
400: 
401: 		if sess.GetConversationID() == "" {
402: 			if !a.discoverAndSetConversationID(ctx, sess, native.previousID, native.startedAt) {
403: 				sess.SwitchToFallback()
404: 			}
405: 		}
406: 
407: 		return out, nil
408: 	}
409: }
410: 
411: func captureConversationID(sess *session.Context, resp *agy.Response) {
412: 	if resp == nil || strings.TrimSpace(resp.ConversationID) == "" {
413: 		return
414: 	}
415: 	if sess.GetConversationID() == resp.ConversationID {
416: 		return
417: 	}
418: 	sess.SetConversationID(resp.ConversationID)
419: 	slog.Info("conversation ID received from agy stream", "conversationId", resp.ConversationID, "sessionId", sess.ID)
420: }
421: 
422: func (a *AgyAgent) executeFallbackTurn(ctx context.Context, sess *session.Context, opts agy.ExecuteOpts, promptText string, onEvent func(agy.StreamEvent)) (string, error) {
423: 	transcript := sess.GetTranscript()
424: 	turnCount := sess.GetTurnCount()
425: 	if len(transcript) > 0 && transcript[len(transcript)-1].Role == session.RoleUser && transcript[len(transcript)-1].Content == promptText {
426: 		transcript = transcript[:len(transcript)-1]
427: 	}
428: 
429: 	contextPath, err := a.promptWriter.WriteContextDump(sess.Cwd, sess.ID, turnCount, transcript, promptText)
430: 	if err != nil {
431: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("write context dump: %w", err)
432: 	}
433: 
434: 	opts.ConversationID = ""
435: 	opts.Prompt = ""
436: 	opts.PromptFilePath = contextPath
437: 
438: 	resp, err := a.runner.ExecuteStream(ctx, opts, onEvent)
439: 	return turnOutput(resp, err, opts)
440: }
441: 
442: func turnOutput(resp *agy.Response, err error, opts agy.ExecuteOpts) (string, error) {
443: 	if err != nil {
444: 		return "", err
445: 	}
446: 	if resp == nil {
447: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("agy returned an empty response")
448: 	}
449: 	if resp.TimedOut {
450: 		return "", &agy.TimeoutError{Timeout: opts.Timeout, Detail: resp.Output}
451: 	}
452: 	return resp.Output, nil
453: }
454: 
455: const maxToolTitleCommandChars = 120
456: 
457: func toolCallTitle(name string, rawInput any) string {
458: 	title := strings.TrimSpace(name)
459: 	if title == "" {
460: 		title = "AGY tool"
461: 	}
462: 	command := commandLineFromInput(rawInput)
463: 	if command == "" {
464: 		return title
465: 	}
466: 	if len(command) > maxToolTitleCommandChars {
467: 		command = command[:maxToolTitleCommandChars] + "…"
468: 	}
469: 	return title + ": " + command
470: }
471: 
472: func commandLineFromInput(rawInput any) string {
473: 	fields, ok := rawInput.(map[string]any)
474: 	if !ok {
475: 		return ""
476: 	}
477: 	for _, key := range []string{"CommandLine", "commandLine", "command", "cmd"} {
478: 		value, ok := fields[key].(string)
479: 		if !ok {
480: 			continue
481: 		}
482: 		if command := strings.TrimSpace(value); command != "" {
483: 			return command
484: 		}
485: 	}
486: 	return ""
487: }
488: 
489: func toolCallUpdateStatus(state string) acp.ToolCallStatus {
490: 	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(state)) {
491: 	case "DONE", "COMPLETED", "COMPLETE", "SUCCESS":
492: 		return acp.ToolCallStatusCompleted
493: 	case "ERROR", "FAILED", "FAIL", "CANCELLED", "CANCELED", "REJECTED":
494: 		return acp.ToolCallStatusFailed
495: 	default:
496: 		return acp.ToolCallStatusInProgress
497: 	}
498: }
499: 
500: func streamEventUpdate(event agy.StreamEvent) (acp.SessionUpdate, bool) {
501: 	switch event.Kind {
502: 	case agy.StreamEventText:
503: 		if event.Text == "" {
504: 			return acp.SessionUpdate{}, false
505: 		}
506: 		return acp.UpdateAgentMessageText(event.Text), true
507: 	case agy.StreamEventToolStart:
508: 		title := toolCallTitle(event.ToolName, event.RawInput)
509: 		opts := []acp.ToolCallStartOpt{acp.WithStartStatus(acp.ToolCallStatusInProgress)}
510: 		if event.RawInput != nil {
511: 			opts = append(opts, acp.WithStartRawInput(event.RawInput))
512: 		}
513: 		return acp.StartToolCall(acp.ToolCallId(event.ToolID), title, opts...), true
514: 	case agy.StreamEventToolUpdate:
515: 		opts := []acp.ToolCallUpdateOpt{acp.WithUpdateStatus(toolCallUpdateStatus(event.ToolState))}
516: 		if event.RawOutput != nil {
517: 			opts = append(opts, acp.WithUpdateRawOutput(event.RawOutput))
518: 		}
519: 		return acp.UpdateToolCall(acp.ToolCallId(event.ToolID), opts...), true
520: 	default:
521: 		return acp.SessionUpdate{}, false
522: 	}
523: }
524: 
525: func (a *AgyAgent) discoverAndSetConversationID(ctx context.Context, sess *session.Context, previousID string, startedAt time.Time) bool {
526: 	id, err := a.discoverer.DiscoverNewConversationID(ctx, sess.Cwd, previousID, startedAt)
527: 	if err != nil {
528: 		slog.Warn("conversation attribution failed, switching to fallback", "error", err, "sessionId", sess.ID)
529: 		return false
530: 	}
531: 	sess.SetConversationID(id)
532: 	slog.Info("conversation ID discovered", "conversationId", id, "sessionId", sess.ID)
533: 	return true
534: }

536: func (a *AgyAgent) Cancel(ctx context.Context, params acp.CancelNotification) error {
537: 	sid := string(params.SessionId)
538: 	if cancel, _ := a.cancelPrompt(sid); cancel != nil {
539: 		cancel()
540: 		slog.Info("cancelled active prompt", "sessionId", sid)
541: 		return nil
542: 	}
543: 	slog.Info("cancel received with no active prompt", "sessionId", sid)
544: 	return nil
545: }
546: 
547: func (a *AgyAgent) CloseSession(ctx context.Context, params acp.CloseSessionRequest) (acp.CloseSessionResponse, error) {
548: 	sid := string(params.SessionId)
549: 	finalizer := a.beginSessionFinalizer(sid)
550: 	if finalizer == nil {
551: 		return acp.CloseSessionResponse{}, nil
552: 	}
553: 	select {
554: 	case <-finalizer.done:
555: 		a.mu.Lock()
556: 		err := finalizer.err
557: 		a.mu.Unlock()
558: 		if err != nil {
559: 			return acp.CloseSessionResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("finalize session %s: %w", sid, err)
560: 		}
561: 		slog.Info("session closed", "sessionId", sid)
562: 		return acp.CloseSessionResponse{}, nil
563: 	case <-ctx.Done():
564: 		// Finalization intentionally continues after the request deadline.
565: 		return acp.CloseSessionResponse{}, ctx.Err()
566: 	}
567: }

581: const (
582: 	modelConfigID           = "model"
583: 	reasoningEffortConfigID = "reasoning_effort"

586: func (a *AgyAgent) SetSessionConfigOption(ctx context.Context, params acp.SetSessionConfigOptionRequest) (acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse, error) {
587: 	if params.ValueId == nil {
588: 		return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("unsupported config option type")
589: 	}
590: 
591: 	configID := string(params.ValueId.ConfigId)
592: 	if configID != modelConfigID && configID != reasoningEffortConfigID {
593: 		return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("unknown config option: %s", params.ValueId.ConfigId)
594: 	}
595: 
596: 	sid := string(params.ValueId.SessionId)
597: 	sess, ok := a.store.Get(sid)
598: 	if !ok {
599: 		return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s not found", sid)
600: 	}
601: 
602: 	value := string(params.ValueId.Value)
603: 	switch configID {
604: 	case modelConfigID:
605: 		newModel, err := a.modelCatalog.ResolveCanonical(value)
606: 		if err != nil {
607: 			return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{}, err
608: 		}
609: 		if _, err := a.modelCatalog.ResolveNative(newModel, sess.GetReasoningEffort()); err != nil {
610: 			return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{}, err
611: 		}
612: 		sess.SetModel(newModel)
613: 		slog.Info("model changed", "sessionId", sid, "model", newModel)
614: 	case reasoningEffortConfigID:
615: 		if _, err := a.modelCatalog.ResolveNative(sess.GetModel(), value); err != nil {
616: 			return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{}, err
617: 		}
618: 		sess.SetReasoningEffort(value)
619: 		slog.Info("reasoning effort changed", "sessionId", sid, "reasoningEffort", value)
620: 	}
621: 
622: 	return acp.SetSessionConfigOptionResponse{
623: 		ConfigOptions: a.buildConfigOptions(sess),
624: 	}, nil
625: }
626: 
627: func (a *AgyAgent) buildConfigOptions(sess *session.Context) []acp.SessionConfigOption {
628: 	currentModel := sess.GetModel()
629: 	if !a.modelCatalog.Contains(currentModel) {
630: 		currentModel = a.modelCatalog.DefaultModelID()
631: 	}
632: 
633: 	profiles := a.modelCatalog.Profiles()
634: 	modelOptions := make(acp.SessionConfigSelectOptionsUngrouped, 0, len(profiles))
635: 	for _, profile := range profiles {
636: 		modelOptions = append(modelOptions, acp.SessionConfigSelectOption{
637: 			Value: acp.SessionConfigValueId(profile.CanonicalID),
638: 			Name:  profile.DisplayName,
639: 		})
640: 	}
641: 
642: 	modelCategory := acp.SessionConfigOptionCategoryModel
643: 	result := []acp.SessionConfigOption{{
644: 		Select: &acp.SessionConfigOptionSelect{
645: 			Id:           acp.SessionConfigId(modelConfigID),
646: 			Name:         "Model",
647: 			Type:         "select",
648: 			Category:     &modelCategory,
649: 			CurrentValue: acp.SessionConfigValueId(currentModel),
650: 			Options:      acp.SessionConfigSelectOptions{Ungrouped: &modelOptions},
651: 		},
652: 	}}
653: 
654: 	efforts := a.modelCatalog.SupportedEfforts(currentModel)
655: 	if len(efforts) == 0 {
656: 		return result
657: 	}
658: 	currentEffort := sess.GetReasoningEffort()
659: 	if currentEffort == "" {
660: 		for _, profile := range profiles {
661: 			if profile.CanonicalID == currentModel {
662: 				currentEffort = profile.DefaultEffort
663: 				break
664: 			}
665: 		}
666: 	}
667: 	effortOptions := make(acp.SessionConfigSelectOptionsUngrouped, 0, len(efforts))
668: 	for _, effort := range efforts {
669: 		effortOptions = append(effortOptions, acp.SessionConfigSelectOption{
670: 			Value: acp.SessionConfigValueId(effort),
671: 			Name:  strings.ToUpper(effort[:1]) + effort[1:],
672: 		})
673: 	}
674: 	result = append(result, acp.SessionConfigOption{Select: &acp.SessionConfigOptionSelect{
675: 		Id:           acp.SessionConfigId(reasoningEffortConfigID),
676: 		Name:         "Reasoning effort",
677: 		Type:         "select",
678: 		CurrentValue: acp.SessionConfigValueId(currentEffort),
679: 		Options:      acp.SessionConfigSelectOptions{Ungrouped: &effortOptions},
680: 	}})
681: 	return result
682: }

688: func (a *AgyAgent) Close() {
689: 	a.mu.Lock()
690: 	if a.closed {
691: 		finalizers := a.finalizerSnapshotLocked()
692: 		a.mu.Unlock()
693: 		waitFinalizers(finalizers)
694: 		return
695: 	}
696: 	a.closed = true
697: 	sessions := a.store.CloseAll()
698: 	type pendingFinalizer struct {
699: 		sess      *session.Context
700: 		prompt    activePrompt
701: 		finalizer *sessionFinalizer
702: 	}
703: 	pending := make([]pendingFinalizer, 0, len(sessions))
704: 	for _, sess := range sessions {
705: 		if _, exists := a.finalizers[sess.ID]; exists {
706: 			continue
707: 		}
708: 		finalizer := &sessionFinalizer{done: make(chan struct{})}
709: 		a.finalizers[sess.ID] = finalizer
710: 		pending = append(pending, pendingFinalizer{sess: sess, prompt: a.cancels[sess.ID], finalizer: finalizer})
711: 	}
712: 	finalizers := a.finalizerSnapshotLocked()
713: 	a.mu.Unlock()
714: 
715: 	for _, item := range pending {
716: 		a.runSessionFinalizer(item.sess, item.prompt, item.finalizer)
717: 	}
718: 	waitFinalizers(finalizers)
719: 	workdirs := a.closeWorkdirs()
720: 	for _, cwd := range workdirs {
721: 		if err := retryCleanup(func() error { return a.promptWriter.CleanupWorkdir(cwd) }); err != nil {
722: 			slog.Error("workdir cleanup failed", "cwd", cwd, "error", err)
723: 		}
724: 	}
725: }
726: 
727: func waitFinalizers(finalizers []*sessionFinalizer) {
728: 	for _, finalizer := range finalizers {
729: 		<-finalizer.done
730: 		if finalizer.err != nil {
731: 			slog.Error("session finalization failed", "error", finalizer.err)
732: 		}
733: 	}
734: }
735: 
736: func (a *AgyAgent) beginSessionFinalizer(sessionID string) *sessionFinalizer {
737: 	a.mu.Lock()
738: 	if finalizer, ok := a.finalizers[sessionID]; ok {
739: 		a.mu.Unlock()
740: 		return finalizer
741: 	}
742: 	sess, ok := a.store.Get(sessionID)
743: 	if !ok {
744: 		a.mu.Unlock()
745: 		return nil
746: 	}
747: 	sess.Close()
748: 	a.store.Delete(sessionID)
749: 	finalizer := &sessionFinalizer{done: make(chan struct{})}
750: 	a.finalizers[sessionID] = finalizer
751: 	prompt := a.cancels[sessionID]
752: 	a.mu.Unlock()
753: 	a.runSessionFinalizer(sess, prompt, finalizer)
754: 	return finalizer
755: }
756: 
757: func (a *AgyAgent) runSessionFinalizer(sess *session.Context, prompt activePrompt, finalizer *sessionFinalizer) {
758: 	if prompt.cancel != nil {
759: 		prompt.cancel()
760: 	}
761: 	go func() {
762: 		if prompt.done != nil {
763: 			<-prompt.done
764: 		}
765: 		err := retryCleanup(func() error {
766: 			return a.promptWriter.CleanupSession(sess.Cwd, sess.ID)
767: 		})
768: 		a.unregisterWorkdir(sess.Cwd)
769: 		a.mu.Lock()
770: 		finalizer.err = err
771: 		delete(a.finalizers, sess.ID)
772: 		close(finalizer.done)
773: 		a.mu.Unlock()
774: 	}()
775: }
776: 
777: func retryCleanup(cleanup func() error) error {
778: 	var err error
779: 	for attempt := 0; attempt < 5; attempt++ {
780: 		if err = cleanup(); err == nil {
781: 			return nil
782: 		}
783: 		time.Sleep(time.Duration(attempt+1) * 25 * time.Millisecond)
784: 	}
785: 	return err
786: }
787: 
788: func (a *AgyAgent) finalizerSnapshotLocked() []*sessionFinalizer {
789: 	result := make([]*sessionFinalizer, 0, len(a.finalizers))
790: 	for _, finalizer := range a.finalizers {
791: 		result = append(result, finalizer)
792: 	}
793: 	return result
794: }
795: 
796: func (a *AgyAgent) startPrompt(sessionID string, cancel context.CancelFunc) (*struct{}, <-chan struct{}, bool) {
797: 	a.mu.Lock()
798: 	defer a.mu.Unlock()
799: 
800: 	if current, ok := a.cancels[sessionID]; ok {
801: 		return nil, current.done, false
802: 	}
803: 	token := &struct{}{}
804: 	done := make(chan struct{})
805: 	a.cancels[sessionID] = activePrompt{token: token, cancel: cancel, done: done}
806: 	return token, done, true
807: }
808: 
809: func (a *AgyAgent) finishPrompt(sessionID string, token *struct{}) {
810: 	a.mu.Lock()
811: 	defer a.mu.Unlock()
812: 
813: 	if current, ok := a.cancels[sessionID]; ok && current.token == token {
814: 		delete(a.cancels, sessionID)
815: 		close(current.done)
816: 	}
817: }
818: 
819: func (a *AgyAgent) cancelPrompt(sessionID string) (context.CancelFunc, <-chan struct{}) {
820: 	a.mu.Lock()
821: 	defer a.mu.Unlock()
822: 
823: 	prompt, ok := a.cancels[sessionID]
824: 	if !ok {
825: 		return nil, nil
826: 	}
827: 	return prompt.cancel, prompt.done
828: }

830: func canonicalSessionCwd(cwd string) (string, error) {
831: 	absolute, err := filepath.Abs(cwd)
832: 	if err != nil {
833: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("resolve cwd: %w", err)
834: 	}
835: 	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(absolute); err == nil {
836: 		absolute = resolved
837: 	}
838: 	return filepath.Clean(absolute), nil
839: }
840: 
841: func workdirKey(cwd string) string {
842: 	key := filepath.Clean(cwd)
843: 	if runtime.GOOS == "windows" {
844: 		key = strings.ToLower(key)
845: 	}
846: 	return key
847: }
848: 
849: func (a *AgyAgent) unregisterWorkdir(cwd string) bool {
850: 	a.mu.Lock()
851: 	defer a.mu.Unlock()
852: 
853: 	key := workdirKey(cwd)
854: 	if a.workdirs[key] <= 1 {
855: 		delete(a.workdirs, key)
856: 		return true
857: 	}
858: 	a.workdirs[key]--
859: 	return false
860: }
861: 
862: func (a *AgyAgent) closeWorkdirs() []string {
863: 	a.mu.Lock()
864: 	defer a.mu.Unlock()
865: 
866: 	workdirs := make([]string, 0, len(a.workdirs))
867: 	for cwd := range a.workdirs {
868: 		workdirs = append(workdirs, cwd)
869: 	}
870: 	a.workdirs = make(map[string]int)
871: 	return workdirs
872: }

874: func Serve(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config, input io.Reader, output io.Writer) error {
875: 	agent := NewAgyAgent(cfg)
876: 	defer agent.Close()
877: 
878: 	conn := acp.NewAgentSideConnection(agent, output, input)
879: 	conn.SetLogger(slog.Default())
880: 	agent.SetAgentConnection(conn)
881: 
882: 	select {
883: 	case <-ctx.Done():
884: 		return nil
885: 	case <-conn.Done():
886: 		return nil
887: 	}
888: }

895: func promptRequestError(err error) error {
896: 	message := "Agy request failed"
897: 	code := -32001
898: 	if agy.IsProviderLimitError(err) {
899: 		message = "Model provider quota or rate limit exceeded"
900: 		code = providerLimitErrorCode
901: 	} else if agy.IsTimeoutError(err) {
902: 		message = "Agy request timed out"
903: 		code = timeoutErrorCode
904: 	}
905: 	return &acp.RequestError{
906: 		Code:    code,
907: 		Message: message,
908: 		Data:    map[string]any{"error": err.Error()},
909: 	}
910: }

912: func extractPromptText(blocks []acp.ContentBlock) string {
913: 	var parts []string
914: 	for _, block := range blocks {
915: 		if block.Text != nil {
916: 			parts = append(parts, block.Text.Text)
917: 		}
918: 	}
919: 	return joinNonEmpty(parts)
920: }
921: 
922: func joinNonEmpty(parts []string) string {
923: 	var result []string
924: 	for _, p := range parts {
925: 		if p != "" {
926: 			result = append(result, p)
927: 		}
928: 	}
929: 	if len(result) == 0 {
930: 		return ""
931: 	}
932: 	return strings.Join(result, "\n\n")
933: }

24: 		return nil, err
25: 	}

36: 	s.mu.RUnlock()

40: func (s *Store) Delete(id string) {
41: 	s.mu.Lock()
42: 	if ctx, ok := s.sessions[id]; ok {
43: 		ctx.Close()
44: 		delete(s.sessions, id)
45: 	}
46: 	s.mu.Unlock()
47: }

49: func (s *Store) CloseAll() []*Context {
50: 	s.mu.Lock()
51: 	closed := make([]*Context, 0, len(s.sessions))
52: 	for id, ctx := range s.sessions {
53: 		ctx.Close()
54: 		closed = append(closed, ctx)
55: 		delete(s.sessions, id)
56: 	}
57: 	s.mu.Unlock()
58: 	return closed

61: func generateSessionID() (string, error) {
62: 	var b [16]byte
63: 	if _, err := rand.Read(b[:]); err != nil {
64: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("generate session ID: %w", err)
65: 	}
66: 	return "sess_" + hex.EncodeToString(b[:]), nil
67: }

16: const repoLockRetryInterval = 25 * time.Millisecond
17: 
18: // RepoCoordinator serializes AGY operations for the same working directory.
19: // Different directories use distinct cache entries and can run concurrently.
20: type RepoCoordinator struct {
21: 	lockDir string
22: }
23: 
24: func NewRepoCoordinator(configDir string) *RepoCoordinator {
25: 	return &RepoCoordinator{lockDir: filepath.Join(configDir, "wrapper-locks")}
26: }
27: 
28: func (c *RepoCoordinator) Lock(ctx context.Context, cwd string) (func() error, error) {
29: 	canonicalCwd, err := canonicalWorkdir(cwd)
30: 	if err != nil {
31: 		return nil, err
32: 	}
33: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(c.lockDir, 0700); err != nil {
34: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create wrapper lock dir: %w", err)
35: 	}
36: 
37: 	// Hash the canonical path to keep lock names portable and avoid exposing it.
38: 	lockKey := canonicalCwd
39: 	if runtime.GOOS == "windows" {
40: 		lockKey = strings.ToLower(lockKey)
41: 	}
42: 	path := filepath.Join(c.lockDir, fmt.Sprintf("agy-workdir-%x.lock", sha256.Sum256([]byte(lockKey))))
43: 	lock := flock.New(path, flock.SetPermissions(0600))
44: 	locked, err := lock.TryLockContext(ctx, repoLockRetryInterval)
45: 	if err != nil {
46: 		_ = lock.Close()
47: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("lock repository %q: %w", cwd, err)
48: 	}
49: 	if !locked {
50: 		_ = lock.Close()
51: 		return nil, ctx.Err()
52: 	}
53: 	return func() error {
54: 		unlockErr := lock.Unlock()
55: 		closeErr := lock.Close()
56: 		if unlockErr != nil {
57: 			return unlockErr
58: 		}
59: 		return closeErr
60: 	}, nil
61: }
62: 
63: func canonicalWorkdir(cwd string) (string, error) {
64: 	if cwd == "" {
65: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("cwd is required")
66: 	}
67: 	absolute, err := filepath.Abs(cwd)
68: 	if err != nil {
69: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("resolve cwd: %w", err)
70: 	}
71: 	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(absolute); err == nil {
72: 		absolute = resolved
73: 	}
74: 	return filepath.Clean(absolute), nil
75: }

61: func (e *ProcessError) Error() string {
62: 	detail := strings.TrimSpace(e.Detail)
63: 	if IsProviderLimitError(e) {
64: 		if detail == "" {
65: 			return "agy model provider quota or rate limit was exceeded"
66: 		}
67: 		return fmt.Sprintf("agy model provider quota or rate limit was exceeded: %s", detail)
68: 	}
69: 	if detail == "" {
70: 		return fmt.Sprintf("agy request failed (exit code %d)", e.ExitCode)
71: 	}
72: 	return fmt.Sprintf("agy request failed (exit code %d): %s", e.ExitCode, detail)

75: var providerLimitMarkers = []string{
76: 	"quota", "resource_exhausted", "resource exhausted", "rate limit",
77: 	"too many requests", "usage limit", "limit reached", "status 429", "code 429",
78: 	"weighted tokens", "tokens remaining", "tokens left", "out of tokens",
79: }
80: 
81: func looksLikeProviderLimit(message string) bool {
82: 	lower := strings.ToLower(message)
83: 	for _, marker := range providerLimitMarkers {
84: 		if strings.Contains(lower, marker) {
85: 			return true
86: 		}
87: 	}
88: 	return false
89: }
90: 
91: func preferredProviderLimitDetail(text string) string {
92: 	text = strings.TrimSpace(text)
93: 	if text == "" {
94: 		return ""
95: 	}
96: 	var best string
97: 	bestHasExact := false
98: 	for _, line := range strings.Split(text, "\n") {
99: 		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
100: 		if line == "" || !looksLikeProviderLimit(line) {
101: 			continue
102: 		}
103: 		lower := strings.ToLower(line)
104: 		exact := strings.Contains(lower, "resource_exhausted") ||
105: 			strings.Contains(lower, "individual quota") ||
106: 			strings.Contains(lower, "code 429")
107: 		if best == "" || (exact && !bestHasExact) || (exact == bestHasExact && len(line) > len(best)) {
108: 			best = line
109: 			bestHasExact = exact
110: 		}
111: 	}
112: 	if best != "" {
113: 		return best
114: 	}
115: 	return text
116: }
117: 
118: func extractProviderLimitDetail(text string) (string, bool) {
119: 	if !looksLikeProviderLimit(text) {
120: 		return "", false
121: 	}
122: 	return preferredProviderLimitDetail(text), true
123: }
124: 
125: func IsProviderLimitError(err error) bool {
126: 	var processErr *ProcessError
127: 	if !errors.As(err, &processErr) {
128: 		return false
129: 	}
130: 	return looksLikeProviderLimit(processErr.Detail)
131: }

133: // TimeoutError is returned when agy hits the wrapper watchdog or print-timeout
134: // while still waiting (for example on a long-running tool).
135: type TimeoutError struct {
136: 	Timeout time.Duration
137: 	Detail  string
138: }
139: 
140: func (e *TimeoutError) Error() string {
141: 	detail := strings.TrimSpace(e.Detail)
142: 	if e.Timeout > 0 {
143: 		if detail == "" {
144: 			return fmt.Sprintf("agy execution timed out after %s", e.Timeout)
145: 		}
146: 		return fmt.Sprintf("agy execution timed out after %s: %s", e.Timeout, detail)
147: 	}
148: 	if detail == "" {
149: 		return "agy print run timed out while waiting"
150: 	}
151: 	return fmt.Sprintf("agy print run timed out while waiting: %s", detail)
152: }
153: 
154: func IsTimeoutError(err error) bool {
155: 	var timeoutErr *TimeoutError
156: 	return errors.As(err, &timeoutErr)
157: }
158: 
159: // ShouldFallback reports whether a failed native --conversation turn should be
160: // retried with a dumped virtual context. Timeouts and provider limits must not
161: // dump context: timeouts would start another long wait, and quota retries are
162: // handled as wait-and-continue on the native conversation instead.
163: func ShouldFallback(err error) bool {
164: 	if err == nil {
165: 		return false
166: 	}
167: 	return !IsProviderLimitError(err) && !IsTimeoutError(err)
168: }

194: func (r *NonInteractiveRunner) ExecuteStream(ctx context.Context, opts ExecuteOpts, onEvent func(StreamEvent)) (*Response, error) {
195: 	args := r.buildArgs(opts)
196: 	slog.Debug("executing agy", "binary", r.binary, "args", args, "cwd", opts.Cwd)
197: 
198: 	var execCtx context.Context
199: 	var cancel context.CancelFunc
200: 	if killAfter := processKillTimeout(opts.Timeout); killAfter > 0 {
201: 		execCtx, cancel = context.WithTimeout(ctx, killAfter)
202: 	} else {
203: 		execCtx, cancel = context.WithCancel(ctx)
204: 	}
205: 	defer cancel()
206: 
207: 	cmd := exec.CommandContext(execCtx, r.binary, args...)
208: 	cmd.Dir = opts.Cwd
209: 	cmd.Env = r.commandEnv()
210: 	processTree, err := configureProcessTree(cmd)
211: 	if err != nil {
212: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("configure agy process tree: %w", err)
213: 	}
214: 	defer processTree.Close()
215: 
216: 	stdoutPipe, err := cmd.StdoutPipe()
217: 	if err != nil {
218: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("stdout pipe: %w", err)
219: 	}
220: 	stderrPipe, err := cmd.StderrPipe()
221: 	if err != nil {
222: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("stderr pipe: %w", err)
223: 	}
224: 
225: 	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
226: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to execute agy: %w", err)
227: 	}
228: 	if err := processTree.AfterStart(cmd); err != nil {
229: 		_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
230: 		_ = cmd.Wait()
231: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("contain agy process tree: %w", err)
232: 	}
233: 	var stream agyJSONStream
234: 	var stderr bytes.Buffer
235: 	var limitOnce sync.Once
236: 	triggerLimit := func(detail string) {
237: 		stream.noteProviderLimit(detail)
238: 		limitOnce.Do(cancel)
239: 	}
240: 	var readers sync.WaitGroup
241: 	readers.Add(2)
242: 	go func() {
243: 		defer readers.Done()
244: 		stream.read(stdoutPipe, onEvent, triggerLimit)
245: 	}()
246: 	go func() {
247: 		defer readers.Done()
248: 		streamPipeLines(stderrPipe, &stderr, func(_ string) {
249: 			if detail, ok := extractProviderLimitDetail(stderr.String()); ok {
250: 				triggerLimit(detail)
251: 			}
252: 		})
253: 	}()
254: 
255: 	err = cmd.Wait()
256: 	readers.Wait()
257: 
258: 	response := &Response{
259: 		Output:         stream.output(),
260: 		ConversationID: stream.conversationID,
261: 	}
262: 	if quota := stream.providerLimitDetail(); quota != "" {
263: 		exitCode := -1
264: 		if err != nil {
265: 			if exitErr, ok := err.(*exec.ExitError); ok {
266: 				exitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
267: 			}
268: 		} else {
269: 			exitCode = 0
270: 		}
271: 		response.ExitCode = exitCode
272: 		if strings.TrimSpace(response.Output) == "" {
273: 			response.Output = quota
274: 		}
275: 		return response, &ProcessError{ExitCode: exitCode, Detail: quota}
276: 	}
277: 
278: 	if err != nil {
279: 		if execCtx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
280: 			response.TimedOut = true
281: 			response.ExitCode = -1
282: 			return response, &TimeoutError{
283: 				Timeout: printTimeoutDuration(opts.Timeout),
284: 				Detail:  strings.TrimSpace(response.Output),
285: 			}
286: 		}
287: 		if ctx.Err() == context.Canceled {
288: 			response.ExitCode = -1
289: 			return response, ctx.Err()
290: 		}
291: 		if exitErr, ok := err.(*exec.ExitError); ok {
292: 			response.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
293: 			detail := normalizeLineEndings(stderr.String())
294: 			if strings.TrimSpace(detail) == "" {
295: 				detail = response.Output
296: 			} else if IsProviderLimitError(&ProcessError{Detail: response.Output}) &&
297: 				!IsProviderLimitError(&ProcessError{Detail: detail}) {
298: 				detail = strings.TrimSpace(detail) + "\n" + strings.TrimSpace(response.Output)
299: 			}
300: 			if response.Output == "" {
301: 				response.Output = detail
302: 			}
303: 			if termErr := stream.terminalError(response.ExitCode); termErr != nil {
304: 				if IsTimeoutError(termErr) {
305: 					response.TimedOut = true
306: 					return response, termErr
307: 				}
308: 				var procErr *ProcessError
309: 				if errors.As(termErr, &procErr) {
310: 					merged := strings.TrimSpace(procErr.Detail)
311: 					stderrDetail := strings.TrimSpace(detail)
312: 					if stderrDetail != "" && !strings.Contains(merged, stderrDetail) {
313: 						if merged == "" {
314: 							merged = stderrDetail
315: 						} else {
316: 							merged = merged + "\n" + stderrDetail
317: 						}
318: 					}
319: 					return response, &ProcessError{ExitCode: response.ExitCode, Detail: merged}
320: 				}
321: 				return response, termErr
322: 			}
323: 			return response, &ProcessError{ExitCode: response.ExitCode, Detail: detail}
324: 		}
325: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to execute agy: %w", err)
326: 	}
327: 
328: 	response.ExitCode = 0
329: 	if termErr := stream.terminalError(response.ExitCode); termErr != nil {
330: 		if IsTimeoutError(termErr) {
331: 			response.TimedOut = true
332: 		}
333: 		return response, termErr
334: 	}
335: 
336: 	if response.Output == "" && opts.ConversationID != "" {
337: 		if extracted := r.extractFromTranscript(opts.ConversationID); len(extracted) > len(response.Output) {
338: 			slog.Debug("extracted fuller response from transcript", "conversationId", opts.ConversationID)
339: 			response.Output = extracted
340: 		}
341: 	}
342: 
343: 	return response, nil
344: }

346: type agyJSONStream struct {
347: 	raw            bytes.Buffer
348: 	streamed       strings.Builder
349: 	result         string
350: 	resultStatus   string
351: 	resultError    string
352: 	conversationID string
353: 	mu             sync.Mutex
354: 	quotaDetail    string
355: }
356: 
357: func (s *agyJSONStream) noteProviderLimit(detail string) {
358: 	detail = strings.TrimSpace(detail)
359: 	if detail == "" {
360: 		return
361: 	}
362: 	s.mu.Lock()
363: 	defer s.mu.Unlock()
364: 	if s.quotaDetail == "" {
365: 		s.quotaDetail = detail
366: 		return
367: 	}
368: 	preferred, _ := extractProviderLimitDetail(s.quotaDetail + "\n" + detail)
369: 	if preferred != "" {
370: 		s.quotaDetail = preferred
371: 	}
372: }
373: 
374: func (s *agyJSONStream) providerLimitDetail() string {
375: 	s.mu.Lock()
376: 	defer s.mu.Unlock()
377: 	return s.quotaDetail
378: }
379: 
380: func (s *agyJSONStream) observeLimitText(text string, onLimit func(string)) {
381: 	detail, ok := extractProviderLimitDetail(text)
382: 	if !ok {
383: 		return
384: 	}
385: 	s.noteProviderLimit(detail)
386: 	if onLimit != nil {
387: 		onLimit(detail)
388: 	}
389: }
390: 
391: func (s *agyJSONStream) read(r io.Reader, onEvent func(StreamEvent), onLimit func(string)) {
392: 	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(r)
393: 	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, 64*1024), 16*1024*1024)
394: 	for scanner.Scan() {
395: 		line := append([]byte(nil), scanner.Bytes()...)
396: 		s.raw.Write(line)
397: 		s.raw.WriteByte('\n')
398: 
399: 		var event struct {
400: 			Event          string `json:"event"`
401: 			ConversationID string `json:"conversation_id"`
402: 			StepUpdate     struct {
403: 				ConversationID string `json:"conversation_id"`
404: 				StepIndex      int    `json:"step_index"`
405: 				State          string `json:"state"`
406: 				StepType       string `json:"step_type"`
407: 				TextDelta      string `json:"text_delta"`
408: 				ToolName       string `json:"tool_name"`
409: 				ToolInfo       struct {
410: 					Name       string `json:"name"`
411: 					Parameters any    `json:"parameters"`
412: 					Output     any    `json:"output"`
413: 				} `json:"tool_info"`
414: 			} `json:"step_update"`
415: 			Result struct {
416: 				ConversationID string `json:"conversation_id"`
417: 				Status         string `json:"status"`
418: 				Response       string `json:"response"`
419: 				Error          string `json:"error"`
420: 			} `json:"result"`
421: 		}
422: 		if json.Unmarshal(line, &event) != nil {
423: 			s.observeLimitText(string(line), onLimit)
424: 			continue
425: 		}
426: 		switch event.Event {
427: 		case "init":
428: 			s.conversationID = event.ConversationID
429: 		case "step_update":
430: 			if event.StepUpdate.ConversationID != "" {
431: 				s.conversationID = event.StepUpdate.ConversationID
432: 			}
433: 			if event.StepUpdate.StepType == "agent_response" && event.StepUpdate.TextDelta != "" {
434: 				chunk := normalizeLineEndings(event.StepUpdate.TextDelta)
435: 				s.streamed.WriteString(chunk)
436: 				if onEvent != nil {
437: 					onEvent(StreamEvent{Kind: StreamEventText, Text: chunk})
438: 				}
439: 			}
440: 			if event.StepUpdate.StepType == "tool" && onEvent != nil {
441: 				toolName := event.StepUpdate.ToolName
442: 				if toolName == "" {
443: 					toolName = event.StepUpdate.ToolInfo.Name
444: 				}
445: 				streamEvent := StreamEvent{
446: 					ToolID:    fmt.Sprintf("agy-step-%d", event.StepUpdate.StepIndex),
447: 					ToolName:  toolName,
448: 					ToolState: event.StepUpdate.State,
449: 					RawInput:  event.StepUpdate.ToolInfo.Parameters,
450: 					RawOutput: event.StepUpdate.ToolInfo.Output,
451: 				}
452: 				if event.StepUpdate.State == "ACTIVE" {
453: 					streamEvent.Kind = StreamEventToolStart
454: 				} else {
455: 					streamEvent.Kind = StreamEventToolUpdate
456: 				}
457: 				onEvent(streamEvent)
458: 			}
459: 		case "result":
460: 			if event.Result.ConversationID != "" {
461: 				s.conversationID = event.Result.ConversationID
462: 			}
463: 			s.resultStatus = event.Result.Status
464: 			s.resultError = normalizeLineEndings(event.Result.Error)
465: 			s.result = normalizeLineEndings(event.Result.Response)
466: 		}
467: 		s.observeLimitText(string(line), onLimit)
468: 		if event.Result.Error != "" {
469: 			s.observeLimitText(event.Result.Error, onLimit)
470: 		}
471: 	}
472: }

474: func (s *agyJSONStream) output() string {
475: 	if s.result != "" {
476: 		return s.result
477: 	}
478: 	if s.streamed.Len() > 0 {
479: 		return s.streamed.String()
480: 	}
481: 	return normalizeLineEndings(s.raw.String())
482: }

484: func (s *agyJSONStream) terminalError(exitCode int) error {
485: 	status := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(s.resultStatus))
486: 	if status == "" || status == "SUCCESS" {
487: 		return nil
488: 	}
489: 	detail := strings.TrimSpace(s.resultError)
490: 	if detail == "" {
491: 		detail = strings.TrimSpace(s.output())
492: 	}
493: 	if status == "WAITING" || status == "RUNNING" {
494: 		if detail == "" {
495: 			detail = fmt.Sprintf("agy ended with status %s", status)
496: 		} else {
497: 			detail = fmt.Sprintf("agy ended with status %s: %s", status, detail)
498: 		}
499: 		return &TimeoutError{Detail: detail}
500: 	}
501: 	if detail == "" {
502: 		detail = fmt.Sprintf("agy print run ended with status %s", status)
503: 	} else {
504: 		detail = fmt.Sprintf("agy print run ended with status %s: %s", status, detail)
505: 	}
506: 	return &ProcessError{ExitCode: exitCode, Detail: detail}
507: }
508: 
509: func streamPipeLines(r io.Reader, dst *bytes.Buffer, onChunk func(string)) {
510: 	buf := make([]byte, 4096)
511: 	var pendingCR bool
512: 	for {
513: 		n, err := r.Read(buf)
514: 		if n > 0 {
515: 			chunk := buf[:n]
516: 			var sb strings.Builder
517: 			sb.Grow(len(chunk))
518: 			for _, b := range chunk {
519: 				if pendingCR {
520: 					if b == '\n' {
521: 						sb.WriteByte('\n')
522: 						pendingCR = false
523: 						continue
524: 					}
525: 					sb.WriteByte('\r')
526: 					pendingCR = false
527: 				}
528: 				if b == '\r' {
529: 					pendingCR = true
530: 				} else {
531: 					sb.WriteByte(b)
532: 				}
533: 			}
534: 			text := sb.String()
535: 			if text != "" {
536: 				_, _ = dst.WriteString(text)
537: 				if onChunk != nil {
538: 					onChunk(text)
539: 				}
540: 			}
541: 		}
542: 		if err != nil {
543: 			if pendingCR {
544: 				_, _ = dst.WriteString("\r")
545: 				if onChunk != nil {
546: 					onChunk("\r")
547: 				}
548: 			}
549: 			return
550: 		}
551: 	}
552: }

554: type transcriptTailer struct {
555: 	configDir      string
556: 	conversationID string
557: 	startedAt      time.Time
558: 	onChunk        func(string)
559: 	states         map[string]transcriptFileState
560: 	lastContent    string
561: 	mu             sync.Mutex
562: }
563: 
564: type transcriptFileState struct {
565: 	offset  int64
566: 	modTime time.Time
567: }
568: 
569: func newTranscriptTailer(configDir, conversationID string, startedAt time.Time, onChunk func(string)) *transcriptTailer {
570: 	return &transcriptTailer{
571: 		configDir:      configDir,
572: 		conversationID: conversationID,
573: 		startedAt:      startedAt,
574: 		onChunk:        onChunk,
575: 		states:         make(map[string]transcriptFileState),
576: 	}
577: }
