579: func (t *transcriptTailer) snapshotExisting() {
580: 	for _, path := range t.candidateTranscriptPaths() {
581: 		info, err := os.Stat(path)
582: 		if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
583: 			continue
584: 		}
585: 		t.states[path] = transcriptFileState{offset: info.Size(), modTime: info.ModTime()}
586: 	}
587: }
588: 
589: func (t *transcriptTailer) run(stop <-chan struct{}) {
590: 	ticker := time.NewTicker(transcriptPollInterval)
591: 	defer ticker.Stop()
592: 	for {
593: 		select {
594: 		case <-stop:
595: 			return
596: 		case <-ticker.C:
597: 			t.scan(false)
598: 		}
599: 	}
600: }
601: 
602: func (t *transcriptTailer) scan(isFinal bool) {
603: 	for _, path := range t.candidateTranscriptPaths() {
604: 		t.scanFile(path, isFinal)
605: 	}
606: }
607: 
608: func (t *transcriptTailer) output() string {
609: 	t.mu.Lock()
610: 	defer t.mu.Unlock()
611: 	return t.lastContent
612: }
613: 
614: func (t *transcriptTailer) candidateTranscriptPaths() []string {
615: 	if t.conversationID != "" {
616: 		return []string{transcriptPath(t.configDir, t.conversationID)}
617: 	}
618: 
619: 	brainDir := filepath.Join(t.configDir, "brain")
620: 	var paths []string
621: 	_ = filepath.WalkDir(brainDir, func(path string, d os.DirEntry, err error) error {
622: 		if err != nil || d.IsDir() || filepath.Base(path) != "transcript.jsonl" {
623: 			return nil
624: 		}
625: 		paths = append(paths, path)
626: 		return nil
627: 	})
628: 	return paths
629: }
630: 
631: func (t *transcriptTailer) scanFile(path string, isFinal bool) {
632: 	info, err := os.Stat(path)
633: 	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
634: 		return
635: 	}
636: 
637: 	state, known := t.states[path]
638: 	if !known {
639: 		if info.ModTime().Before(t.startedAt.Add(-time.Second)) {
640: 			return
641: 		}
642: 		state = transcriptFileState{}
643: 	}
644: 	if known && info.Size() == state.offset && info.ModTime().Equal(state.modTime) && !isFinal {
645: 		return
646: 	}
647: 	if info.Size() < state.offset {
648: 		state.offset = 0
649: 	}
650: 
651: 	data, nextOffset, err := readJSONLFrom(path, state.offset, isFinal)
652: 	if err != nil {
653: 		slog.Debug("tail transcript read failed", "path", path, "error", err)
654: 		return
655: 	}
656: 	if len(data) > 0 {
657: 		t.consume(data)
658: 	}
659: 	t.states[path] = transcriptFileState{offset: nextOffset, modTime: info.ModTime()}
660: }
661: 
662: func (t *transcriptTailer) consume(data []byte) {
663: 	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(bytes.NewReader(data))
664: 	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, 64*1024), 16*1024*1024)
665: 	for scanner.Scan() {
666: 		var entry struct {
667: 			Type    string `json:"type"`
668: 			Content string `json:"content"`
669: 		}
670: 		if json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &entry) != nil || entry.Type != "PLANNER_RESPONSE" || entry.Content == "" {
671: 			continue
672: 		}
673: 		t.emit(entry.Content)
674: 	}
675: }
676: 
677: func (t *transcriptTailer) emit(content string) {
678: 	content = normalizeLineEndings(content)
679: 	t.mu.Lock()
680: 	previous := t.lastContent
681: 	if content == previous {
682: 		t.mu.Unlock()
683: 		return
684: 	}
685: 	t.lastContent = content
686: 	t.mu.Unlock()
687: 
688: 	chunk := content
689: 	if strings.HasPrefix(content, previous) {
690: 		chunk = content[len(previous):]
691: 	}
692: 	if chunk != "" && t.onChunk != nil {
693: 		t.onChunk(chunk)
694: 	}
695: }
696: 
697: func readJSONLFrom(path string, offset int64, isFinal bool) ([]byte, int64, error) {
698: 	f, err := os.Open(path)
699: 	if err != nil {
700: 		return nil, offset, err
701: 	}
702: 	defer f.Close()
703: 	if offset > 0 {
704: 		if _, err := f.Seek(offset, io.SeekStart); err != nil {
705: 			return nil, offset, err
706: 		}
707: 	}
708: 	data, err := io.ReadAll(f)
709: 	if err != nil {
710: 		return nil, offset, err
711: 	}
712: 	if len(data) == 0 {
713: 		return nil, offset, nil
714: 	}
715: 	lastNewline := bytes.LastIndexByte(data, '\n')
716: 	if lastNewline < 0 {
717: 		if isFinal {
718: 			return data, offset + int64(len(data)), nil
719: 		}
720: 		return nil, offset, nil
721: 	}
722: 	if isFinal && lastNewline < len(data)-1 {
723: 		return data, offset + int64(len(data)), nil
724: 	}
725: 	complete := data[:lastNewline+1]
726: 	return complete, offset + int64(len(complete)), nil
727: }
728: 
729: func transcriptPath(configDir, conversationID string) string {
730: 	return filepath.Join(configDir, "brain", conversationID, ".system_generated", "logs", "transcript.jsonl")
731: }

733: func (r *NonInteractiveRunner) commandEnv() []string {

740: 		return env
741: 	}

744: 	}
745: 	if os.Getenv("USERPROFILE") == "" {
746: 		env = append(env, "USERPROFILE="+home)
747: 	}
748: 	return env
749: }

777: func (r *NonInteractiveRunner) extractFromTranscript(conversationID string) string {
778: 	if r.configDir == "" {
779: 		return ""
780: 	}
781: 	f, err := os.Open(transcriptPath(r.configDir, conversationID))
782: 	if err != nil {
783: 		return ""
784: 	}
785: 	defer f.Close()
786: 
787: 	var lastResponse string
788: 	reader := bufio.NewReader(f)
789: 	for {
790: 		line, err := readJSONLLine(reader)
791: 		if err == io.EOF {
792: 			break
793: 		}
794: 		if err != nil {
795: 			slog.Warn("failed to read transcript", "conversationId", conversationID, "error", err)
796: 			return ""
797: 		}
798: 
799: 		var entry struct {
800: 			Type    string `json:"type"`
801: 			Content string `json:"content"`
802: 		}
803: 		if json.Unmarshal(line, &entry) == nil {
804: 			if entry.Type == "PLANNER_RESPONSE" && entry.Content != "" {
805: 				lastResponse = entry.Content
806: 			}
807: 		}
808: 	}
809: 	return lastResponse
810: }
811: 
812: func readJSONLLine(r io.ByteReader) ([]byte, error) {
813: 	var line []byte
814: 	for {
815: 		b, err := r.ReadByte()
816: 		if err != nil {
817: 			if err == io.EOF && len(line) > 0 {
818: 				return line, nil
819: 			}
820: 			return nil, err
821: 		}
822: 		if b == '\n' {
823: 			return line, nil
824: 		}
825: 		if b == '\r' {
826: 			continue
827: 		}
828: 		line = append(line, b)
829: 	}
830: }
831: 
832: func normalizeLineEndings(s string) string {
833: 	return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "\r\n", "\n")
834: }

836: func printTimeoutDuration(timeout time.Duration) time.Duration {
837: 	if timeout > 0 {
838: 		return timeout
839: 	}
840: 	return unboundedPrintTimeout
841: }

847: func processKillTimeout(timeout time.Duration) time.Duration {
848: 	if timeout <= 0 {
849: 		return 0
850: 	}
851: 	if timeout <= printTimeoutKillGrace {
852: 		return timeout
853: 	}
854: 	return timeout + printTimeoutKillGrace
855: }

45: func init() {
46: 	if os.Getenv(processHelperEnv) != "1" || os.Getenv(processSpecEnv) == "" || os.Getenv(processGateEnv) == "" {
47: 		return
48: 	}
49: 	os.Exit(runProcessHelper())
50: }
51: 
52: func hideWindow(cmd *exec.Cmd) {
53: 	if cmd.SysProcAttr == nil {
54: 		cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{}
55: 	}
56: 	cmd.SysProcAttr.HideWindow = true
57: 	cmd.SysProcAttr.CreationFlags |= windows.CREATE_NO_WINDOW
58: }
59: 
60: func runProcessHelper() int {
61: 	var spec helperProcessSpec
62: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(os.Getenv(processSpecEnv)), &spec); err != nil {
63: 		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "go-agy process helper: invalid spec: %v\n", err)
64: 		return 125
65: 	}
66: 	gate := os.Getenv(processGateEnv)
67: 	deadline := time.Now().Add(processGateExpiry)
68: 	for {
69: 		if _, err := os.Stat(gate); err == nil {
70: 			break
71: 		}
72: 		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
73: 			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "go-agy process helper: gate wait timed out")
74: 			return 124
75: 		}
76: 		time.Sleep(processGateWait)
77: 	}
78: 
79: 	cmd := exec.Command(spec.Path, spec.Args...)
80: 	cmd.Env = spec.Env
81: 	cmd.Dir = spec.Dir
82: 	cmd.Stdin = os.Stdin
83: 	cmd.Stdout = os.Stdout
84: 	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
85: 	hideWindow(cmd)
86: 	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
87: 		if exitErr, ok := err.(*exec.ExitError); ok {
88: 			return exitErr.ExitCode()
89: 		}
90: 		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "go-agy process helper: execute command: %v\n", err)
91: 		return 126
92: 	}
93: 	return 0
94: }
95: 
96: func configureProcessTree(cmd *exec.Cmd) (*processTreeController, error) {
97: 	cmd.WaitDelay = 5 * time.Second
98: 	hideWindow(cmd)
99: 	job, err := windows.CreateJobObject(nil, nil)
100: 	if err != nil {
101: 		return nil, err
102: 	}
103: 	info := windows.JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION{}
104: 	info.BasicLimitInformation.LimitFlags = windows.JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE
105: 	_, err = windows.SetInformationJobObject(
106: 		job,
107: 		windows.JobObjectExtendedLimitInformation,
108: 		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&info)),
109: 		uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(info)),
110: 	)
111: 	if err != nil {
112: 		_ = windows.CloseHandle(job)
113: 		return nil, err
114: 	}
115: 
116: 	spec, err := json.Marshal(helperProcessSpec{Path: cmd.Path, Args: cmd.Args[1:], Env: cmd.Env, Dir: cmd.Dir})
117: 	if err != nil {
118: 		_ = windows.CloseHandle(job)
119: 		return nil, err
120: 	}
121: 	var gateID [16]byte
122: 	if _, err := io.ReadFull(rand.Reader, gateID[:]); err != nil {
123: 		_ = windows.CloseHandle(job)
124: 		return nil, err
125: 	}
126: 	gatePath := filepath.Join(os.TempDir(), "go-agy-process-gate-"+hex.EncodeToString(gateID[:]))
127: 	helper, err := os.Executable()
128: 	if err != nil {
129: 		_ = windows.CloseHandle(job)
130: 		return nil, err
131: 	}
132: 	cmd.Path = helper
133: 	cmd.Args = []string{helper}
134: 	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), processHelperEnv+"=1", processSpecEnv+"="+string(spec), processGateEnv+"="+gatePath)
135: 
136: 	controller := &processTreeController{job: job, gatePath: gatePath}
137: 	cmd.Cancel = controller.Close
138: 	return controller, nil
139: }
140: 
141: func (c *processTreeController) AfterStart(cmd *exec.Cmd) error {
142: 	process, err := windows.OpenProcess(windows.PROCESS_SET_QUOTA|windows.PROCESS_TERMINATE, false, uint32(cmd.Process.Pid))
143: 	if err != nil {
144: 		return err
145: 	}
146: 	defer windows.CloseHandle(process)
147: 	if err := windows.AssignProcessToJobObject(c.job, process); err != nil {
148: 		return err
149: 	}
150: 	return os.WriteFile(c.gatePath, nil, 0600)
151: }
152: 
153: func (c *processTreeController) Close() error {
154: 	c.closeOnce.Do(func() {

156: 		c.closeErr = windows.CloseHandle(c.job)
157: 		if errors.Is(c.closeErr, windows.ERROR_INVALID_HANDLE) {
158: 			c.closeErr = nil
159: 		}
160: 	})
161: 	return c.closeErr
162: }

46:     async def resolve_session_id(self, context: RequestContext) -> str:
47:         """Resolve or create a canonical internal A-leg session id."""
48:         if context.b2bua_identity is not None:
49:             context.session_id = context.b2bua_identity.a_session_id
50:             return context.b2bua_identity.a_session_id
51: 
52:         client_session_id = self._client_session_extractor.extract_client_session_id(
53:             context
54:         )
55:         auth_scope_id = await self._auth_scope_resolver.resolve_auth_scope_id(context)
56: 
57:         if (
58:             client_session_id is not None
59:             and self._session_id_factory.is_canonical_a_session_id(client_session_id)
60:         ):
61:             echo_continuity = await self._mapping_store.try_resolve_echoed_a_session_id(
62:                 a_session_id=client_session_id.strip(),
63:                 requesting_auth_scope_id=auth_scope_id,
64:             )
65:             if echo_continuity is not None:
66:                 a_session_id = echo_continuity.a_session_id
67:                 context.ensure_processing_context().update(
68:                     {
69:                         "b2bua_continuity_reused_existing": echo_continuity.reused_existing,
70:                         "b2bua_continuity_store_error": echo_continuity.had_store_error,
71:                     }
72:                 )
73:                 context.session_id = a_session_id
74:                 context.b2bua_identity = B2buaIdentity(
75:                     a_session_id=a_session_id,
76:                     client_session_id=client_session_id,
77:                     auth_scope_id=auth_scope_id,
78:                 )
79:                 return a_session_id
80: 
81:         if client_session_id is not None and auth_scope_id is not None:
82:             continuity = await self._mapping_store.resolve_or_create_a_session_id(
83:                 auth_scope_id=auth_scope_id,
84:                 client_session_id=client_session_id,
85:                 create_a_session_id=self._session_id_factory.generate_a_session_id,
86:             )
87:             a_session_id = continuity.a_session_id
88:             context.ensure_processing_context().update(
89:                 {
90:                     "b2bua_continuity_reused_existing": continuity.reused_existing,
91:                     "b2bua_continuity_store_error": continuity.had_store_error,
92:                 }
93:             )
94:         else:
95:             # Strict isolation default: no client session id or no auth scope -> new A-leg.
96:             # If heuristic session inference is enabled, try to derive a stable client session id.
97:             heuristic_client_id: str | None = None
98:             if client_session_id is None and self._config is not None:
99:                 try:
100:                     if (
101:                         self._config.session.b2bua.enable_unsafe_heuristic_session_inference
102:                     ):
103:                         agent = context.agent
104:                         client_host = context.client_host
105:                         agent_text = str(agent).strip() if agent is not None else ""
106:                         host_text = (
107:                             str(client_host).strip() if client_host is not None else ""
108:                         )
109: 
110:                         first_user_msg = ""
111:                         if context.domain_request and hasattr(
112:                             context.domain_request, "messages"
113:                         ):
114:                             for msg in context.domain_request.messages:
115:                                 if msg.role == "user":
116:                                     content = msg.content
117:                                     if isinstance(content, str):
118:                                         first_user_msg = content
119:                                     elif isinstance(content, list):
120:                                         parts = []
121:                                         for part in content:
122:                                             if (
123:                                                 isinstance(part, dict)
124:                                                 and part.get("type") == "text"
125:                                             ):
126:                                                 parts.append(part.get("text", ""))
127:                                         first_user_msg = "".join(parts)
128:                                     break
129: 
130:                         if first_user_msg:
131:                             digest = hashlib.sha256(
132:                                 first_user_msg.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
133:                             ).hexdigest()[:16]
134:                             heuristic_client_id = f"b2bua-fallback:{digest}"
135:                         elif agent_text or host_text:
136:                             digest = hashlib.sha256(
137:                                 f"{agent_text}|{host_text}".encode(
138:                                     "utf-8", errors="ignore"
139:                                 )
140:                             ).hexdigest()[:16]
141:                             heuristic_client_id = f"b2bua-fallback:{digest}"
142:                 except Exception:
143:                     if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
144:                         logger.debug(
145:                             "Heuristic session inference failed", exc_info=True
146:                         )
147: 
148:             if heuristic_client_id is not None and auth_scope_id is not None:
149:                 continuity = await self._mapping_store.resolve_or_create_a_session_id(
150:                     auth_scope_id=auth_scope_id,
151:                     client_session_id=heuristic_client_id,
152:                     create_a_session_id=self._session_id_factory.generate_a_session_id,
153:                 )
154:                 a_session_id = continuity.a_session_id
155:                 context.ensure_processing_context().update(
156:                     {
157:                         "b2bua_continuity_reused_existing": continuity.reused_existing,
158:                         "b2bua_continuity_store_error": continuity.had_store_error,
159:                     }
160:                 )
161:                 # Update client_session_id so it's recorded in identity
162:                 client_session_id = heuristic_client_id
163:             else:
164:                 generated_a_session_id = (
165:                     self._session_id_factory.generate_a_session_id()
166:                 )
167:                 continuity = await self._mapping_store.resolve_or_create_a_session_id(
168:                     auth_scope_id=f"{_ANONYMOUS_AUTH_SCOPE_PREFIX}:{generated_a_session_id}",
169:                     client_session_id=_ANONYMOUS_CLIENT_SESSION_ID,
170:                     create_a_session_id=lambda: generated_a_session_id,
171:                 )
172:                 a_session_id = continuity.a_session_id
173:                 context.ensure_processing_context().update(
174:                     {
175:                         "b2bua_continuity_reused_existing": False,
176:                         "b2bua_continuity_store_error": continuity.had_store_error,
177:                     }
178:                 )
179: 
180:         context.session_id = a_session_id
181:         context.b2bua_identity = B2buaIdentity(
182:             a_session_id=a_session_id,
183:             client_session_id=client_session_id,
184:             auth_scope_id=auth_scope_id,
185:         )
186:         return a_session_id

1: """Client session identifier extraction with strict trust-boundary rules."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import logging
6: from collections.abc import Mapping
7: from typing import Any, Final
8: 
9: from src.core.domain.request_context import RequestContext
10: from src.core.interfaces.client_session_id_extractor_interface import (
11:     IClientSessionIdExtractor,
12: )
13: from src.core.interfaces.configuration_interface import IConfig
14: 
15: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
16: 
17: _DEFAULT_ECHO_HEADER: Final[str] = "x-b2bua-session-id"
18: 
19: 
20: class DefaultClientSessionIdExtractor(IClientSessionIdExtractor):
21:     """Extract client session id as untrusted metadata only."""
22: 
23:     def __init__(self, config: IConfig | None = None) -> None:
24:         self._config = config
25:         self._configured_echo_header_name = self._resolve_configured_echo_header_name()
26: 
27:     def extract_client_session_id(self, context: RequestContext) -> str | None:
28:         """Extract client session id using precedence rules and conflict diagnostics."""
29:         header_candidate = self._extract_header_candidate(context)
30:         body_candidate, extra_body_candidate = self._extract_body_candidates(context)
31: 
32:         candidates = {
33:             "header": header_candidate,
34:             "body": body_candidate,
35:             "extra_body": extra_body_candidate,
36:         }
37: 
38:         self._record_conflict_diagnostic_if_needed(context, candidates)
39: 
40:         extracted = header_candidate or body_candidate or extra_body_candidate or None
41:         if extracted is not None:
42:             context.ensure_processing_context().update(
43:                 {"b2bua_client_session_id": extracted}
44:             )
45: 
46:         return extracted
47: 
48:     def _resolve_configured_echo_header_name(self) -> str:
49:         if self._config is None:
50:             return _DEFAULT_ECHO_HEADER
51: 
52:         try:
53:             session_cfg = getattr(self._config, "session", None)
54:             b2bua_cfg = getattr(session_cfg, "b2bua", None)
55:             configured = getattr(b2bua_cfg, "echo_header_name", None)
56:             if isinstance(configured, str):
57:                 normalized = configured.strip().lower()
58:                 if normalized:
59:                     return normalized
60:         except (AttributeError, TypeError):
61:             return _DEFAULT_ECHO_HEADER
62: 
63:         return _DEFAULT_ECHO_HEADER
64: 
65:     def _extract_header_candidate(self, context: RequestContext) -> str | None:
66:         # If operators configure echo header name to x-session-id, inbound x-session-id
67:         # must still be ignored for identity to prevent trust-boundary confusion.
68:         if self._configured_echo_header_name == "x-session-id":
69:             return None
70: 
71:         from_x_session = self._normalize_candidate(context.get_header("x-session-id"))
72:         if from_x_session is not None:
73:             return from_x_session
74: 
75:         return self._normalize_candidate(
76:             context.get_header(self._configured_echo_header_name)
77:         )
78: 
79:     @staticmethod
80:     def _extract_body_candidates(
81:         context: RequestContext,
82:     ) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
83:         body_candidate: str | None = None
84:         extra_body_candidate: str | None = None
85: 
86:         domain_request = context.domain_request
87:         if domain_request is None:
88:             return body_candidate, extra_body_candidate
89: 
90:         body_candidate = DefaultClientSessionIdExtractor._normalize_candidate(
91:             getattr(domain_request, "session_id", None)
92:         )
93: 
94:         extra_body = getattr(domain_request, "extra_body", None)
95:         if isinstance(extra_body, Mapping):
96:             extra_body_candidate = DefaultClientSessionIdExtractor._normalize_candidate(
97:                 extra_body.get("session_id")
98:             )
99: 
100:         return body_candidate, extra_body_candidate
101: 
102:     @staticmethod
103:     def _normalize_candidate(value: Any) -> str | None:
104:         if not isinstance(value, str):
105:             return None
106: 
107:         normalized = value.strip()
108:         if not normalized:
109:             return None
110: 
111:         return normalized
112: 
113:     @staticmethod
114:     def _record_conflict_diagnostic_if_needed(
115:         context: RequestContext,
116:         candidates: Mapping[str, str | None],
117:     ) -> None:
118:         present_values = [value for value in candidates.values() if value is not None]
119:         if len(set(present_values)) <= 1:
120:             return
121: 
122:         context.ensure_processing_context().update(
123:             {"b2bua_client_session_id_conflict": True}
124:         )
125: 
126:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.WARNING):
127:             conflicting_sources = [
128:                 source for source, value in candidates.items() if value is not None
129:             ]
130:             logger.warning(
131:                 "Conflicting client session identifiers detected from sources=%s; "
132:                 "using precedence header > body > extra_body",
133:                 conflicting_sources,
134:             )

1: """B2BUA session identifier generation helpers."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import re
6: from collections.abc import Callable
7: from typing import Final
8: from uuid import UUID, uuid4
9: 
10: _A_SESSION_PREFIX: Final[str] = "llm-b2bua-"
11: _B_SESSION_PREFIX: Final[str] = "llm-b2bua-b-"
12: _A_SESSION_PATTERN: Final[re.Pattern[str]] = re.compile(
13:     r"^llm-b2bua-(?P<a_uuid>[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-"
14:     r"[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12})$"
15: )
16: _HEADER_SAFE_IDENTIFIER_PATTERN: Final[re.Pattern[str]] = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$")
17: 
18: 
19: class B2BUASessionIdFactory:
20:     """Generate A-leg and B-leg session identifiers for B2BUA mode."""
21: 
22:     def __init__(self, uuid_factory: Callable[[], UUID] | None = None) -> None:
23:         self._uuid_factory = uuid_factory if uuid_factory is not None else uuid4
24: 
25:     def generate_a_session_id(self) -> str:
26:         """Create a new A-leg identifier in canonical format."""
27:         return f"{_A_SESSION_PREFIX}{self._uuid_factory()}"
28: 
29:     def generate_b_session_id(self, a_session_id: str, seq: int) -> str:
30:         """Create a B-leg identifier bound to A-leg UUID and sequence number."""
31:         if seq < 1:
32:             raise ValueError("seq must be >= 1")
33: 
34:         canonical_a_uuid = self.extract_a_uuid(a_session_id)
35:         return f"{_B_SESSION_PREFIX}{canonical_a_uuid}-{seq}"
36: 
37:     @staticmethod
38:     def extract_a_uuid(a_session_id: str) -> str:
39:         """Extract and canonicalize the UUID component from an A-leg identifier."""
40:         if not isinstance(a_session_id, str):
41:             raise ValueError("Invalid a_session_id format")
42: 
43:         candidate = a_session_id.strip()
44:         match = _A_SESSION_PATTERN.fullmatch(candidate)
45:         if match is None:
46:             raise ValueError("Invalid a_session_id format")
47: 
48:         try:
49:             return str(UUID(match.group("a_uuid")))
50:         except ValueError as exc:
51:             raise ValueError("Invalid a_session_id format") from exc
52: 
53:     @staticmethod
54:     def is_header_safe(identifier: str) -> bool:
55:         """Return True when identifier is safe to place in HTTP headers."""
56:         return _HEADER_SAFE_IDENTIFIER_PATTERN.fullmatch(identifier) is not None
57: 
58:     @staticmethod
59:     def is_canonical_a_session_id(value: str) -> bool:
60:         """Return True when ``value`` matches the canonical ``llm-b2bua-<uuid>`` shape."""
61:         if not isinstance(value, str):
62:             return False
63:         return _A_SESSION_PATTERN.fullmatch(value.strip()) is not None

395:     def _resolve_client_session_id(
396:         self, request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest
397:     ) -> str:
398:         """Resolve the logical client session used to key ACP subprocess pools.
399: 
400:         When neither ``ConnectorRequestContext.session_id`` nor
401:         ``request.request.session_id`` is set, callers share the pool key
402:         ``"default"`` (one ACP runtime per ``(project_dir, model)`` for all
403:         such traffic). Upstream layers should set a stable session id when
404:         isolation between clients or tabs is required.
405:         """
406: 
407:         sid: str | None = None
408:         if request.context is not None and request.context.session_id:
409:             sid = request.context.session_id
410:         if not sid:
411:             raw = getattr(request.request, "session_id", None)
412:             if isinstance(raw, str) and raw.strip():
413:                 sid = raw
414:         if isinstance(sid, str) and sid.strip():
415:             return sid.strip()
416:         return "default"
417:

430:         )
431:         client_session_id = self._resolve_client_session_id(request)
432:         runtime_key = self._build_runtime_key(
433:             project_dir,
434:             requested_model,
435:             client_session_id,
436:             responses_text_only=self._is_responses_text_only_request(request),
437:         )
438:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
439:             logger.debug(

441:                 runtime_key,
442:                 project_dir,
443:                 requested_model,
444:                 client_session_id,
445:             )
446:

448:             runtime = self._runtimes.get(runtime_key)
449:             if runtime is None:

451:                     project_dir, requested_model, client_session_id
452:                 )
453:                 self._runtimes[runtime_key] = runtime
454: 
455:         return await self._reap_idle_runtime(runtime_key, runtime)

40:     def _resolve_auxiliary_effective_session_id(
41:         context: RequestContext | None,
42:     ) -> str | None:
43:         if context is None:
44:             return None
45:         extensions = getattr(context, "extensions", None)
46:         if not isinstance(extensions, dict):
47:             return None
48:         if not bool(extensions.get("auxiliary_request")):
49:             return None
50:         auxiliary_session_id = extensions.get("auxiliary_effective_session_id")
51:         if not isinstance(auxiliary_session_id, str):
52:             return None
53:         normalized = auxiliary_session_id.strip()
54:         return normalized or None
55: 
56:     async def resolve_session(
57:         self, context: RequestContext | None, request: CanonicalChatRequest
58:     ) -> tuple[ISession | None, str | None]:
59:         """Resolve session from context or request."""
60:         session: ISession | None = None
61:         session_id_for_backend: str | None = None
62:         b2bua_mode = False
63:         if context is not None:
64:             b2bua_mode = isinstance(
65:                 getattr(context, "b2bua_identity", None), B2buaIdentity
66:             )
67: 
68:         # Resolve session from context when available
69:         if context:
70:             auxiliary_session_id = self._resolve_auxiliary_effective_session_id(context)
71:             if auxiliary_session_id:
72:                 session_id_for_backend = auxiliary_session_id
73:             else:
74:                 b2bua_a_leg = self._resolve_b2bua_a_leg_session_id(context)
75:                 if b2bua_a_leg:
76:                     session_id_for_backend = b2bua_a_leg
77:                 elif getattr(context, "session_id", None):
78:                     session_id_for_backend = context.session_id
79: 
80:         if session_id_for_backend:
81:             try:
82:                 session = await self._session_service.get_session(
83:                     session_id_for_backend
84:                 )
85:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
86:                 # Propagate cancellation - session resolution should not block cancellation
87:                 raise
88:             except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError, KeyError) as e:
89:                 # Catch specific exceptions from repository/service layer
90:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
91:                     logger.debug(
92:                         "Failed to load session '%s' for backend call: %s",
93:                         session_id_for_backend,
94:                         e,
95:                         exc_info=True,
96:                     )
97:                 session = None
98:             except Exception as e:
99:                 # Fallback for unexpected errors - log and continue (fail-open)
100:                 logger.warning(
101:                     "Unexpected error loading session '%s' for backend call: %s",
102:                     session_id_for_backend,
103:                     e,
104:                     exc_info=True,
105:                 )
106:                 session = None
107: 
108:         # In B2BUA mode, request-provided session IDs are never used for session state.
109:         if b2bua_mode:
110:             return session, session_id_for_backend
111: 
112:         # Legacy mode: try to get session from request extra_body if not found in context
113:         request_session_id = (
114:             request.extra_body.get("session_id") if request.extra_body else None
115:         )
116:         if (
117:             session is None
118:             and isinstance(request_session_id, str)
119:             and request_session_id
120:         ):
121:             if session_id_for_backend is None:
122:                 session_id_for_backend = request_session_id
123:             try:
124:                 session = await self._session_service.get_session(request_session_id)
125:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
126:                 # Propagate cancellation - session resolution should not block cancellation
127:                 raise
128:             except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError, KeyError) as e:
129:                 # Catch specific exceptions from repository/service layer
130:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
131:                     logger.debug(
132:                         "Could not load session %s for backend from backend-only service: %s",
133:                         request_session_id,
134:                         e,
135:                         exc_info=True,
136:                     )
137:                 session = None
138:             except Exception as e:
139:                 # Fallback for unexpected errors - log and continue (fail-open)
140:                 logger.warning(
141:                     "Unexpected error loading session %s for backend from backend-only service: %s",
142:                     request_session_id,
143:                     e,
144:                     exc_info=True,
145:                 )
146:                 session = None
147: 
148:         return session, session_id_for_backend

33: def _echo_continuity_auth_allows(
34:     *,
35:     stored_auth_scope_id: str,
36:     requesting_auth_scope_id: str | None,
37: ) -> bool:
38:     """Whether an echoed A-leg id may be reused under the current auth scope."""
39:     if requesting_auth_scope_id == stored_auth_scope_id:
40:         return True
41:     if stored_auth_scope_id.startswith(_ANON_AUTH_SCOPE_PREFIX):
42:         if requesting_auth_scope_id is None:
43:             return True
44:         if requesting_auth_scope_id == _LOCALHOST_AUTH_SCOPE:
45:             return True
46:     return False

49: def _read_config_ttl_seconds(config: IConfig | None) -> int:
50:     if config is None:
51:         return _DEFAULT_CONTINUITY_TTL_SECONDS
52: 
53:     try:
54:         session_cfg = getattr(config, "session", None)
55:         b2bua_cfg = getattr(session_cfg, "b2bua", None)
56:         configured = getattr(b2bua_cfg, "continuity_max_age_seconds", None)
57:         if isinstance(configured, int) and configured >= 1:
58:             return configured
59:     except (AttributeError, TypeError):
60:         return _DEFAULT_CONTINUITY_TTL_SECONDS
61: 
62:     return _DEFAULT_CONTINUITY_TTL_SECONDS

67:         return True
68:

71:         b2bua_cfg = getattr(session_cfg, "b2bua", None)
72:         configured = getattr(b2bua_cfg, "continuity_sliding_expiration", None)
73:         if isinstance(configured, bool):
74:             return configured

76:         return True

127:     async def resolve_or_create_a_session_id(
128:         self,
129:         *,
130:         auth_scope_id: str,
131:         client_session_id: str,
132:         create_a_session_id: Callable[[], str],
133:     ) -> B2buaContinuityResolution:
134:         """Resolve existing active mapping or create a new one.
135: 
136:         Fails open by returning a fresh A-leg id when an internal store error occurs.
137:         """
138:         try:
139:             return await self._resolve_or_create_core(
140:                 auth_scope_id,
141:                 client_session_id,
142:                 create_a_session_id,
143:             )
144:         except Exception as exc:  # - must fail open on store faults
145:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.ERROR):
146:                 logger.error(
147:                     "B2BUA continuity mapping store failure; failing open with new A-leg id",
148:                     exc_info=exc,
149:                 )
150:             return B2buaContinuityResolution(
151:                 a_session_id=create_a_session_id(),
152:                 reused_existing=False,
153:                 had_store_error=True,

155: 
156:     async def try_resolve_echoed_a_session_id(
157:         self,
158:         *,
159:         a_session_id: str,
160:         requesting_auth_scope_id: str | None,
161:     ) -> B2buaContinuityResolution | None:
162:         try:
163:             normalized_a = _normalize_required_identifier(
164:                 a_session_id,
165:                 "a_session_id",
166:             )
167:         except ValueError:
168:             return None
169: 
170:         now = self._time_provider()
171:         async with self._lock:
172:             self._cleanup_expired_entries(now)
173:             key = self._a_session_to_key.get(normalized_a)
174:             if key is None:
175:                 return None
176:             entry = self._entries.get(key)
177:             if entry is None or entry.expires_at <= now:
178:                 return None
179:             stored_auth, _stored_client = key
180:             if not _echo_continuity_auth_allows(
181:                 stored_auth_scope_id=stored_auth,
182:                 requesting_auth_scope_id=requesting_auth_scope_id,
183:             ):
184:                 return None
185:             entry.last_accessed_at = now
186:             if self._sliding_expiration:
187:                 entry.expires_at = now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds
188:             return B2buaContinuityResolution(
189:                 a_session_id=entry.a_session_id,
190:                 reused_existing=True,
191:                 had_store_error=False,
192:             )
193: 
194:     async def allocate_next_b_seq(self, a_session_id: str) -> int:
195:         """Allocate the next per-A-leg B-leg sequence number."""
196:         normalized_a_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
197:             a_session_id,
198:             "a_session_id",
199:         )
200:         now = self._time_provider()
201: 
202:         async with self._lock:
203:             self._cleanup_expired_entries(now)
204:             key = self._a_session_to_key.get(normalized_a_session_id)
205:             if key is None:
206:                 raise KeyError(
207:                     f"A-leg session mapping not found for a_session_id={normalized_a_session_id}"
208:                 )
209: 
210:             entry = self._entries.get(key)
211:             if entry is None:
212:                 self._a_session_to_key.pop(normalized_a_session_id, None)
213:                 raise KeyError(
214:                     f"A-leg session mapping not found for a_session_id={normalized_a_session_id}"
215:                 )
216: 
217:             entry.last_b_seq += 1
218:             entry.last_accessed_at = now
219:             if self._sliding_expiration:
220:                 entry.expires_at = now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds
221: 
222:             return entry.last_b_seq
223: 
224:     async def record_attempt(
225:         self,
226:         *,
227:         a_session_id: str,
228:         b_session_id: str,
229:         seq: int,
230:         backend_type: str | None,
231:         effective_model: str | None,
232:         reason: str | None,
233:     ) -> None:
234:         """Record attempt metadata and retain until mapping expiration."""
235:         normalized_a_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
236:             a_session_id,
237:             "a_session_id",
238:         )
239:         normalized_b_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
240:             b_session_id,
241:             "b_session_id",
242:         )
243:         if seq < 1:
244:             raise ValueError("seq must be >= 1")
245: 
246:         now = self._time_provider()
247:         async with self._lock:
248:             self._cleanup_expired_entries(now)
249:             if normalized_a_session_id not in self._a_session_to_key:
250:                 raise KeyError(
251:                     "A-leg session mapping not found for "
252:                     f"a_session_id={normalized_a_session_id}"
253:                 )
254: 
255:             record = B2buaAttemptRecord(
256:                 b_session_id=normalized_b_session_id,
257:                 a_session_id=normalized_a_session_id,
258:                 seq=seq,
259:                 backend_type=backend_type,
260:                 effective_model=effective_model,
261:                 reason=reason,
262:             )
263:             records = self._attempt_records.setdefault(normalized_a_session_id, [])
264:             records.append(record)
265: 
266:     async def get_attempt_records(self, a_session_id: str) -> list[B2buaAttemptRecord]:
267:         """Return attempt records retained for one A-leg session."""
268:         normalized_a_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
269:             a_session_id,
270:             "a_session_id",
271:         )
272:         now = self._time_provider()
273:         async with self._lock:
274:             self._cleanup_expired_entries(now)
275:             return list(self._attempt_records.get(normalized_a_session_id, []))
276: 
277:     async def _resolve_or_create_core(
278:         self,
279:         auth_scope_id: str,
280:         client_session_id: str,
281:         create_a_session_id: Callable[[], str],
282:     ) -> B2buaContinuityResolution:
283:         key = (
284:             _normalize_required_identifier(auth_scope_id, "auth_scope_id"),
285:             _normalize_required_identifier(client_session_id, "client_session_id"),
286:         )
287:         now = self._time_provider()
288: 
289:         async with self._lock:
290:             self._cleanup_expired_entries(now)
291: 
292:             existing = self._entries.get(key)
293:             if existing is not None:
294:                 existing.last_accessed_at = now
295:                 if self._sliding_expiration:
296:                     existing.expires_at = now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds
297:                 return B2buaContinuityResolution(
298:                     a_session_id=existing.a_session_id,
299:                     reused_existing=True,
300:                     had_store_error=False,
301:                 )
302: 
303:             a_session_id = create_a_session_id()
304:             entry = _ContinuityEntry(
305:                 a_session_id=a_session_id,
306:                 expires_at=now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds,
307:                 last_accessed_at=now,
308:                 last_b_seq=0,
309:             )
310:             self._entries[key] = entry
311:             self._a_session_to_key[a_session_id] = key
312:             self._evict_if_needed()
313: 
314:             return B2buaContinuityResolution(
315:                 a_session_id=a_session_id,
316:                 reused_existing=False,
317:                 had_store_error=False,
318:             )
319: 
320:     def _cleanup_expired_entries(self, now: float) -> None:
321:         expired_keys = [
322:             key for key, entry in self._entries.items() if entry.expires_at <= now
323:         ]
324:         for key in expired_keys:
325:             entry = self._entries.pop(key, None)
326:             if entry is not None:
327:                 self._a_session_to_key.pop(entry.a_session_id, None)
328:                 self._attempt_records.pop(entry.a_session_id, None)
329: 
330:     def _evict_if_needed(self) -> None:
331:         while len(self._entries) > self._max_entries:
332:             oldest_key = min(
333:                 self._entries.items(),
334:                 key=lambda item: item[1].last_accessed_at,
335:             )[0]
336:             entry = self._entries.pop(oldest_key, None)
337:             if entry is not None:
338:                 self._a_session_to_key.pop(entry.a_session_id, None)
339:                 self._attempt_records.pop(entry.a_session_id, None)

342: class PersistentB2buaMappingStore(IB2buaMappingStore):
343:     """SQLite-backed continuity mapping store for restart and worker safety."""
344: 
345:     def __init__(
346:         self,
347:         *,
348:         database_path: str | Path | None = None,
349:         config: IConfig | None = None,
350:         continuity_ttl_seconds: int | None = None,
351:         sliding_expiration: bool | None = None,
352:         max_entries: int = _DEFAULT_MAX_MAPPINGS,
353:         time_provider: Callable[[], float] | None = None,
354:     ) -> None:
355:         if max_entries < 1:
356:             raise ValueError("max_entries must be >= 1")
357:

359:         config_sliding = _read_config_sliding_expiration(config)
360: 
361:         self._database_path = (
362:             Path(database_path)
363:             if database_path is not None
364:             else _DEFAULT_PERSISTENT_DB_PATH
365:         )
366:         self._database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
367:         self._continuity_ttl_seconds = (
368:             continuity_ttl_seconds if continuity_ttl_seconds is not None else config_ttl
369:         )
370:         self._sliding_expiration = (
371:             sliding_expiration if sliding_expiration is not None else config_sliding
372:         )
373:         self._max_entries = max_entries
374:         self._time_provider = time_provider if time_provider is not None else time.time
375:         self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
376: 
377:         self._initialize_database()

379:     async def resolve_or_create_a_session_id(
380:         self,
381:         *,
382:         auth_scope_id: str,
383:         client_session_id: str,
384:         create_a_session_id: Callable[[], str],
385:     ) -> B2buaContinuityResolution:
386:         """Resolve existing active mapping or create a new one.
387: 
388:         Fails open by returning a fresh A-leg id when an internal store error occurs.
389:         """
390:         try:
391:             return await self._resolve_or_create_core(
392:                 auth_scope_id,
393:                 client_session_id,
394:                 create_a_session_id,
395:             )
396:         except Exception as exc:  # - must fail open on store faults
397:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.ERROR):
398:                 logger.error(
399:                     "Persistent B2BUA continuity store failure; failing open with new A-leg id",
400:                     exc_info=exc,
401:                 )
402:             return B2buaContinuityResolution(
403:                 a_session_id=create_a_session_id(),
404:                 reused_existing=False,
405:                 had_store_error=True,
406:             )
407: 
408:     async def try_resolve_echoed_a_session_id(
409:         self,
410:         *,
411:         a_session_id: str,
412:         requesting_auth_scope_id: str | None,
413:     ) -> B2buaContinuityResolution | None:
414:         try:
415:             normalized_a = _normalize_required_identifier(
416:                 a_session_id,
417:                 "a_session_id",
418:             )
419:         except ValueError:
420:             return None
421: 
422:         now = self._time_provider()
423:         try:
424:             async with self._lock:
425:                 with self._connect() as conn:
426:                     conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
427:                     self._cleanup_expired_entries(conn, now)
428:                     row = conn.execute(
429:                         """
430:                         SELECT auth_scope_id, expires_at
431:                         FROM b2bua_mappings
432:                         WHERE a_session_id = ?
433:                         """,
434:                         (normalized_a,),
435:                     ).fetchone()
436:                     if row is None:
437:                         conn.commit()
438:                         return None
439:                     expires_at = float(row["expires_at"])
440:                     if expires_at <= now:
441:                         conn.commit()
442:                         return None
443:                     stored_auth = str(row["auth_scope_id"])
444:                     if not _echo_continuity_auth_allows(
445:                         stored_auth_scope_id=stored_auth,
446:                         requesting_auth_scope_id=requesting_auth_scope_id,
447:                     ):
448:                         conn.commit()
449:                         return None
450:                     if self._sliding_expiration:
451:                         conn.execute(
452:                             """
453:                             UPDATE b2bua_mappings
454:                             SET last_accessed_at = ?, expires_at = ?
455:                             WHERE a_session_id = ?
456:                             """,
457:                             (
458:                                 now,
459:                                 now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds,
460:                                 normalized_a,
461:                             ),
462:                         )
463:                     else:
464:                         conn.execute(
465:                             """
466:                             UPDATE b2bua_mappings
467:                             SET last_accessed_at = ?
468:                             WHERE a_session_id = ?
469:                             """,
470:                             (now, normalized_a),
471:                         )
472:                     conn.commit()
473:                     return B2buaContinuityResolution(
474:                         a_session_id=normalized_a,
475:                         reused_existing=True,
476:                         had_store_error=False,
477:                     )
478:         except Exception as exc:  # - must fail open on store faults
479:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.ERROR):
480:                 logger.error(
481:                     "Persistent B2BUA echo continuity lookup failure",
482:                     exc_info=exc,
483:                 )
484:             return None
485: 
486:     async def allocate_next_b_seq(self, a_session_id: str) -> int:
487:         """Atomically allocate next B-leg sequence for an active A-leg mapping."""
488:         normalized_a_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
489:             a_session_id,
490:             "a_session_id",
491:         )
492:         now = self._time_provider()
493: 
494:         async with self._lock:
495:             with self._connect() as conn:
496:                 conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
497:                 self._cleanup_expired_entries(conn, now)
498:                 row = conn.execute(
499:                     """
500:                     SELECT last_b_seq
501:                     FROM b2bua_mappings
502:                     WHERE a_session_id = ?
503:                     """,
504:                     (normalized_a_session_id,),
505:                 ).fetchone()
506:                 if row is None:
507:                     raise KeyError(
508:                         "A-leg session mapping not found for "
509:                         f"a_session_id={normalized_a_session_id}"
510:                     )
511: 
512:                 next_seq = int(row["last_b_seq"]) + 1
513:                 if self._sliding_expiration:
514:                     conn.execute(
515:                         """
516:                         UPDATE b2bua_mappings
517:                         SET last_b_seq = ?, last_accessed_at = ?, expires_at = ?
518:                         WHERE a_session_id = ?
519:                         """,
520:                         (
521:                             next_seq,
522:                             now,
523:                             now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds,
524:                             normalized_a_session_id,
525:                         ),
526:                     )
527:                 else:
528:                     conn.execute(
529:                         """
530:                         UPDATE b2bua_mappings
531:                         SET last_b_seq = ?, last_accessed_at = ?
532:                         WHERE a_session_id = ?
533:                         """,
534:                         (
535:                             next_seq,
536:                             now,
537:                             normalized_a_session_id,
538:                         ),
539:                     )
540:                 conn.commit()
541:                 return next_seq
542: 
543:     async def record_attempt(
544:         self,
545:         *,
546:         a_session_id: str,
547:         b_session_id: str,
548:         seq: int,
549:         backend_type: str | None,
550:         effective_model: str | None,
551:         reason: str | None,
552:     ) -> None:
553:         """Persist backend attempt metadata for diagnostics."""
554:         normalized_a_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
555:             a_session_id,
556:             "a_session_id",
557:         )
558:         normalized_b_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
559:             b_session_id,
560:             "b_session_id",
561:         )
562:         if seq < 1:
563:             raise ValueError("seq must be >= 1")
564: 
565:         now = self._time_provider()
566:         async with self._lock:
567:             with self._connect() as conn:
568:                 conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
569:                 self._cleanup_expired_entries(conn, now)
570:                 mapping_row = conn.execute(
571:                     """
572:                     SELECT 1
573:                     FROM b2bua_mappings
574:                     WHERE a_session_id = ?
575:                     """,
576:                     (normalized_a_session_id,),
577:                 ).fetchone()
578:                 if mapping_row is None:
579:                     raise KeyError(
580:                         "A-leg session mapping not found for "
581:                         f"a_session_id={normalized_a_session_id}"
582:                     )
583: 
584:                 conn.execute(
585:                     """
586:                     INSERT INTO b2bua_attempts (
587:                         b_session_id,
588:                         a_session_id,
589:                         seq,
590:                         backend_type,
591:                         effective_model,
592:                         reason,
593:                         created_at
594:                     ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
595:                     """,
596:                     (
597:                         normalized_b_session_id,
598:                         normalized_a_session_id,
599:                         seq,
600:                         backend_type,
601:                         effective_model,
602:                         reason,
603:                         now,
604:                     ),
605:                 )
606: 
607:                 if self._sliding_expiration:
608:                     conn.execute(
609:                         """
610:                         UPDATE b2bua_mappings
611:                         SET last_accessed_at = ?, expires_at = ?
612:                         WHERE a_session_id = ?
613:                         """,
614:                         (
615:                             now,
616:                             now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds,
617:                             normalized_a_session_id,
618:                         ),
619:                     )
620:                 else:
621:                     conn.execute(
622:                         """
623:                         UPDATE b2bua_mappings
624:                         SET last_accessed_at = ?
625:                         WHERE a_session_id = ?
626:                         """,
627:                         (
628:                             now,
629:                             normalized_a_session_id,
630:                         ),
631:                     )
632:                 conn.commit()
633: 
634:     async def get_attempt_records(self, a_session_id: str) -> list[B2buaAttemptRecord]:
635:         """Load attempt records for one A-leg ordered by sequence."""
636:         normalized_a_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
637:             a_session_id,
638:             "a_session_id",
639:         )
640:         now = self._time_provider()
641:         async with self._lock:
642:             with self._connect() as conn:
643:                 conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
644:                 self._cleanup_expired_entries(conn, now)
645:                 rows = conn.execute(
646:                     """
647:                     SELECT b_session_id, a_session_id, seq, backend_type, effective_model, reason
648:                     FROM b2bua_attempts
649:                     WHERE a_session_id = ?
650:                     ORDER BY seq ASC, created_at ASC
651:                     """,
652:                     (normalized_a_session_id,),
653:                 ).fetchall()
654:                 conn.commit()
655:                 return [
656:                     B2buaAttemptRecord(
657:                         b_session_id=str(row["b_session_id"]),
658:                         a_session_id=str(row["a_session_id"]),
659:                         seq=int(row["seq"]),
660:                         backend_type=row["backend_type"],
661:                         effective_model=row["effective_model"],
662:                         reason=row["reason"],
663:                     )
664:                     for row in rows
665:                 ]
666: 
667:     async def _resolve_or_create_core(
668:         self,
669:         auth_scope_id: str,
670:         client_session_id: str,
671:         create_a_session_id: Callable[[], str],
672:     ) -> B2buaContinuityResolution:
673:         normalized_auth_scope = _normalize_required_identifier(
674:             auth_scope_id,
675:             "auth_scope_id",
676:         )
677:         normalized_client_session_id = _normalize_required_identifier(
678:             client_session_id,
679:             "client_session_id",
680:         )
681:         now = self._time_provider()
682: 
683:         async with self._lock:
684:             with self._connect() as conn:
685:                 conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
686:                 self._cleanup_expired_entries(conn, now)
687:                 existing_row = conn.execute(
688:                     """
689:                     SELECT a_session_id
690:                     FROM b2bua_mappings
691:                     WHERE auth_scope_id = ? AND client_session_id = ?
692:                     """,
693:                     (normalized_auth_scope, normalized_client_session_id),
694:                 ).fetchone()
695:                 if existing_row is not None:
696:                     a_session_id = str(existing_row["a_session_id"])
697:                     if self._sliding_expiration:
698:                         conn.execute(
699:                             """
700:                             UPDATE b2bua_mappings
701:                             SET last_accessed_at = ?, expires_at = ?
702:                             WHERE auth_scope_id = ? AND client_session_id = ?
703:                             """,
704:                             (
705:                                 now,
706:                                 now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds,
707:                                 normalized_auth_scope,
708:                                 normalized_client_session_id,
709:                             ),
710:                         )
711:                     else:
712:                         conn.execute(
713:                             """
714:                             UPDATE b2bua_mappings
715:                             SET last_accessed_at = ?
716:                             WHERE auth_scope_id = ? AND client_session_id = ?
717:                             """,
718:                             (
719:                                 now,
720:                                 normalized_auth_scope,
721:                                 normalized_client_session_id,
722:                             ),
723:                         )
724:                     conn.commit()
725:                     return B2buaContinuityResolution(
726:                         a_session_id=a_session_id,
727:                         reused_existing=True,
728:                         had_store_error=False,
729:                     )
730: 
731:                 a_session_id = create_a_session_id()
732:                 conn.execute(
733:                     """
734:                     INSERT INTO b2bua_mappings (
735:                         auth_scope_id,
736:                         client_session_id,
737:                         a_session_id,
738:                         last_b_seq,
739:                         created_at,
740:                         last_accessed_at,
741:                         expires_at
742:                     ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, ?)
743:                     """,
744:                     (
745:                         normalized_auth_scope,
746:                         normalized_client_session_id,
747:                         a_session_id,
748:                         now,
749:                         now,
750:                         now + self._continuity_ttl_seconds,
751:                     ),
752:                 )
753:                 self._evict_if_needed(conn)
754:                 conn.commit()
755:                 return B2buaContinuityResolution(
756:                     a_session_id=a_session_id,
757:                     reused_existing=False,
758:                     had_store_error=False,
759:                 )
760: 
761:     def _initialize_database(self) -> None:
762:         with self._connect() as conn:
763:             conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
764:             conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
765:             conn.execute(
766:                 """
767:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS b2bua_mappings (
768:                     auth_scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,
769:                     client_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
770:                     a_session_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
771:                     last_b_seq INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
772:                     created_at REAL NOT NULL,
773:                     last_accessed_at REAL NOT NULL,
774:                     expires_at REAL NOT NULL,
775:                     PRIMARY KEY (auth_scope_id, client_session_id)
776:                 )
777:                 """
778:             )
779:             conn.execute(
780:                 """
781:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS b2bua_attempts (
782:                     b_session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
783:                     a_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
784:                     seq INTEGER NOT NULL,
785:                     backend_type TEXT,
786:                     effective_model TEXT,
787:                     reason TEXT,
788:                     created_at REAL NOT NULL,
789:                     FOREIGN KEY (a_session_id)
790:                         REFERENCES b2bua_mappings(a_session_id)
791:                         ON DELETE CASCADE
792:                 )
793:                 """
794:             )
795:             conn.execute(
796:                 """
797:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_b2bua_mappings_expires_at
798:                 ON b2bua_mappings(expires_at)
799:                 """
800:             )
801:             conn.execute(
802:                 """
803:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_b2bua_mappings_last_accessed
804:                 ON b2bua_mappings(last_accessed_at)
805:                 """
806:             )
807:             conn.execute(
808:                 """
809:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_b2bua_attempts_a_session
810:                 ON b2bua_attempts(a_session_id)
811:                 """
812:             )
813:             conn.commit()
814: 
815:     def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
816:         connection = sqlite3.connect(
817:             str(self._database_path),
818:             timeout=_SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
819:             isolation_level=None,
820:             check_same_thread=False,
821:         )
822:         connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
823:         connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
824:         return connection
825: 
826:     def _cleanup_expired_entries(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: float) -> None:
827:         conn.execute(
828:             """
829:             DELETE FROM b2bua_mappings
830:             WHERE expires_at <= ?
831:             """,
832:             (now,),
833:         )
834: 
835:     def _evict_if_needed(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
836:         count_row = conn.execute(
837:             "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM b2bua_mappings"
838:         ).fetchone()
839:         total_entries = int(count_row["total"]) if count_row is not None else 0
840:         overflow = total_entries - self._max_entries
841:         if overflow <= 0:
842:             return
843: 
844:         rows_to_evict = conn.execute(
845:             """
846:             SELECT auth_scope_id, client_session_id
847:             FROM b2bua_mappings
848:             ORDER BY last_accessed_at ASC
849:             LIMIT ?
850:             """,
851:             (overflow,),
852:         ).fetchall()
853: 
854:         for row in rows_to_evict:
855:             conn.execute(
856:                 """
857:                 DELETE FROM b2bua_mappings
858:                 WHERE auth_scope_id = ? AND client_session_id = ?
859:                 """,
860:                 (
861:                     str(row["auth_scope_id"]),
862:                     str(row["client_session_id"]),
863:                 ),
864:             )
865: 
866:

33: // profiles for the lifetime of the catalog instance.

68: 	c := &ModelCatalog{binary: "test"}

74: // EnsureLoaded discovers models once. Cancellation is returned rather than
75: // cached as a fallback so a later call can build a fresh catalog instance.
76: func (c *ModelCatalog) EnsureLoaded(ctx context.Context) error {
77: 	var loadErr error
78: 	c.once.Do(func() {
79: 		loadErr = c.load(ctx)
80: 	})
81: 	if loadErr != nil {
82: 		return loadErr
83: 	}
84: 	c.mu.RLock()
85: 	loaded := c.loaded
86: 	c.mu.RUnlock()
87: 	if !loaded {
88: 		return fmt.Errorf("model catalog was not loaded")
89: 	}
90: 	return nil
91: }
92: 
93: func (c *ModelCatalog) load(ctx context.Context) error {
94: 	ids, err := discoverModelIDs(ctx, c.binary)
95: 	if err != nil {
96: 		if errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
97: 			return err
98: 		}
99: 		if !c.allowFallback {
100: 			return err
101: 		}
102: 		slog.Warn("agy model discovery failed, using fallback list", "error", err)
103: 		ids = fallbackModelIDs
104: 	} else if len(ids) == 0 {
105: 		if !c.allowFallback {
106: 			return fmt.Errorf("agy model discovery returned no models")
107: 		}
108: 		slog.Warn("agy model discovery returned no models, using fallback list")
109: 		ids = fallbackModelIDs
110: 	} else {
111: 		slog.Info("discovered agy models", "count", len(ids))
112: 	}
113: 	profiles := normalizeModelIDs(ids)
114: 	if len(profiles) == 0 {
115: 		if !c.allowFallback {
116: 			return fmt.Errorf("agy model discovery produced no recognized models")
117: 		}
118: 		slog.Warn("agy model discovery produced no recognized models, using fallback list")
119: 		profiles = normalizeModelIDs(fallbackModelIDs)
120: 	}
121: 	c.setProfiles(profiles)
122: 	return nil
123: }
124: 
125: func (c *ModelCatalog) setProfiles(profiles []ModelProfile) {
126: 	byID := make(map[string]ModelProfile, len(profiles))
127: 	aliases := make(map[string]string)
128: 	for _, profile := range profiles {
129: 		byID[profile.CanonicalID] = profile
130: 		aliases[profile.CanonicalID] = profile.CanonicalID
131: 		aliases[profile.DisplayName] = profile.CanonicalID
132: 		if profile.Native != "" {
133: 			aliases[profile.Native] = profile.CanonicalID
134: 		}
135: 		for _, native := range profile.NativeByEffort {
136: 			aliases[native] = profile.CanonicalID
137: 		}
138: 	}
139: 	addLegacyAliases(aliases, byID)
140: 
141: 	c.mu.Lock()
142: 	defer c.mu.Unlock()
143: 	c.profiles = profiles
144: 	c.byID = byID
145: 	c.aliases = aliases
146: 	c.defaultModel = pickDefaultModel(profiles)
147: 	c.loaded = true
148: }

169: func (c *ModelCatalog) Profiles() []ModelProfile {
170: 	c.mu.RLock()
171: 	defer c.mu.RUnlock()
172: 	out := make([]ModelProfile, len(c.profiles))
173: 	copy(out, c.profiles)
174: 	return out
175: }

178: 	c.mu.RLock()
179: 	defer c.mu.RUnlock()
180: 	return c.defaultModel

183: func (c *ModelCatalog) Contains(id string) bool {
184: 	c.mu.RLock()
185: 	defer c.mu.RUnlock()
186: 	_, ok := c.byID[id]
187: 	return ok
188: }

190: // ResolveCanonical maps public, native, and legacy identifiers to a canonical ID.
191: func (c *ModelCatalog) ResolveCanonical(input string) (string, error) {
192: 	input = strings.TrimSpace(input)
193: 	if input == "" || input == "auto" {
194: 		return c.DefaultModelID(), nil
195: 	}
196: 	c.mu.RLock()
197: 	canonical, ok := c.aliases[input]
198: 	c.mu.RUnlock()
199: 	if !ok {
200: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("unknown model: %s", input)
201: 	}
202: 	return canonical, nil
203: }
204: 
205: // ResolveNative selects the exact ID accepted by agy for a canonical model and effort.
206: func (c *ModelCatalog) ResolveNative(model, effort string) (string, error) {
207: 	canonical, err := c.ResolveCanonical(model)
208: 	if err != nil {
209: 		return "", err
210: 	}
211: 	c.mu.RLock()
212: 	profile := c.byID[canonical]
213: 	c.mu.RUnlock()
214: 
215: 	effort = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(effort))
216: 	if effort == "" {
217: 		effort = profile.DefaultEffort
218: 	}
219: 	if effort != "" {
220: 		if native, ok := profile.NativeByEffort[effort]; ok {
221: 			return native, nil
222: 		}
223: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("model %s does not support reasoning effort %s", canonical, effort)
224: 	}
225: 	if profile.Native != "" {
226: 		return profile.Native, nil
227: 	}
228: 	return "", fmt.Errorf("model %s has no native agy variant", canonical)
229: }
230: 
231: func (c *ModelCatalog) SupportedEfforts(model string) []string {
232: 	canonical, err := c.ResolveCanonical(model)
233: 	if err != nil {
234: 		return nil
235: 	}
236: 	c.mu.RLock()
237: 	profile := c.byID[canonical]
238: 	c.mu.RUnlock()
239: 	return orderedEfforts(profile.NativeByEffort)
240: }
241: 
242: func discoverModelIDs(ctx context.Context, binary string) ([]string, error) {
243: 	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, binary, "models")
244: 	hideWindow(cmd)
245: 	cmd.Env = agyEnvironment()
246: 	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
247: 	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
248: 	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
249: 	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
250: 		if ctxErr := ctx.Err(); ctxErr != nil {
251: 			return nil, ctxErr
252: 		}
253: 		msg := strings.TrimSpace(stderr.String())
254: 		if msg == "" {
255: 			msg = err.Error()
256: 		}
257: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("agy models: %s", msg)
258: 	}
259: 
260: 	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
261: 	ids := make([]string, 0)
262: 	for line := range strings.Lines(stdout.String()) {
263: 		id := strings.TrimSpace(line)
264: 		if id == "" || strings.ContainsAny(id, " \t") {
265: 			continue
266: 		}
267: 		if _, ok := seen[id]; ok {
268: 			continue
269: 		}
270: 		seen[id] = struct{}{}
271: 		ids = append(ids, id)
272: 	}
273: 	return ids, nil
274: }
275: 
276: func agyEnvironment() []string {
277: 	env := os.Environ()
278: 	home := strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv("USERPROFILE"))
279: 	if home == "" {
280: 		home = strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv("HOME"))
281: 	}
282: 	if home == "" {
283: 		if current, err := user.Current(); err == nil {
284: 			home = strings.TrimSpace(current.HomeDir)
285: 		}
286: 	}
287: 	if home != "" {
288: 		if os.Getenv("USERPROFILE") == "" {
289: 			env = append(env, "USERPROFILE="+home)
290: 		}
291: 		if os.Getenv("HOME") == "" {
292: 			env = append(env, "HOME="+home)
293: 		}
294: 	}
295: 	return env
296: }
297: 
298: func normalizeModelIDs(ids []string) []ModelProfile {
299: 	type builder struct {
300: 		profile ModelProfile
301: 	}
302: 	byCanonical := make(map[string]*builder)
303: 	order := make([]string, 0)
304: 	for _, native := range ids {
305: 		canonical, display, effort, thinking, ok := parseNativeModelID(strings.TrimSpace(native))
306: 		if !ok {
307: 			slog.Warn("unrecognized agy model omitted", "model", native)
308: 			continue
309: 		}
310: 		b := byCanonical[canonical]
311: 		if b == nil {
312: 			b = &builder{profile: ModelProfile{CanonicalID: canonical, DisplayName: display, NativeByEffort: make(map[string]string)}}
313: 			byCanonical[canonical] = b
314: 			order = append(order, canonical)
315: 		}
316: 		if effort != "" {
317: 			b.profile.NativeByEffort[effort] = native
318: 		} else {
319: 			b.profile.Native = native
320: 		}
321: 		if thinking && b.profile.Native == "" {
322: 			b.profile.Native = native
323: 		}
324: 	}
325: 
326: 	profiles := make([]ModelProfile, 0, len(order))
327: 	for _, canonical := range order {
328: 		p := byCanonical[canonical].profile
329: 		p.DefaultEffort = pickDefaultEffort(p.NativeByEffort)
330: 		profiles = append(profiles, p)
331: 	}
332: 	return profiles
333: }
334: 
335: func parseNativeModelID(native string) (canonical, display, effort string, thinking, ok bool) {
336: 	if native == "" {
337: 		return "", "", "", false, false
338: 	}
339: 	base := native
340: 	for _, candidate := range []string{ReasoningLow, ReasoningMedium, ReasoningHigh} {
341: 		if strings.HasSuffix(base, "-"+candidate) {
342: 			effort = candidate
343: 			base = strings.TrimSuffix(base, "-"+candidate)
344: 			break
345: 		}
346: 	}
347: 	if strings.HasSuffix(base, "-thinking") {
348: 		thinking = true
349: 		base = strings.TrimSuffix(base, "-thinking")
350: 	}
351: 
352: 	switch {
353: 	case strings.HasPrefix(base, "gemini-"):
354: 		name := strings.TrimPrefix(base, "gemini-")
355: 		canonical = "google/gemini-" + name
356: 		display = "Gemini " + displayModelTail(name)
357: 	case strings.HasPrefix(base, "claude-"):
358: 		name := strings.TrimPrefix(base, "claude-")
359: 		name = normalizeClaudeVersion(name)
360: 		canonical = "anthropic/claude-" + name
361: 		display = "Claude " + displayModelTail(name)
362: 	case strings.HasPrefix(base, "gpt-"):
363: 		name := strings.TrimPrefix(base, "gpt-")
364: 		canonical = "openai/gpt-" + name
365: 		display = "GPT " + displayModelTail(name)
366: 	default:
367: 		return "", "", "", false, false
368: 	}
369: 	return canonical, display, effort, thinking, true
370: }
371: 
372: func normalizeClaudeVersion(name string) string {
373: 	parts := strings.Split(name, "-")
374: 	for i := 0; i+1 < len(parts); i++ {
375: 		if allDigits(parts[i]) && allDigits(parts[i+1]) {
376: 			parts[i] += "." + parts[i+1]
377: 			parts = append(parts[:i+1], parts[i+2:]...)
378: 			break
379: 		}
380: 	}
381: 	return strings.Join(parts, "-")
382: }
383: 
384: func formatModelDisplayName(id string) string {
385: 	_, display, effort, thinking, ok := parseNativeModelID(id)
386: 	if !ok {
387: 		return id
388: 	}
389: 	if effort != "" {
390: 		display += " (" + strings.ToUpper(effort[:1]) + effort[1:] + ")"
391: 	} else if thinking {
392: 		display += " (Thinking)"
393: 	}
394: 	return display
395: }
396: 
397: func displayModelTail(name string) string {
398: 	parts := strings.Split(name, "-")
399: 	for i, part := range parts {
400: 		if part == "oss" {
401: 			parts[i] = "OSS"
402: 			continue
403: 		}
404: 		if part != "" {
405: 			parts[i] = strings.ToUpper(part[:1]) + part[1:]
406: 		}
407: 	}
408: 	return strings.Join(parts, " ")
409: }
410: 
411: func allDigits(s string) bool {
412: 	if s == "" {
413: 		return false
414: 	}
415: 	for _, r := range s {
416: 		if r < '0' || r > '9' {
417: 			return false
418: 		}
419: 	}
420: 	return true
421: }
422: 
423: func pickDefaultEffort(native map[string]string) string {
424: 	for _, effort := range []string{ReasoningMedium, ReasoningHigh, ReasoningLow} {
425: 		if _, ok := native[effort]; ok {
426: 			return effort
427: 		}
428: 	}
429: 	return ""
430: }
431: 
432: func pickDefaultModel(profiles []ModelProfile) string {
433: 	for _, preferred := range []string{"google/gemini-3.6-flash", "google/gemini-3.5-flash"} {
434: 		for _, profile := range profiles {
435: 			if profile.CanonicalID == preferred {
436: 				return preferred
437: 			}
438: 		}
439: 	}
440: 	if len(profiles) > 0 {
441: 		return profiles[0].CanonicalID
442: 	}
443: 	return ""
444: }
445: 
446: func orderedEfforts(values map[string]string) []string {
447: 	out := make([]string, 0, len(values))
448: 	for _, effort := range []string{ReasoningLow, ReasoningMedium, ReasoningHigh} {
449: 		if _, ok := values[effort]; ok {
450: 			out = append(out, effort)
451: 		}
452: 	}
453: 	unknown := make([]string, 0)
454: 	for effort := range values {
455: 		if effort != ReasoningLow && effort != ReasoningMedium && effort != ReasoningHigh {
456: 			unknown = append(unknown, effort)
457: 		}
458: 	}
459: 	sort.Strings(unknown)
460: 	return append(out, unknown...)
461: }
462: 
463: func addLegacyAliases(aliases map[string]string, profiles map[string]ModelProfile) {
464: 	legacy := map[string]string{
465: 		"google/gemini-3.5-flash-high":       "google/gemini-3.5-flash",
466: 		"google/gemini-3.5-flash-medium":     "google/gemini-3.5-flash",
467: 		"google/gemini-3.5-flash-low":        "google/gemini-3.5-flash",
468: 		"google/gemini-3.1-pro-high":         "google/gemini-3.1-pro",
469: 		"google/gemini-3.1-pro-low":          "google/gemini-3.1-pro",
470: 		"anthropic/claude-opus-4.6-thinking": "anthropic/claude-opus-4.6",
471: 	}
472: 	for alias, canonical := range legacy {
473: 		if _, ok := profiles[canonical]; ok {
474: 			aliases[alias] = canonical
475: 		}
476: 	}
477: }
