14: type ConversationDiscoverer struct {
15: 	configDir string
16: }
17: 
18: const (
19: 	conversationReadAttempts = 10
20: 	conversationReadDelay    = 20 * time.Millisecond
21: )
22: 
23: var errConversationNotFound = errors.New("conversation not found")
24: 
25: func NewConversationDiscoverer(configDir string) *ConversationDiscoverer {
26: 	return &ConversationDiscoverer{configDir: configDir}
27: }
28: 
29: func (d *ConversationDiscoverer) DiscoverConversationID(cwd string) (string, error) {
30: 	return d.DiscoverConversationIDContext(context.Background(), cwd)
31: }
32: 
33: func (d *ConversationDiscoverer) DiscoverConversationIDContext(ctx context.Context, cwd string) (string, error) {
34: 	convMap, err := d.readConversationMap(ctx)
35: 	if err != nil {
36: 		return "", err
37: 	}
38: 	return lookupConversationID(convMap, cwd)
39: }
40: 
41: // SnapshotConversationID returns the pre-invocation mapping. A missing cache or
42: // cwd entry is a valid empty snapshot; malformed or unreadable state is not.
43: func (d *ConversationDiscoverer) SnapshotConversationID(ctx context.Context, cwd string) (string, error) {
44: 	convMap, err := d.readConversationMap(ctx)
45: 	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
46: 		return "", nil
47: 	}
48: 	if err != nil {
49: 		return "", err
50: 	}
51: 	id, err := lookupConversationID(convMap, cwd)
52: 	if errors.Is(err, errConversationNotFound) {
53: 		return "", nil
54: 	}
55: 	return id, err
56: }
57: 
58: func isSafeConversationID(id string) bool {
59: 	if id == "" || id == "." || id == ".." || filepath.IsAbs(id) {
60: 		return false
61: 	}
62: 	if filepath.Clean(id) != id || filepath.Base(id) != id {
63: 		return false
64: 	}
65: 	return !strings.ContainsAny(id, `/\\`)
66: }
67: 
68: // DiscoverNewConversationID accepts only a changed mapping whose brain directory
69: // was created or updated during this invocation.
70: func (d *ConversationDiscoverer) DiscoverNewConversationID(ctx context.Context, cwd, previousID string, startedAt time.Time) (string, error) {
71: 	convMap, err := d.readConversationMap(ctx)
72: 	if err != nil {
73: 		return "", err
74: 	}
75: 	id, err := lookupConversationID(convMap, cwd)
76: 	if err != nil {
77: 		return "", err
78: 	}
79: 	if id == previousID {
80: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("conversation for cwd %q unchanged (%q)", cwd, id)
81: 	}
82: 	if !isSafeConversationID(id) {
83: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("discovered conversation ID %q is not a safe single path component", id)
84: 	}
85: 	brainRoot := filepath.Join(d.configDir, "brain")
86: 	brainPath := filepath.Join(brainRoot, id)
87: 	relative, err := filepath.Rel(brainRoot, brainPath)
88: 	if err != nil || relative != id {
89: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("discovered conversation ID %q escapes brain directory", id)
90: 	}
91: 	info, err := os.Stat(brainPath)
92: 	if err != nil || !info.IsDir() {
93: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("discovered conversation %q has no brain directory", id)
94: 	}
95: 	if info.ModTime().Before(startedAt.Add(-time.Second)) {
96: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("discovered conversation %q predates this invocation", id)
97: 	}
98: 	return id, nil
99: }
100: 
101: func (d *ConversationDiscoverer) readConversationMap(ctx context.Context) (map[string]string, error) {
102: 	path := filepath.Join(d.configDir, "cache", "last_conversations.json")
103: 	var lastErr error
104: 	for attempt := 0; attempt < conversationReadAttempts; attempt++ {
105: 		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
106: 			return nil, err
107: 		}
108: 		data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
109: 		if err == nil {
110: 			var convMap map[string]string
111: 			if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &convMap); err == nil {
112: 				return convMap, nil
113: 			}
114: 			lastErr = err
115: 		} else {
116: 			lastErr = err
117: 		}
118: 		if attempt+1 >= conversationReadAttempts {
119: 			break
120: 		}
121: 		select {
122: 		case <-ctx.Done():
123: 			return nil, ctx.Err()
124: 		case <-time.After(conversationReadDelay):
125: 		}
126: 	}
127: 	return nil, fmt.Errorf("read stable last_conversations.json: %w", lastErr)
128: }
129: 
130: func lookupConversationID(convMap map[string]string, cwd string) (string, error) {
131: 	normalizedCwd := filepath.Clean(cwd)
132: 	if id, ok := convMap[normalizedCwd]; ok {
133: 		return id, nil
134: 	}
135: 	for key, id := range convMap {
136: 		if filepath.Clean(key) == normalizedCwd {
137: 			return id, nil
138: 		}
139: 	}
140: 	return "", fmt.Errorf("%w for cwd %q", errConversationNotFound, cwd)
141: }
142: 
143: func (d *ConversationDiscoverer) ValidateConversationID(id string) bool {
144: 	brainPath := filepath.Join(d.configDir, "brain", id)
145: 	info, err := os.Stat(brainPath)
146: 	if err != nil {
147: 		return false
148: 	}
149: 	return info.IsDir()
150: }

129: func (a *AgyAgent) Prompt(ctx context.Context, params acp.PromptRequest) (acp.PromptResponse, error) {
130: 	sid := string(params.SessionId)
131: 	sess, ok := a.store.Get(sid)
132: 	if !ok {
133: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s not found", sid)
134: 	}
135: 
136: 	promptText := extractPromptText(params.Prompt)
137: 	if promptText == "" {
138: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("empty prompt")
139: 	}
140: 
141: 	promptCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
142: 	token, _, ok := a.startPrompt(sid, cancel)
143: 	if !ok {
144: 		cancel()
145: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s already has an active prompt", sid)
146: 	}
147: 	defer a.finishPrompt(sid, token)
148: 
149: 	unlock, err := a.coordinator.Lock(promptCtx, sess.Cwd)
150: 	if err != nil {
151: 		if promptCtx.Err() == context.Canceled {
152: 			return acp.PromptResponse{StopReason: acp.StopReasonCancelled}, nil
153: 		}
154: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("coordinate repository prompt: %w", err)
155: 	}
156: 	defer func() {
157: 		if err := unlock(); err != nil {
158: 			slog.Warn("unlock repository prompt failed", "sessionId", sid, "error", err)
159: 		}
160: 	}()
161: 
162: 	if sess.IsClosed() {
163: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("session %s is closed", sid)
164: 	}
165: 	if a.cfg.InjectExecutionEnvNote {
166: 		promptText = applyExecutionEnvNote(promptText, sess.EnvNoteInjected())
167: 		sess.MarkEnvNoteInjected()
168: 	}
169: 	sess.AddUserMessage(promptText)
170: 
171: 	var streamedMu sync.Mutex
172: 	var streamedBuf strings.Builder
173: 	response, err := a.executeTurn(promptCtx, sess, promptText, func(event agy.StreamEvent) {
174: 		update, ok := streamEventUpdate(event)
175: 		if !ok {
176: 			return
177: 		}
178: 		if a.conn == nil {
179: 			return
180: 		}
181: 		if err := a.conn.SessionUpdate(promptCtx, acp.SessionNotification{
182: 			SessionId: params.SessionId,
183: 			Update:    update,
184: 		}); err != nil {
185: 			slog.Warn("send streamed session update failed", "sessionId", sid, "error", err)
186: 			return
187: 		}
188: 
189: 		if event.Kind == agy.StreamEventText {
190: 			streamedMu.Lock()
191: 			streamedBuf.WriteString(event.Text)
192: 			streamedMu.Unlock()
193: 		}
194: 	})
195: 	if err != nil {
196: 		if promptCtx.Err() == context.Canceled {
197: 			return acp.PromptResponse{StopReason: acp.StopReasonCancelled}, nil
198: 		}
199: 		return acp.PromptResponse{}, promptRequestError(err)
200: 	}
201: 
202: 	sess.AddAssistantMessage(response)
203: 
204: 	streamedMu.Lock()
205: 	streamedText := streamedBuf.String()
206: 	streamedMu.Unlock()
207: 
208: 	tail := response
209: 	if streamedText != "" && strings.HasPrefix(response, streamedText) {
210: 		tail = response[len(streamedText):]
211: 	}
212: 
213: 	if tail != "" && a.conn != nil {
214: 		if err := a.conn.SessionUpdate(ctx, acp.SessionNotification{
215: 			SessionId: params.SessionId,
216: 			Update:    acp.UpdateAgentMessageText(tail),
217: 		}); err != nil {
218: 			return acp.PromptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("send session update: %w", err)
219: 		}
220: 	}
221: 
222: 	return acp.PromptResponse{StopReason: acp.StopReasonEndTurn}, nil
223: }

116: class BackendCompletionFlow(IBackendCompletionFlow):
117:     """Orchestrates backend completion requests with failover, retry, and observability.
118: 
119:     This coordinator delegates substantial logic to focused collaborators:
120:     - BackendAvailabilityChecker: Availability gating (disabled backends, resilience checks)
121:     - CompletionSessionResolver: Session resolution and per-session backend selection
122:     - BackendRequestPreparer: Request preparation, config application, and target synchronization
123:     - BackendManager: Backend instance acquisition and lifecycle management
124:     - WireCaptureOrchestrator: Wire capture orchestration (outbound/inbound/errors)
125:     - UsageAccountingOrchestrator: Usage tracking, response wrapping, and accounting
126:     - FailureRecoveryExecutor: Failure recovery, retry, and failover execution
127: 
128:     The orchestrator owns flow ordering and shared context only. All substantial logic
129:     is delegated to collaborators to maintain clear boundaries and improve testability.
130: 
131:     Exception Handling Flow:
132:         Errors are handled in two layers:
133:         1. Inner exception handler: Catches exceptions from backend calls. When allow_failover=False,
134:            calls record_failure() and marks the exception to prevent double-processing.
135:         2. Outer exception handler: Catches exceptions that escape the inner handler (e.g., from
136:            apply_failure_recovery failures, auth failures that raise immediately). Calls record_failure()
137:            unless the exception is already marked as handled.
138: 
139:         The marker pattern (__handled_by_inner_handler__) prevents double-calling record_failure()
140:         when an exception propagates from inner to outer handler. This is necessary because:
141:         - When allow_failover=False, we call record_failure() in the inner handler before raising
142:         - The raised exception is caught by the outer handler
143:         - Without the marker, record_failure() would be called twice for the same error
144: 
145:     Raises:
146:         BackendError: If backend call fails and recovery is not possible
147:         RateLimitExceededError: If backend is rate limited
148:         AuthenticationError: If authentication fails
149:     """
150: 
151:     def __init__(
152:         self,
153:         availability_checker: IBackendAvailabilityChecker,
154:         request_preparer: IBackendRequestPreparer,
155:         session_resolver: ICompletionSessionResolver,
156:         backend_invoker: IBackendInvoker,
157:         failover_executor: IFailureRecoveryExecutor,
158:         wire_capture_orchestrator: IWireCaptureOrchestrator,
159:         usage_accounting_orchestrator: IUsageAccountingOrchestrator,
160:         exception_normalizer: IExceptionNormalizer,
161:         stream_formatting_service: IStreamFormattingService,
162:         connector_invoker: ConnectorInvoker,
163:         resilience_coordinator: IResilienceCoordinator | None = None,
164:         eos_adapter: BackendCompletionFlowEosAdapter | None = None,  # type: ignore[invalid-type-form]
165:         cancellation_coordinator: ISessionCancellationCoordinator | None = None,
166:         backend_work_guard: IBackendWorkGuard | None = None,
167:         non_forwardable_enforcer: INonForwardableMessageEnforcer | None = None,
168:         b2bua_bleg_allocator: B2buaBlegAllocator | None = None,
169:         interleaved_thinking_transformer: (
170:             InterleavedThinkingRequestTransformer | None
171:         ) = None,
172:         interleaved_thinking_output_recorder: (
173:             InterleavedThinkingOutputRecorder | None
174:         ) = None,
175:     ) -> None:
176:         """Initialize the completion flow orchestrator."""
177:         self._availability_checker = availability_checker
178:         self._request_preparer = request_preparer
179:         self._session_resolver = session_resolver
180:         self._backend_invoker = backend_invoker
181:         self._failover_executor = failover_executor
182:         self._wire_capture_orchestrator = wire_capture_orchestrator
183:         self._usage_accounting = usage_accounting_orchestrator
184: 
185:         # Store dependencies needed for local logic
186:         self._resilience = resilience_coordinator
187:         self._exception_normalizer = exception_normalizer
188:         self._stream_formatting_service = stream_formatting_service
189:         self._eos_adapter = eos_adapter  # type: ignore[reportUnknownVariableType]
190:         self._cancellation_coordinator = cancellation_coordinator
191:         self._backend_work_guard = backend_work_guard
192:         self._non_forwardable_enforcer = non_forwardable_enforcer
193:         self._connector_invoker = connector_invoker
194:         self._b2bua_bleg_allocator = b2bua_bleg_allocator
195:         self._interleaved_thinking_transformer = interleaved_thinking_transformer
196:         self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder = (
197:             interleaved_thinking_output_recorder
198:         )
199:         self._parallel_orchestrator = ParallelCompletionOrchestrator()
200:         # Track cancellation tasks to prevent resource leaks
201:         self._cancellation_tasks: set[asyncio.Task[None]] = set()
202:         self._cancellation_tasks_lock = threading.Lock()
203: 
204:     def _attach_resilience_context(
205:         self,
206:         error: Exception,
207:         backend_type: str,
208:         context: RequestContext | None,
209:     ) -> None:
210:         """Attach resilience metadata to the error for handler decisions."""
211:         if getattr(error, "__resilience_context__", None) is not None:
212:             return
213:         error.__resilience_context__ = build_resilience_error_context(  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
214:             backend_type, context
215:         )
216: 
217:     def _record_failure(
218:         self,
219:         backend_type: str,
220:         effective_model: str,
221:         error: Exception,
222:         context: RequestContext | None,
223:     ) -> None:
224:         """Record failure with scoped instance id and attached context."""
225:         if not self._resilience:
226:             return
227:         instance_id = build_resilience_instance_id(backend_type, context)
228:         self._attach_resilience_context(error, backend_type, context)
229:         self._resilience.record_failure(instance_id, effective_model, error)
230: 
231:     def _cancellation_tasks_lock_sync_discard(self, task: asyncio.Task[None]) -> None:
232:         """Synchronous discard for task done callback.
233: 
234:         Thread-safe: Uses the cancellation tasks lock to prevent races with
235:         cleanup operations and other threads.
236:         """
237:         with self._cancellation_tasks_lock:
238:             self._cancellation_tasks.discard(task)
239: 
240:     def _normalize_backend_exception(
241:         self, exc: Exception, backend_type: str
242:     ) -> Exception:
243:         candidate = self._exception_normalizer.normalize(exc, backend_type)
244: 
245:         if isinstance(candidate, LLMProxyError):
246:             # Preserve status_code from original exception if candidate doesn't have one
247:             if (
248:                 not hasattr(candidate, "status_code")
249:                 or getattr(candidate, "status_code", None) is None
250:             ):
251:                 original_status_code = getattr(exc, "status_code", None)
252:                 if isinstance(original_status_code, int):
253:                     candidate.status_code = original_status_code
254:             return candidate
255: 
256:         if isinstance(getattr(candidate, "status_code", None), int) and not isinstance(
257:             candidate, LLMProxyError
258:         ):
259:             # Fallback: ensure framework/transport exceptions (e.g. HTTPException) are
260:             # translated into domain errors even if an injected normalizer is mocked or
261:             # otherwise fails to translate them.
262:             from src.core.services.exception_normalizer import ExceptionNormalizer
263: 
264:             fallback_candidate = ExceptionNormalizer().normalize(
265:                 candidate, backend_type
266:             )
267:             if isinstance(fallback_candidate, LLMProxyError):
268:                 return fallback_candidate
269: 
270:         return candidate
271: 
272:     @staticmethod
273:     def _exception_from_streaming_error_envelope(
274:         result: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
275:     ) -> Exception | None:
276:         """Convert terminal streaming error envelopes back into domain errors."""
277:         status_code = getattr(result, "status_code", None)
278:         if not isinstance(status_code, int) or status_code < 400:
279:             return None
280: 
281:         metadata = getattr(result, "metadata", None)
282:         error_message = f"Backend returned {status_code} error"
283:         error_type = ""
284:         error_code = ""
285:         error_details: dict[str, Any] = {}
286: 
287:         if isinstance(metadata, dict):
288:             raw_message = metadata.get("error_message")
289:             if isinstance(raw_message, str) and raw_message.strip():
290:                 error_message = raw_message
291: 
292:             raw_type = metadata.get("error_type")
293:             if isinstance(raw_type, str):
294:                 error_type = raw_type
295: 
296:             raw_code = metadata.get("error_code")
297:             if isinstance(raw_code, str):
298:                 error_code = raw_code
299: 
300:             raw_details = metadata.get("error_details")
301:             if isinstance(raw_details, dict):
302:                 error_details = dict(raw_details)
303: 
304:         if not error_code and status_code >= 400:
305:             # Preserve the HTTP status in the OpenAI-compatible error payload
306:             # when the upstream stream supplied no provider-specific code.
307:             error_code = str(status_code)
308: 
309:         if status_code == 401:
310:             return AuthenticationError(error_message)
311: 
312:         if status_code == 404 or error_type == "RoutingError":
313:             return RoutingError(
314:                 message=error_message,
315:                 details=error_details,
316:                 code=error_details.get("code") or error_code or "unknown_model",
317:             )
318: 
319:         return BackendError(
320:             message=error_message,
321:             status_code=status_code,
322:             details=error_details,
323:             code=error_code or None,
324:         )
325: 
326:     async def _enforce_non_forwardable_content(
327:         self,
328:         session_id: str,
329:         canonical_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
330:         domain_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
331:         context: RequestContext | None,
332:         backend_type: str,
333:     ) -> tuple[CanonicalChatRequest, CanonicalChatRequest]:
334:         """Apply non-forwardable message filtering."""
335:         if self._non_forwardable_enforcer is None:
336:             return canonical_request, domain_request
337: 
338:         try:
339:             filtered_messages, filtered_count = (
340:                 await self._non_forwardable_enforcer.filter_messages(
341:                     session_id=session_id,
342:                     messages=domain_request.messages,
343:                     context=context,
344:                 )
345:             )
346: 
347:             # Update both canonical_request and domain_request with filtered messages
348:             canonical_request = canonical_request.model_copy(
349:                 update={"messages": filtered_messages}
350:             )
351:             domain_request = domain_request.model_copy(
352:                 update={"messages": filtered_messages}
353:             )
354: 
355:             # Log filtering decision if messages were filtered (requirement 6.1, 6.2)
356:             if filtered_count > 0 and logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
357:                 log_extra = {
358:                     "session_id": session_id,
359:                     "filtered_count": filtered_count,
360:                     "remaining_count": len(filtered_messages),
361:                 }
362:                 # Include request correlation identifier if available (requirement 6.1)
363:                 if context and context.request_id:
364:                     log_extra["request_id"] = context.request_id
365:                 logger.info(
366:                     "Filtered non-forwardable messages from backend request",
367:                     extra=log_extra,
368:                 )
369: 
370:             return canonical_request, domain_request
371: 
372:         except (NoForwardableContentError, NonForwardableEnforcementError) as e:
373:             # Fail closed: do not proceed with backend call
374:             raise BackendError(
375:                 message=f"Non-forwardable message enforcement failed: {e!s}",
376:                 backend_name=backend_type,
377:             ) from e
378: 
379:     async def _handle_streaming_response(
380:         self,
381:         result: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
382:         backend_type: str,
383:         effective_model: str,
384:         context: RequestContext | None,
385:         domain_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
386:         session_id_for_backend: str | None,
387:         session_key: Any | None,
388:     ) -> StreamingResponseEnvelope:
389:         """Handle streaming response with wire capture and session ID injection."""
390:         # Wire-capture: capture inbound stream
391:         key_name = self._wire_capture_orchestrator.detect_key_name(backend_type)
392:         session_id = getattr(context, "session_id", None)
393: 
394:         if result.content is not None:
395:             capture_metadata = self._build_capture_metadata(
396:                 context=context,
397:                 status_code=result.status_code,
398:                 headers=result.headers,
399:                 response_metadata=result.metadata,
400:             )
401: 
402:             # Adapt domain stream to bytes for capture
403:             byte_stream = self._stream_formatting_service.stream_as_sse_bytes(
404:                 result.content
405:             )
406:             wrapped_stream = self._wire_capture_orchestrator.wrap_inbound_stream(
407:                 context=context,
408:                 session_id=session_id,
409:                 backend_type=backend_type,
410:                 effective_model=effective_model,
411:                 key_name=key_name,
412:                 stream=byte_stream,
413:                 capture_metadata=capture_metadata,
414:             )
415: 
416:             # Convert back to ProcessedResponse stream for adapters
417:             async def _to_processed_with_capture() -> Any:
418:                 import json as _json_mod
419: 
420:                 from src.core.interfaces.response_processor_interface import (
421:                     ProcessedResponse,
422:                 )
423: 
424:                 def _split_sse_frames(payload: bytes) -> list[bytes]:
425:                     """Split possibly coalesced SSE payload into frame-sized chunks.
426: 
427:                     Some transports/coordinators may coalesce multiple SSE events into a
428:                     single bytes chunk. Downstream decoders in the response adapter
429:                     expect one SSE frame at a time; passing coalesced payloads can cause
430:                     JSON parsing to fail and produce synthetic empty assistant chunks.
431:                     """
432:                     if not payload:
433:                         return []
434: 
435:                     normalized = payload.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
436:                     if b"\n\n" not in normalized:
437:                         return [payload]
438: 
439:                     frames: list[bytes] = []
440:                     parts = normalized.split(b"\n\n")
441:                     for part in parts:
442:                         if not part.strip():
443:                             continue
444:                         frames.append(part + b"\n\n")
445: 
446:                     return frames or [payload]
447: 
448:                 async for b in wrapped_stream:
449:                     for frame in _split_sse_frames(b):
450:                         # Normalize bytes to ProcessedChunkContent before wrapping
451:                         normalized_content = normalize_to_processed_chunk_content(frame)
452: 
453:                         # Extract metadata from SSE bytes to preserve model info
454:                         extracted_metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}
455:                         if session_id:
456:                             extracted_metadata["session_id"] = session_id
457:                             extracted_metadata["stream_id"] = session_id
458: 
459:                         # Try to parse SSE data and extract metadata
460:                         try:
461:                             text = frame.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
462:                             stripped = text.strip()
463:                             if stripped == "data:" or stripped == "data: ":
464:                                 extracted_metadata["_keepalive"] = True
465:                                 extracted_metadata["model"] = effective_model
466:                             elif stripped.startswith("data: "):
467:                                 json_part = stripped[6:].strip()
468:                                 if json_part and json_part != "[DONE]":
469:                                     try:
470:                                         parsed = _json_mod.loads(json_part)
471:                                         if isinstance(parsed, dict):
472:                                             for key in (
473:                                                 "id",
474:                                                 "model",
475:                                                 "created",
476:                                             ):
477:                                                 if key in parsed:
478:                                                     extracted_metadata[key] = parsed[
479:                                                         key
480:                                                     ]
481:                                     except _json_mod.JSONDecodeError:
482:                                         pass
483:                         except (UnicodeDecodeError, AttributeError):
484:                             pass
485: 
486:                         yield ProcessedResponse(
487:                             content=normalized_content,
488:                             metadata=extracted_metadata,
489:                         )
490: 
491:             result.content = _to_processed_with_capture()  # type: ignore[assignment]
492: 
493:         streaming_result = await self._usage_accounting.handle_streaming_response(
494:             result=result,
495:             backend_type=backend_type,
496:             effective_model=effective_model,
497:             context=context,
498:             request=domain_request,
499:             session_id_for_backend=session_id_for_backend,
500:             key_name=key_name,
501:         )
502: 
503:         # Check cancellation status before returning
504:         if (
505:             self._cancellation_coordinator is not None
506:             and session_key is not None
507:             and self._cancellation_coordinator.is_cancelled(session_key)
508:         ):
509:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
510:                 logger.info(
511:                     "Backend call completed but session was cancelled - "
512:                     "treating streaming result as non-deliverable",
513:                     extra={"session_key": session_key.primary_id},
514:                 )
515:             raise SessionCancelledError(session_key=session_key)
516: 
517:         return streaming_result
518: 
519:     async def _handle_non_streaming_response(
520:         self,
521:         result: ResponseEnvelope,
522:         backend_type: str,
523:         effective_model: str,
524:         context: RequestContext | None,
525:         session_id_for_backend: str | None,
526:         session_key: Any | None,
527:     ) -> ResponseEnvelope:
528:         """Handle non-streaming response with wire capture and usage recording."""
529:         # Wire-capture: capture inbound response
530:         key_name = self._wire_capture_orchestrator.detect_key_name(backend_type)
531:         # Serialize content for capture (best effort)
532:         response_content: Any = result
533:         if hasattr(result, "model_dump") and not isinstance(result, dict):
534:             response_content = result.model_dump()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
535:         elif hasattr(result, "__dict__"):
536:             response_content = result.__dict__
537: 
538:         # Extract canonical usage from envelope if present
539:         canonical_usage_for_capture: CanonicalUsageRecord | None = None
540:         if hasattr(result, "canonical_usage") and result.canonical_usage is not None:
541:             canonical_usage_for_capture = result.canonical_usage
542: 
543:         capture_metadata = self._build_capture_metadata(
544:             context=context,
545:             status_code=result.status_code,
546:             headers=result.headers,
547:             response_metadata=result.metadata,
548:         )
549: 
550:         await self._wire_capture_orchestrator.capture_inbound_response(
551:             context=context,
552:             session_id=getattr(context, "session_id", None),
553:             backend_type=backend_type,
554:             effective_model=effective_model,
555:             key_name=key_name,
556:             response_content=response_content,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownArgumentType]
557:             canonical_usage=canonical_usage_for_capture,
558:             capture_metadata=capture_metadata,
559:         )
560: 
561:         # Check cancellation status before returning
562:         if (
563:             self._cancellation_coordinator is not None
564:             and session_key is not None
565:             and self._cancellation_coordinator.is_cancelled(session_key)
566:         ):
567:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
568:                 logger.info(
569:                     "Backend call completed but session was cancelled - "
570:                     "treating non-streaming result as non-deliverable",
571:                     extra={"session_key": session_key.primary_id},
572:                 )
573:             raise SessionCancelledError(session_key=session_key)
574: 
575:         return await self._usage_accounting.handle_non_streaming_response(
576:             result=result,
577:             backend_type=backend_type,
578:             effective_model=effective_model,
579:             session_id_for_backend=session_id_for_backend,
580:             context=context,
581:         )
582: 
583:     @classmethod
584:     def _strip_client_reasoning_content_from_request(
585:         cls,
586:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
587:         context: RequestContext | None,
588:     ) -> CanonicalChatRequest:
589:         if (
590:             not cls._is_interleaved_composite_attempt(context)
591:             or context is None
592:             or not bool(context.extensions.get(COMPOSITE_SELECTED_LEAF_IS_THINKER_KEY))
593:         ):
594:             return request
595: 
596:         messages: list[ChatMessage] = []
597:         stripped_count = 0
598:         dropped_count = 0
599:         for message in request.messages:
600:             if message.reasoning_content is None:
601:                 messages.append(message)
602:                 continue
603: 
604:             stripped_count += 1
605:             stripped_message = message.model_copy(update={"reasoning_content": None})
606:             if cls._is_empty_reasoning_only_message(stripped_message):
607:                 dropped_count += 1
608:                 continue
609:             messages.append(stripped_message)
610: 
611:         if stripped_count <= 0:
612:             return request
613:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
614:             logger.info(
615:                 "Stripped client-carried reasoning_content from interleaved "
616:                 "backend request: request_id=%s session_id=%s stripped_messages=%d "
617:                 "dropped_empty_messages=%d",
618:                 request_id(context),
619:                 session_id(context, None),
620:                 stripped_count,
621:                 dropped_count,
622:             )
623:         return request.model_copy(update={"messages": messages})
624: 
625:     @staticmethod
626:     def _is_empty_reasoning_only_message(message: ChatMessage) -> bool:
627:         content = message.content
628:         has_content = False
629:         if isinstance(content, str):
630:             has_content = bool(content.strip())
631:         elif content is not None:
632:             has_content = True
633:         return (
634:             not has_content
635:             and not message.tool_calls
636:             and not message.tool_call_id
637:             and not message.name
638:         )
639: 
640:     @staticmethod
641:     def _is_interleaved_composite_attempt(context: RequestContext | None) -> bool:
642:         if context is None:
643:             return False
644:         return COMPOSITE_SELECTED_LEAF_IS_THINKER_KEY in context.extensions
645: 
646:     @staticmethod
647:     def _extract_retry_after(headers: dict[str, str] | None) -> float | None:
648:         if not headers:
649:             return None
650:         value = headers.get("Retry-After") or headers.get("retry-after")
651:         if not value:
652:             return None
653:         try:
654:             return float(value)
655:         except (TypeError, ValueError):
656:             return None
657: 
658:     @staticmethod
659:     def _initialize_retry_metadata(context: RequestContext) -> None:
660:         extensions = context.extensions
661:         if "retry_attempt" not in extensions:
662:             extensions["retry_attempt"] = 0
663:         if "is_retry" not in extensions:
664:             extensions["is_retry"] = False
665: 
666:     @staticmethod
667:     def _set_composite_routing_surface(context: RequestContext) -> None:
668:         surface = resolve_composite_routing_surface(context)
669:         context.extensions[COMPOSITE_ROUTING_SURFACE_KEY] = surface.value
670: 
671:     def _should_record_interleaved_thinking(
672:         self,
673:         context: RequestContext | None,
674:     ) -> bool:
675:         if self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder is None or context is None:
676:             return False
677:         return bool(context.extensions.get(INTERLEAVED_THINKING_ACTIVE_KEY))
678: 
679:     def _should_stream_interleaved_thinking_to_client(
680:         self,
681:         context: RequestContext | None,
682:     ) -> bool:
683:         if not self._should_record_interleaved_thinking(context):
684:             return False
685:         assert self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder is not None
686:         return self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder.stream_to_client
687: 
688:     def _record_interleaved_thinking_streaming_response(
689:         self,
690:         *,
691:         result: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
692:         session: ISession | None,
693:         context: RequestContext | None,
694:         backend_type: str,
695:         effective_model: str,
696:     ) -> StreamingResponseEnvelope:
697:         if not self._should_record_interleaved_thinking(context):
698:             return result
699:         assert self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder is not None
700:         return self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder.wrap_streaming(
701:             response=result,
702:             session=session,
703:             context=context,
704:             backend_type=backend_type,
705:             effective_model=effective_model,
706:         )
707: 
708:     def _record_interleaved_thinking_non_streaming_response(
709:         self,
710:         *,
711:         result: ResponseEnvelope,
712:         session: ISession | None,
713:         context: RequestContext | None,
714:         backend_type: str,
715:         effective_model: str,
716:     ) -> None:
717:         if not self._should_record_interleaved_thinking(context):
718:             return
719:         assert self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder is not None
720:         self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder.capture_non_streaming(
721:             response=result,
722:             session=session,
723:             context=context,
724:             backend_type=backend_type,
725:             effective_model=effective_model,
726:         )
727: 
728:     @staticmethod
729:     async def _drain_streaming_response(result: StreamingResponseEnvelope) -> None:
730:         if result.content is None:
731:             return
732:         async for _item in result.content:
733:             pass
734: 
735:     def _drain_interleaved_thinking_stream_in_background(
736:         self,
737:         result: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
738:         context: RequestContext | None,
739:     ) -> None:
740:         async def _drain() -> None:
741:             await self._drain_streaming_response(result)
742: 
743:         task = asyncio.create_task(_drain())
744: 
745:         def _observe_completion(done: asyncio.Task[None]) -> None:
746:             if done.cancelled():
747:                 return
748:             exception = done.exception()
749:             if exception is None:
750:                 return
751:             logger.warning(
752:                 "Background interleaved thinking stream drain failed: "
753:                 "request_id=%s session_id=%s",
754:                 request_id(context),
755:                 session_id(context, None),
756:                 exc_info=(type(exception), exception, exception.__traceback__),
757:             )
758: 
759:         task.add_done_callback(_observe_completion)
760: 
761:     @staticmethod
762:     def _build_interleaved_thinking_continuation_context(
763:         context: RequestContext | None,
764:         *,
765:         suppress_memo_injection: bool = False,
766:     ) -> RequestContext | None:
767:         if context is None:
768:             return None
769:         continuation = context.with_processing_context()
770:         continuation.extensions.pop(INTERLEAVED_THINKING_ACTIVE_KEY, None)
771:         continuation.extensions.pop(INTERLEAVED_THINKING_DIAGNOSTIC_KEY, None)
772:         continuation.extensions.pop(COMPOSITE_SELECTED_LEAF_SELECTOR_KEY, None)
773:         continuation.extensions[COMPOSITE_SELECTED_LEAF_IS_THINKER_KEY] = False
774:         continuation.extensions[INTERLEAVED_THINKING_SUPPRESS_THINKER_SELECTION_KEY] = (
775:             True
776:         )
777:         continuation.extensions.pop("resolved_uri_params", None)
778:         continuation.extensions.pop(COMPOSITE_ROUTING_STATE_KEY, None)
779:         if suppress_memo_injection:
780:             continuation.extensions[
781:                 INTERLEAVED_THINKING_SUPPRESS_MEMO_INJECTION_KEY
782:             ] = True
783:         return continuation
784: 
785:     async def _continue_after_interleaved_thinking(
786:         self,
787:         *,
788:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
789:         stream: bool,
790:         allow_failover: bool,
791:         context: RequestContext | None,
792:         suppress_memo_injection: bool = False,
793:     ) -> ResponseEnvelope | StreamingResponseEnvelope:
794:         return await self.call_completion(
795:             request=request,
796:             stream=stream,
797:             allow_failover=allow_failover,
798:             context=self._build_interleaved_thinking_continuation_context(
799:                 context,
800:                 suppress_memo_injection=suppress_memo_injection,
801:             ),
802:         )
803: 
804:     @staticmethod
805:     def _append_visible_interleaved_thinking_output(
806:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
807:         memo: str,
808:     ) -> CanonicalChatRequest:
809:         normalized_memo = memo.strip()
810:         if not normalized_memo:
811:             return request
812:         messages = [
813:             *request.messages,
814:             ChatMessage(
815:                 role="assistant",
816:                 content=normalized_memo,
817:                 metadata={
818:                     "source": "interleaved_thinking",
819:                     "kind": "visible_thinker_output",
820:                 },
821:             ),
822:         ]
823:         return request.model_copy(update={"messages": messages})
824: 
825:     async def _build_visible_interleaved_thinking_stream(
826:         self,
827:         *,
828:         thinker_response: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
829:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
830:         stream: bool,
831:         allow_failover: bool,
832:         context: RequestContext | None,
833:     ) -> StreamingResponseEnvelope:
834:         assert self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder is not None
835:         output_recorder = self._interleaved_thinking_output_recorder
836:         queue: asyncio.Queue[Any] = asyncio.Queue()
837:         stream_finished = object()
838: 
839:         async def _produce_stream() -> None:
840:             visible_memo_parts: list[str] = []
841:             try:
842:                 if thinker_response.content is not None:
843:                     async for item in output_recorder.sanitize_visible_stream(
844:                         thinker_response.content,
845:                         as_reasoning_content=True,
846:                     ):
847:                         extracted_text = output_recorder.extract_memo_text(item.content)
848:                         if extracted_text:
849:                             current_memo = "".join(visible_memo_parts)
850:                             if extracted_text != current_memo:
851:                                 visible_memo_parts.append(extracted_text)
852:                         await queue.put(item)
853: 
854:                 continuation_request = self._append_visible_interleaved_thinking_output(
855:                     request,
856:                     "".join(visible_memo_parts),
857:                 )
858:                 continuation = await self._continue_after_interleaved_thinking(
859:                     request=continuation_request,
860:                     stream=stream,
861:                     allow_failover=allow_failover,
862:                     context=context,
863:                     suppress_memo_injection=True,
864:                 )
865:                 if isinstance(continuation, StreamingResponseEnvelope):
866:                     if continuation.content is not None:
867:                         async for item in continuation.content:
868:                             await queue.put(item)
869:                     return
870: 
871:                 from src.core.interfaces.response_processor_interface import (
872:                     ProcessedResponse,
873:                 )
874: 
875:                 await queue.put(
876:                     ProcessedResponse(
877:                         content=continuation.content,
878:                         usage=continuation.usage,
879:                         metadata=dict(continuation.metadata or {}),
880:                     )
881:                 )
882:             except Exception as exc:
883:                 await queue.put(exc)
884:             finally:
885:                 await queue.put(stream_finished)
886: 
887:         producer_task = asyncio.create_task(_produce_stream())
888: 
889:         def _observe_producer_completion(task: asyncio.Task[None]) -> None:
890:             if task.cancelled():
891:                 return
892:             exception = task.exception()
893:             if exception is not None:
894:                 logger.warning(
895:                     "Visible interleaved thinking producer failed unexpectedly",
896:                     exc_info=(type(exception), exception, exception.__traceback__),
897:                 )
898: 
899:         producer_task.add_done_callback(_observe_producer_completion)
900: 
901:         async def _combined_stream() -> AsyncIterator[Any]:
902:             try:
903:                 while True:
904:                     item = await queue.get()
905:                     if item is stream_finished:
906:                         return
907:                     if isinstance(item, Exception):
908:                         raise item
909:                     yield item
910:             finally:
911:                 if not producer_task.done():
912:                     producer_task.cancel()
913:                     with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError, Exception):
914:                         await producer_task
915: 
916:         return StreamingResponseEnvelope(
917:             content=_combined_stream(),
918:             media_type=thinker_response.media_type,
919:             headers=thinker_response.headers,
920:             status_code=thinker_response.status_code,
921:             metadata=dict(thinker_response.metadata or {}),
922:         )
923: 
924:     async def _build_hidden_interleaved_thinking_stream(
925:         self,
926:         *,
927:         thinker_response: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
928:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
929:         stream: bool,
930:         allow_failover: bool,
931:         context: RequestContext | None,
932:     ) -> StreamingResponseEnvelope:
933:         queue: asyncio.Queue[Any] = asyncio.Queue()
934:         stream_finished = object()
935: 
936:         async def _produce_stream() -> None:
937:             try:
938:                 await self._drain_streaming_response(thinker_response)
939:                 continuation = await self._continue_after_interleaved_thinking(
940:                     request=request,
941:                     stream=stream,
942:                     allow_failover=allow_failover,
943:                     context=context,
944:                 )
945:                 if isinstance(continuation, StreamingResponseEnvelope):
946:                     if continuation.content is not None:
947:                         async for item in continuation.content:
948:                             await queue.put(item)
949:                     return
950: 
951:                 from src.core.interfaces.response_processor_interface import (
952:                     ProcessedResponse,
953:                 )
954: 
955:                 await queue.put(
956:                     ProcessedResponse(
957:                         content=continuation.content,
958:                         usage=continuation.usage,
959:                         metadata=dict(continuation.metadata or {}),
960:                     )
961:                 )
962:             except Exception as exc:
963:                 await queue.put(exc)
964:             finally:
965:                 await queue.put(stream_finished)
966: 
967:         producer_task = asyncio.create_task(_produce_stream())
968: 
969:         def _observe_producer_completion(task: asyncio.Task[None]) -> None:
970:             if task.cancelled():
971:                 return
972:             exception = task.exception()
973:             if exception is not None:
974:                 logger.warning(
975:                     "Hidden interleaved thinking producer failed unexpectedly",
976:                     exc_info=(type(exception), exception, exception.__traceback__),
977:                 )
978: 
979:         producer_task.add_done_callback(_observe_producer_completion)
980: 
981:         async def _combined_stream() -> AsyncIterator[Any]:
982:             try:
983:                 while True:
984:                     item = await queue.get()
985:                     if item is stream_finished:
986:                         return
987:                     if isinstance(item, Exception):
988:                         raise item
989:                     yield item
990:             finally:
991:                 if not producer_task.done():
992:                     producer_task.cancel()
993:                     with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError, Exception):
994:                         await producer_task
995: 
996:         return StreamingResponseEnvelope(
997:             content=_combined_stream(),
998:             media_type=thinker_response.media_type,
999:             headers=thinker_response.headers,
1000:             status_code=thinker_response.status_code,
1001:             metadata=dict(thinker_response.metadata or {}),
1002:         )
1003: 
1004:     async def _maybe_continue_after_interleaved_thinking_stream(
1005:         self,
1006:         *,
1007:         handled_streaming_response: StreamingResponseEnvelope,
1008:         attempt_context: RequestContext | None,
1009:         original_client_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
1010:         stream: bool,
1011:         allow_failover: bool,
1012:         context: RequestContext | None,
1013:     ) -> StreamingResponseEnvelope | None:
1014:         if not self._should_record_interleaved_thinking(attempt_context):
1015:             return None
1016:         if self._should_stream_interleaved_thinking_to_client(attempt_context):
1017:             return await self._build_visible_interleaved_thinking_stream(
1018:                 thinker_response=handled_streaming_response,
1019:                 request=original_client_request,
1020:                 stream=stream,
1021:                 allow_failover=allow_failover,
1022:                 context=context,
1023:             )
1024:         return await self._build_hidden_interleaved_thinking_stream(
1025:             thinker_response=handled_streaming_response,
1026:             request=original_client_request,
1027:             stream=stream,
1028:             allow_failover=allow_failover,
1029:             context=context,
1030:         )

1032:     @staticmethod
1033:     async def _single_non_streaming_response_as_stream(
1034:         response: ResponseEnvelope,
1035:     ) -> AsyncIterator[Any]:
1036:         from src.core.interfaces.response_processor_interface import ProcessedResponse
1037: 
1038:         yield ProcessedResponse(
1039:             content=response.content,
1040:             usage=response.usage,
1041:             metadata=dict(response.metadata or {}),
1042:         )
