1044:     @staticmethod
1045:     def _coerce_canonical_request(
1046:         request: ChatRequest,
1047:         context: RequestContext | None,
1048:     ) -> CanonicalChatRequest:
1049:         # BOUNDARY HARDENING: Reject dict input - coercion must happen at adapter boundaries
1050:         if isinstance(request, dict):
1051:             from src.core.common.exceptions import InvalidRequestError
1052: 
1053:             log_boundary_validation_failure(
1054:                 logger=logger,
1055:                 message="BackendCompletionFlow received dict input. "
1056:                 "Dict-to-domain coercion is centralized at adapter boundaries (transport adapters). "
1057:                 "Expected ChatRequest or CanonicalChatRequest.",
1058:                 context=context,
1059:                 service="BackendCompletionFlow",
1060:                 violation_type="dict_input",
1061:                 details={
1062:                     "received_type": "dict",
1063:                     "expected_type": "ChatRequest | CanonicalChatRequest",
1064:                 },
1065:             )
1066: 
1067:             raise InvalidRequestError(
1068:                 message="BackendCompletionFlow received dict input. "
1069:                 "Dict-to-domain coercion is centralized at adapter boundaries (transport adapters). "
1070:                 "Expected ChatRequest or CanonicalChatRequest.",
1071:                 details={
1072:                     "received_type": "dict",
1073:                     "service": "BackendCompletionFlow",
1074:                 },
1075:             )
1076: 
1077:         return (
1078:             request
1079:             if isinstance(request, CanonicalChatRequest)
1080:             else CanonicalChatRequest.model_validate(request.model_dump())
1081:         )
1082: 
1083:     def _build_capture_metadata(
1084:         self,
1085:         *,
1086:         context: RequestContext | None,
1087:         status_code: int,
1088:         headers: dict[str, str] | None,
1089:         response_metadata: dict[str, JsonValue] | None = None,
1090:     ) -> dict[str, JsonValue]:
1091:         capture_metadata: dict[str, JsonValue] = {"status_code": status_code}
1092:         retry_after = self._extract_retry_after(headers)
1093:         if retry_after is not None:
1094:             capture_metadata["retry_after_seconds"] = retry_after
1095: 
1096:         if context is not None:
1097:             for key in (
1098:                 "account_id",
1099:                 "retry_attempt",
1100:                 "is_retry",
1101:                 "compression_correlation_id",
1102:                 "compression_records_count",
1103:             ):
1104:                 if key in context.extensions:
1105:                     capture_metadata[key] = context.extensions[key]
1106: 
1107:         if response_metadata is not None:
1108:             for key in ("account_id", "retry_attempt", "is_retry"):
1109:                 if key in response_metadata:
1110:                     capture_metadata[key] = response_metadata[key]
1111: 
1112:         return capture_metadata
1113: 
1114:     @staticmethod
1115:     def _resolve_b2bua_attempt_reason(context: RequestContext | None) -> str:
1116:         if context is None:
1117:             return "initial"
1118: 
1119:         explicit_reason = context.extensions.get("b2bua_attempt_reason")
1120:         if isinstance(explicit_reason, str) and explicit_reason.strip():
1121:             return explicit_reason.strip()
1122: 
1123:         is_retry = context.extensions.get("is_retry")
1124:         if isinstance(is_retry, bool) and is_retry:
1125:             return "retry"
1126: 
1127:         return "initial"
1128: 
1129:     @staticmethod
1130:     def _is_auxiliary_request(context: RequestContext | None) -> bool:
1131:         if context is None:
1132:             return False
1133:         extensions = getattr(context, "extensions", None)
1134:         if not isinstance(extensions, dict):
1135:             return False
1136:         return bool(extensions.get("auxiliary_request"))
1137: 
1138:     @classmethod
1139:     def _refresh_auxiliary_effective_session_identity(
1140:         cls,
1141:         *,
1142:         context: RequestContext | None,
1143:         backend_type: str,
1144:         effective_model: str,
1145:     ) -> None:
1146:         if context is None:
1147:             return
1148:         extensions = getattr(context, "extensions", None)
1149:         if not isinstance(extensions, dict):
1150:             return
1151:         if not bool(extensions.get("auxiliary_request")):
1152:             return
1153: 
1154:         root_session_id = extensions.get("auxiliary_root_session_id")
1155:         if not isinstance(root_session_id, str) or not root_session_id.strip():
1156:             context_session_id = getattr(context, "session_id", None)
1157:             if isinstance(context_session_id, str) and context_session_id.strip():
1158:                 root_session_id = context_session_id
1159:             else:
1160:                 return
1161:         normalized_root_session_id = root_session_id.strip()
1162: 
1163:         purpose = extensions.get("auxiliary_purpose")
1164:         if not isinstance(purpose, str) or not purpose.strip():
1165:             purpose = f"{backend_type}:{effective_model}"
1166:         normalized_purpose = purpose.strip()
1167: 
1168:         operation_key = extensions.get("auxiliary_operation_key")
1169:         if not isinstance(operation_key, str) or not operation_key.strip():
1170:             operation_key = derive_auxiliary_operation_key(
1171:                 context=context,
1172:                 request_data=None,
1173:                 purpose=normalized_purpose,
1174:             )
1175:         normalized_operation_key = operation_key.strip()
1176: 
1177:         retry_attempt_raw = extensions.get("retry_attempt")
1178:         retry_attempt = 0
1179:         if isinstance(retry_attempt_raw, int):
1180:             retry_attempt = max(0, retry_attempt_raw)
1181:         elif isinstance(retry_attempt_raw, str) and retry_attempt_raw.strip():
1182:             try:
1183:                 retry_attempt = max(0, int(retry_attempt_raw.strip()))
1184:             except ValueError:
1185:                 retry_attempt = 0
1186:         auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = retry_attempt + 1
1187: 
1188:         extensions["auxiliary_root_session_id"] = normalized_root_session_id
1189:         extensions["auxiliary_purpose"] = normalized_purpose
1190:         extensions["auxiliary_operation_key"] = normalized_operation_key
1191:         extensions["auxiliary_attempt_ordinal"] = auxiliary_attempt_ordinal
1192:         extensions["auxiliary_effective_session_id"] = (
1193:             build_auxiliary_effective_session_id(
1194:                 root_session_id=normalized_root_session_id,
1195:                 purpose=normalized_purpose,
1196:                 operation_key=normalized_operation_key,
1197:                 attempt_ordinal=auxiliary_attempt_ordinal,
1198:             )
1199:         )
1200: 
1201:     async def _allocate_b2bua_attempt_context(
1202:         self,
1203:         *,
1204:         context: RequestContext | None,
1205:         a_session_id: str | None,
1206:         backend_type: str,
1207:         effective_model: str,
1208:     ) -> tuple[RequestContext | None, str | None]:
1209:         identity = getattr(context, "b2bua_identity", None) if context else None
1210:         if (
1211:             context is None
1212:             or not isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity)
1213:             or self._b2bua_bleg_allocator is None
1214:         ):
1215:             return context, None
1216:         if self._is_auxiliary_request(context):
1217:             return context, None
1218: 
1219:         resolved_a_session_id = a_session_id or identity.a_session_id
1220:         if not resolved_a_session_id:
1221:             return context, None
1222: 
1223:         try:
1224:             reason = self._resolve_b2bua_attempt_reason(context)
1225:             allocation = await self._b2bua_bleg_allocator.allocate(
1226:                 a_session_id=resolved_a_session_id,
1227:                 backend_type=backend_type,
1228:                 effective_model=effective_model,
1229:                 reason=reason,
1230:             )
1231:             attempt_context = context.with_b2bua_attempt_identity(
1232:                 b_session_id=allocation.b_session_id,
1233:                 b_seq=allocation.seq,
1234:             )
1235:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1236:                 logger.debug(
1237:                     "Allocated B2BUA backend attempt identity",
1238:                     extra={
1239:                         "a_session_id": resolved_a_session_id,
1240:                         "b_session_id": allocation.b_session_id,
1241:                         "b_seq": allocation.seq,
1242:                         "backend_type": backend_type,
1243:                         "effective_model": effective_model,
1244:                         "reason": reason,
1245:                     },
1246:                 )
1247:             return attempt_context, allocation.b_session_id
1248:         except Exception:
1249:             # Fail-open: retain A-leg processing and avoid leaking A-leg id outbound.
1250:             logger.warning(
1251:                 "Failed to allocate B2BUA attempt identity",
1252:                 exc_info=True,
1253:                 extra={
1254:                     "a_session_id": resolved_a_session_id,
1255:                     "backend_type": backend_type,
1256:                     "effective_model": effective_model,
1257:                 },
1258:             )
1259:             return context, None
1260: 
1261:     async def _begin_call_completion(
1262:         self,
1263:         *,
1264:         request: ChatRequest,
1265:         context: RequestContext | None,
1266:         stream: bool,
1267:     ) -> tuple[CanonicalChatRequest, ParallelCompletionResult | None]:
1268:         canonical_request = self._coerce_canonical_request(request, context)
1269:         if context is not None:
1270:             self._initialize_retry_metadata(context)
1271:             self._set_composite_routing_surface(context)
1272:         parallel_result = await self._maybe_execute_parallel_completion(
1273:             request=canonical_request,
1274:             context=context,
1275:             stream=stream,
1276:         )
1277:         return canonical_request, parallel_result
1278: 
1279:     async def _maybe_execute_parallel_completion(
1280:         self,
1281:         *,
1282:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
1283:         context: RequestContext | None,
1284:         stream: bool,
1285:     ) -> ParallelCompletionResult | None:
1286:         if (
1287:             context is not None
1288:             and context.extensions.get(PARALLEL_COMPLETION_ACTIVE_KEY) is True
1289:         ):
1290:             return None
1291: 
1292:         special_route_session = await self._load_special_parallel_cycle_state(
1293:             request=request,
1294:             context=context,
1295:         )
1296:         parse_context = context
1297:         if (
1298:             context is not None
1299:             and special_route_session is None
1300:             and self._is_thinker_parallel_selector(request.model)
1301:         ):
1302:             # Keep special-route cycle mutations ephemeral when no durable session
1303:             # is available to persist them.
1304:             parse_context = context.with_processing_context()
1305: 
1306:         parallel_plan = try_parse_parallel_plan(request, parse_context)
1307:         if parallel_plan is None:
1308:             return None
1309: 
1310:         if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1311:             logger.debug(
1312:                 "Executing parallel composite completion: stream=%s selector=%s",
1313:                 stream,
1314:                 parallel_plan.normalized_selector,
1315:             )
1316: 
1317:         try:
1318:             return await self._execute_parallel_streaming_completion(
1319:                 plan=parallel_plan,
1320:                 request=request,
1321:                 context=context,
1322:                 stream=stream,
1323:             )
1324:         finally:
1325:             self._persist_special_parallel_cycle_state(
1326:                 session=special_route_session,
1327:                 context=context,
1328:             )
1329: 
1330:     async def _load_special_parallel_cycle_state(
1331:         self,
1332:         *,
1333:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
1334:         context: RequestContext | None,
1335:     ) -> ISession | None:
1336:         if context is None or not self._is_thinker_parallel_selector(request.model):
1337:             return None
1338: 
1339:         session, _session_id_for_backend = await self._session_resolver.resolve_session(
1340:             context,
1341:             request,
1342:         )
1343:         if session is None:
1344:             return None
1345: 
1346:         cycle_state = getattr(
1347:             session.state,
1348:             "interleaved_thinking_weighted_cycle_state",
1349:             None,
1350:         )
1351:         if isinstance(cycle_state, dict):
1352:             context.extensions[INTERLEAVED_THINKING_WEIGHTED_CYCLE_STATE_KEY] = cast(
1353:                 JsonValue,
1354:                 cycle_state,
1355:             )
1356:         return session
1357: 
1358:     @staticmethod
1359:     def _is_thinker_parallel_selector(model: str) -> bool:
1360:         selector = model.strip()
1361:         return (
1362:             "[thinker]" in selector
1363:             and contains_top_level_operator(selector, "^")
1364:             and contains_top_level_operator(selector, "!")
1365:         )
1366: 
1367:     @staticmethod
1368:     def _persist_special_parallel_cycle_state(
1369:         *,
1370:         session: ISession | None,
1371:         context: RequestContext | None,
1372:     ) -> None:
1373:         if session is None or context is None:
1374:             return
1375:         raw_cycle_state = context.extensions.get(
1376:             INTERLEAVED_THINKING_WEIGHTED_CYCLE_STATE_KEY
1377:         )
1378:         if not isinstance(raw_cycle_state, dict):
1379:             return
1380:         next_state = session.state.with_interleaved_thinking_weighted_cycle_state(
1381:             cast(dict[str, Any], raw_cycle_state)
1382:         )
1383:         session.update_state(next_state)
1384: 
1385:     async def _execute_parallel_streaming_completion(
1386:         self,
1387:         *,
1388:         plan: CompositeRoutePlan,
1389:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
1390:         context: RequestContext | None,
1391:         stream: bool,
1392:     ) -> ParallelCompletionResult:
1393:         return await self._parallel_orchestrator.execute(
1394:             plan=plan,
1395:             request=request,
1396:             context=context,
1397:             stream=stream,
1398:             call_completion=cast(CallCompletionFn, self.call_completion),
1399:         )
1400: 
1401:     async def call_completion(
1402:         self,
1403:         request: ChatRequest,
1404:         stream: bool = False,
1405:         allow_failover: bool = True,
1406:         context: RequestContext | None = None,
1407:     ) -> ResponseEnvelope | StreamingResponseEnvelope:
1408:         """Execute completion orchestration with failover, retry, and observability.
1409: 
1410:         Boundary Hardening (Requirement 5.2):
1411:             This method enforces typed contract boundaries by rejecting dict inputs.
1412:             Dict-to-contract coercion must occur at explicit adapter boundaries (transport
1413:             adapters) before reaching this core orchestration service. This ensures a
1414:             single canonical representation per concept throughout the core pipeline
1415:             (Requirement 5.3).
1416: 
1417:         Args:
1418:             request: Chat request contract (ChatRequest or CanonicalChatRequest).
1419:                 Dict inputs are rejected with InvalidRequestError.
1420:             stream: Whether to stream the response
1421:             allow_failover: Whether to allow failover to alternative backends
1422:             context: Optional request context for correlation and metadata
1423: 
1424:         Returns:
1425:             Response envelope (streaming or non-streaming) with completion result
1426: 
1427:         Raises:
1428:             InvalidRequestError: If request is a dict. Dict-to-domain coercion is
1429:                 centralized at adapter boundaries (transport adapters). Expected
1430:                 ChatRequest or CanonicalChatRequest.
1431:             BackendError: If backend call fails and recovery is not possible
1432:             RateLimitExceededError: If backend is rate limited
1433:             AuthenticationError: If authentication fails
1434:         """
1435:         canonical_request, parallel_result = await self._begin_call_completion(
1436:             request=request,
1437:             context=context,
1438:             stream=stream,
1439:         )
1440:         if parallel_result is not None:
1441:             return parallel_result
1442:         original_client_request = canonical_request.model_copy(deep=True)
1443: 
1444:         # Step 1: Prepare request (resolve target + synchronize)
1445:         target = await self._request_preparer.prepare_request(
1446:             canonical_request, context
1447:         )
1448:         canonical_request = self._request_preparer.synchronize_request_with_target(
1449:             canonical_request, target
1450:         )
1451:         canonical_request = self._strip_client_reasoning_content_from_request(
1452:             canonical_request,
1453:             context,
1454:         )
1455:         recovery_canonical_request = canonical_request.model_copy(deep=True)
1456:         backend_type = target.backend
1457:         effective_model = target.model
1458:         uri_params = target.uri_params
1459:         self._refresh_auxiliary_effective_session_identity(
1460:             context=context,
1461:             backend_type=backend_type,
1462:             effective_model=effective_model,
1463:         )
1464: 
1465:         # Step 2: Check if complex failover applies
1466:         if allow_failover and await self._failover_executor.check_complex_failover(
1467:             canonical_request, effective_model, backend_type, stream, context
1468:         ):
1469:             # Complex failover handled, return result
1470:             return await self._failover_executor.execute_complex_failover(
1471:                 canonical_request,
1472:                 effective_model,
1473:                 backend_type,
1474:                 stream,
1475:                 self.call_completion,
1476:                 context,
1477:             )
1478: 
1479:         # Step 3: Check backend availability (disabled + resilience)
1480:         try:
1481:             await self._availability_checker.check_backend_availability(
1482:                 backend_type, effective_model, allow_failover, context
1483:             )
1484:         except Exception as exc:
1485:             # For streaming requests, prefer returning a terminal SSE error chunk
1486:             # rather than raising and forcing the transport layer to emit a JSON error.
1487:             # This matches OpenAI streaming behavior more closely and gives clients
1488:             # actionable feedback instead of a generic "connection error".
1489:             if stream:
1490:                 return await self._build_terminal_error_stream_envelope(
1491:                     error=exc, provider=backend_type
1492:                 )
1493:             raise
1494: 
1495:         # Step 4: Initialize failure strategy tracking
1496:         attempt_start_time = time.time()
1497:         recovery_budget = get_or_init_stream_recovery_budget(context)
1498:         budget_start_time = (
1499:             recovery_budget.budget_start_time
1500:             if recovery_budget is not None
1501:             else attempt_start_time
1502:         )
1503:         attempted_backends: list[str] = (
1504:             recovery_budget.attempted_backends if recovery_budget is not None else []
1505:         )
1506:         current_backend = backend_type
1507:         content_started = (
1508:             bool(context.extensions.get("meaningful_output_emitted"))
1509:             if context is not None
1510:             else False
1511:         )
1512:         session_id_for_backend: str | None = None
1513:         outbound_session_id: str | None = None
1514:         attempt_context: RequestContext | None = context
1515: 
1516:         try:
1517: 
1518:             # Step 5: Resolve session
1519:             (
1520:                 session,
1521:                 session_id_for_backend,
1522:             ) = await self._session_resolver.resolve_session(context, canonical_request)
1523: 
1524:             # Ensure session_id is non-empty (Requirement 8.1)
1525:             if not session_id_for_backend:
1526:                 session_id_for_backend = str(uuid4())
1527:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
1528:                     logger.debug(
1529:                         "Generated new session ID for backend call: %s",
1530:                         session_id_for_backend,
1531:                     )
1532: 
1533:             # Synchronize session_id back to context if it's missing (Requirement 8.1)
1534:             if context and not context.session_id:
1535:                 context.session_id = session_id_for_backend
1536: 
1537:             # In B2BUA mode allocate per-attempt B-leg identity for outbound correlation
1538:             (
1539:                 attempt_context,
1540:                 outbound_session_id,
1541:             ) = await self._allocate_b2bua_attempt_context(
1542:                 context=context,
1543:                 a_session_id=session_id_for_backend,
1544:                 backend_type=backend_type,
1545:                 effective_model=effective_model,
1546:             )
1547:             if self._is_auxiliary_request(context):
1548:                 outbound_session_id = session_id_for_backend
1549:             elif not isinstance(
1550:                 getattr(context, "b2bua_identity", None), B2buaIdentity
1551:             ):
1552:                 # Legacy mode keeps the same outbound correlation semantics.
1553:                 outbound_session_id = session_id_for_backend
1554: 
1555:             attempt_identity = (
1556:                 getattr(attempt_context, "b2bua_identity", None)
1557:                 if attempt_context is not None
1558:                 else None
1559:             )
1560:             if isinstance(attempt_identity, B2buaIdentity) and logger.isEnabledFor(
1561:                 logging.INFO
1562:             ):
1563:                 logger.info(
1564:                     "Dispatching backend attempt",
1565:                     extra={
1566:                         "request_id": getattr(attempt_context, "request_id", None),
1567:                         "backend_type": backend_type,
1568:                         "effective_model": effective_model,
1569:                         "a_session_id": attempt_identity.a_session_id,
1570:                         "b_session_id": attempt_identity.b_session_id,
1571:                         "b_seq": attempt_identity.b_seq,
1572:                     },
1573:                 )
1574: 
1575:             # Step 6: Acquire backend instance
1576:             backend = await self._backend_invoker.acquire_backend(
1577:                 backend_type, session_id_for_backend
1578:             )
1579: 
1580:             # Step 7: Prepare backend request (configs + URI params)
1581:             domain_request = await self._request_preparer.prepare_backend_request(
1582:                 canonical_request, backend_type, session, uri_params
1583:             )
1584: 
1585:             if self._interleaved_thinking_transformer is not None:
1586:                 domain_request = self._interleaved_thinking_transformer.transform(
1587:                     request=domain_request,
1588:                     target=target,
1589:                     session=session,
1590:                     context=attempt_context,
1591:                 )
1592:                 canonical_request = domain_request
1593: 
1594:             # Preserve original canonical_request for verbatim token calculation
1595:             # (before non-forwardable filtering modifies it)
1596:             # Make an explicit copy to ensure it's not accidentally modified
1597:             original_canonical_request = canonical_request.model_copy()
1598: 
1599:             # Step 7.5: Apply non-forwardable message filtering
1600:             # This happens before wire capture
1601:             # to ensure filtered messages are used for both capture and backend calls
1602:             (
1603:                 canonical_request,
1604:                 domain_request,
1605:             ) = await self._enforce_non_forwardable_content(
1606:                 session_id=session_id_for_backend,
1607:                 canonical_request=canonical_request,
1608:                 domain_request=domain_request,
1609:                 context=attempt_context,
1610:                 backend_type=backend_type,
1611:             )
1612: 
1613:             # Step 8: Prepare wire capture context (identity + backend config)
1614:             identity = (
1615:                 await self._wire_capture_orchestrator.prepare_wire_capture_context(
1616:                     backend_type, session
1617:                 )
1618:             )
1619: 
1620:             # Step 9: Execute backend call (with wire capture + usage tracking)
1621:             result: ResponseEnvelope | StreamingResponseEnvelope | None = None
1622:             try:
1623:                 # Wire-capture: capture outbound payload pre-call (best-effort)
1624:                 await self._wire_capture_orchestrator.capture_wire_outbound(
1625:                     backend_type=backend_type,
1626:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1627:                     domain_request=domain_request,
1628:                     context=attempt_context,
1629:                 )
1630: 
1631:                 # Resolve SessionKey and enforce cancellation before backend call.
1632:                 if self._backend_work_guard is not None:
1633:                     session_key = self._backend_work_guard.ensure_session_active(
1634:                         context=attempt_context,
1635:                         purpose="primary_completion",
1636:                         require_scope=False,
1637:                     )
1638:                 else:
1639:                     session_key = resolve_session_key_from_request_context(
1640:                         attempt_context
1641:                     )
1642:                     if (
1643:                         self._cancellation_coordinator is not None
1644:                         and session_key is not None
1645:                     ):
1646:                         self._cancellation_coordinator.ensure_not_cancelled(session_key)
1647: 
1648:                 # Prepare backend call kwargs
1649:                 backend_call_kwargs = self._request_preparer.prepare_backend_kwargs(
1650:                     session_id_for_backend=outbound_session_id,
1651:                     session=session,
1652:                     context=attempt_context,
1653:                     backend_type=backend_type,
1654:                 )
1655: 
1656:                 # Calculate outbound tokens and record usage
1657:                 # Use original_canonical_request for verbatim token calculation
1658:                 # (before non-forwardable filtering was applied)
1659:                 (
1660:                     outbound_tokens,
1661:                     ctp_record_id,
1662:                     ptb_record_id,
1663:                 ) = await self._usage_accounting.calculate_and_record_usage(
1664:                     domain_request=domain_request,
1665:                     request=original_canonical_request,
1666:                     backend_type=backend_type,
1667:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1668:                     session=session,
1669:                     session_id_for_backend=session_id_for_backend,
1670:                     context=attempt_context,
1671:                 )
1672: 
1673:                 # Execute the backend call through ConnectorInvoker
1674:                 result = await self._connector_invoker.invoke(
1675:                     backend=backend,
1676:                     domain_request=domain_request,
1677:                     canonical_request=canonical_request,
1678:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1679:                     identity=identity,
1680:                     cancellation_token=session_key,
1681:                     cancellation_coordinator=self._cancellation_coordinator,
1682:                     context=attempt_context,
1683:                     options=backend_call_kwargs,
1684:                 )
1685: 
1686:                 if (
1687:                     self._backend_work_guard is not None
1688:                     and isinstance(result, StreamingResponseEnvelope)
1689:                     and result.content is not None
1690:                 ):
1691:                     result.content = (
1692:                         self._backend_work_guard.wrap_stream_with_cancellation(
1693:                             stream=result.content,
1694:                             session_key=session_key,
1695:                             purpose="primary_completion",
1696:                         )
1697:                     )
1698: 
1699:                 # Register cancellable work if coordinator and session_key are available
1700:                 if (
1701:                     self._cancellation_coordinator is not None
1702:                     and session_key is not None
1703:                 ):
1704:                     from src.core.interfaces.session_cancellation_coordinator_interface import (
1705:                         ICancellable,
1706:                     )
1707: 
1708:                     # Create cancellable wrapper for streaming responses
1709:                     if (
1710:                         isinstance(result, StreamingResponseEnvelope)
1711:                         and result.cancel_callback is not None
1712:                     ):
1713: 
1714:                         outer = self
1715: 
1716:                         class StreamingCancellable:
1717:                             """Cancellable wrapper for streaming backend work."""
1718: 
1719:                             def __init__(
1720:                                 self,
1721:                                 cancel_callback: Callable[[], Awaitable[None]],
1722:                             ):
1723:                                 self._cancel_callback = cancel_callback
1724:                                 self._cancelled: bool = False
1725: 
1726:                             def cancel(self) -> None:
1727:                                 """Cancel streaming backend work."""
1728:                                 if not self._cancelled:
1729:                                     self._cancelled = True
1730:                                     # Schedule cancellation callback execution
1731:                                     import contextlib
1732: 
1733:                                     # Suppress RuntimeError when no event loop exists (intentionally silent control flow)
1734:                                     with contextlib.suppress(RuntimeError):
1735:                                         loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
1736:                                         # Call cancel_callback to get coroutine, then create task and track it
1737:                                         coro = self._cancel_callback()
1738:                                         # Type cast: cancel_callback returns Awaitable[None] but create_task expects Coroutine
1739:                                         # In practice, create_task accepts coroutines which are a subtype of Awaitable
1740:                                         coroutine: Coroutine[Any, Any, None] = cast(
1741:                                             Coroutine[Any, Any, None], coro
1742:                                         )
1743:                                         task: asyncio.Task[None] = loop.create_task(
1744:                                             coroutine
1745:                                         )
1746:                                         # Track task to prevent resource leaks
1747:                                         with outer._cancellation_tasks_lock:
1748:                                             outer._cancellation_tasks.add(task)
1749:                                         # Remove task from set when done to prevent unbounded growth
1750:                                         task.add_done_callback(
1751:                                             outer._cancellation_tasks_lock_sync_discard
1752:                                         )
1753: 
1754:                         cancellable: ICancellable = StreamingCancellable(
1755:                             result.cancel_callback,
1756:                         )
1757:                         self._cancellation_coordinator.register_cancellable(
1758:                             session_key, cancellable
1759:                         )
1760:                     # For non-streaming responses, HTTP calls are typically fast
1761:                     # and gating prevents new calls, so we skip registration
1762: 
1763:                 # Wrap result for usage tracking
1764:                 assert result is not None, "Backend call must return a response"
1765:                 result = await self._usage_accounting.wrap_response_for_usage(
1766:                     result=result,
1767:                     outbound_tokens=outbound_tokens,
1768:                     ctp_record_id=ctp_record_id,
1769:                     ptb_record_id=ptb_record_id,
1770:                     start_time=attempt_start_time,
1771:                     context=attempt_context,
1772:                     backend_type=backend_type,
1773:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1774:                 )
1775: 
1776:                 # Step 10: Handle streaming response (wire capture + session ID injection)
1777:                 if isinstance(result, StreamingResponseEnvelope):
1778:                     if allow_failover:
1779:                         streaming_error = self._exception_from_streaming_error_envelope(
1780:                             result
1781:                         )
1782:                         if streaming_error is not None:
1783:                             raise streaming_error
1784:                     result = self._record_interleaved_thinking_streaming_response(
1785:                         result=result,
1786:                         session=session,
1787:                         context=attempt_context,
1788:                         backend_type=backend_type,
1789:                         effective_model=effective_model,
1790:                     )
1791:                     handled_streaming_response = await self._handle_streaming_response(
1792:                         result=result,
1793:                         backend_type=backend_type,
1794:                         effective_model=effective_model,
1795:                         context=attempt_context,
1796:                         domain_request=domain_request,
1797:                         session_id_for_backend=session_id_for_backend,
1798:                         session_key=session_key,
1799:                     )
1800:                     interleaved_continuation = (
1801:                         await self._maybe_continue_after_interleaved_thinking_stream(
1802:                             handled_streaming_response=handled_streaming_response,
1803:                             attempt_context=attempt_context,
1804:                             original_client_request=original_client_request,
1805:                             stream=stream,
1806:                             allow_failover=allow_failover,
1807:                             context=context,
1808:                         )
1809:                     )
1810:                     if interleaved_continuation is not None:
1811:                         return interleaved_continuation
1812:                     return handled_streaming_response
1813: 
1814:                 # Step 11: Handle non-streaming response
1815:                 # Wire-capture: capture inbound response
1816:                 self._record_interleaved_thinking_non_streaming_response(
1817:                     result=result,
1818:                     session=session,
1819:                     context=attempt_context,
1820:                     backend_type=backend_type,
1821:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1822:                 )
1823:                 handled_non_streaming_response = (
1824:                     await self._handle_non_streaming_response(
1825:                         result=result,
1826:                         backend_type=backend_type,
1827:                         effective_model=effective_model,
1828:                         context=attempt_context,
1829:                         session_id_for_backend=session_id_for_backend,
1830:                         session_key=session_key,
1831:                     )
1832:                 )
1833:                 if self._should_record_interleaved_thinking(attempt_context):
1834:                     return await self._continue_after_interleaved_thinking(
1835:                         request=original_client_request,
1836:                         stream=stream,
1837:                         allow_failover=allow_failover,
1838:                         context=context,
1839:                     )
1840:                 return handled_non_streaming_response
1841: 
1842:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
1843:                 raise
1844:             except SessionCancelledError:
1845:                 # Preserve SessionCancelledError - do not normalize
1846:                 raise
1847:             except Exception as call_exc:
1848:                 # Normalize the exception immediately for consistent handling
1849:                 normalized_exc = self._normalize_backend_exception(
1850:                     call_exc, backend_type
1851:                 )
1852: 
1853:                 # Check if this is an authentication failure first
1854:                 is_auth_failure = False
1855:                 if isinstance(normalized_exc, AuthenticationError) or (
1856:                     hasattr(normalized_exc, "status_code")
1857:                     and getattr(normalized_exc, "status_code", None) == 401
1858:                 ):
1859:                     is_auth_failure = True
1860: 
1861:                 if is_auth_failure:
1862:                     # Handle authentication failures with backend lifecycle side effects
1863:                     await self._usage_accounting.handle_auth_failure(
1864:                         normalized_exc,
1865:                         backend,
1866:                         backend_type,
1867:                         session_id_for_backend,
1868:                     )
1869:                     raise normalized_exc
1870: 
1871:                 # Handle backend error (wire capture + usage/resilience updates)
1872:                 # 1. Wire capture error
1873:                 key_name = self._wire_capture_orchestrator.detect_key_name(backend_type)
1874:                 error_payload: dict[str, Any]
1875:                 if isinstance(normalized_exc, LLMProxyError):
1876:                     error_payload = normalized_exc.to_dict()
1877:                     # Ensure status code is present if available
1878:                     import contextlib
1879: 
1880:                     with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
1881:                         if (
1882:                             isinstance(error_payload.get("error"), dict)
1883:                             and "status_code" not in error_payload["error"]
1884:                         ):
1885:                             error_payload["error"]["status_code"] = getattr(
1886:                                 normalized_exc, "status_code", None
1887:                             )
1888:                 else:
1889:                     error_payload = {
1890:                         "error": {
1891:                             "message": str(normalized_exc),
1892:                             "type": type(normalized_exc).__name__,
1893:                         }
1894:                     }
1895: 
1896:                 # Extract canonical usage from envelope if present (may be None for errors)
1897:                 canonical_usage_for_error: CanonicalUsageRecord | None = None
1898:                 if (
1899:                     result is not None
1900:                     and isinstance(result, ResponseEnvelope)
1901:                     and result.canonical_usage is not None
1902:                 ):
1903:                     canonical_usage_for_error = result.canonical_usage
1904:                 # Note: For error cases, canonical_usage may not be available
1905: 
1906:                 await self._wire_capture_orchestrator.capture_inbound_response(
1907:                     context=attempt_context,
1908:                     session_id=getattr(attempt_context, "session_id", None),
1909:                     backend_type=backend_type,
1910:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1911:                     key_name=key_name,
1912:                     response_content=error_payload,
1913:                     canonical_usage=canonical_usage_for_error,
1914:                 )
1915: 
1916:                 # 2. Update resilience/usage via accounting collaborator
1917:                 await self._usage_accounting.handle_backend_error(
1918:                     call_exc=call_exc,
1919:                     backend_type=current_backend,
1920:                     effective_model=effective_model,
1921:                     context=context,
1922:                     request=canonical_request,
1923:                     backend=backend,
1924:                     normalized_exc=normalized_exc,
1925:                 )
1926: 
1927:                 # Record each failed attempt so routing/availability state is updated
1928:                 # even when failover succeeds and no error escapes this call.
1929:                 if self._resilience and not getattr(
1930:                     normalized_exc, "__handled_by_inner_handler__", False
1931:                 ):
1932:                     self._record_failure(
1933:                         current_backend, effective_model, normalized_exc, context
1934:                     )
1935:                     normalized_exc.__handled_by_inner_handler__ = True  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
1936: 
1937:                 # 3. Record EoS error termination signal (fail-open)
1938:                 if self._eos_adapter is not None:  # type: ignore[reportUnknownVariableType]
1939:                     try:
1940:                         await self._eos_adapter.record_error_termination(  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
1941:                             error=normalized_exc,
1942:                             session_id=session_id_for_backend,
1943:                             backend_type=current_backend,
1944:                             context=context,
1945:                         )
1946:                         # Mark as handled to prevent outer handler from calling record_error_termination again.
1947:                         # This marker is necessary because when allow_failover=False, we call
1948:                         # record_error_termination here and then raise, which will be caught by the outer
1949:                         # handler. Without this marker, record_error_termination would be called twice for
1950:                         # the same error.
1951:                         normalized_exc.__eos_recorded_by_inner_handler__ = True  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
1952:                     except Exception as eos_error:
1953:                         # Fail-open: log but don't interfere with error handling
1954:                         logger.warning(
1955:                             "Failed to record EoS error termination: %s",
1956:                             eos_error,
1957:                             exc_info=True,
1958:                         )
1959: 
1960:                 # Step 13: Apply failure recovery (retry/failover)
1961:                 if allow_failover:
1962:                     return await self._failover_executor.apply_failure_recovery(
1963:                         error=normalized_exc,
1964:                         model=effective_model,
1965:                         backend_type=current_backend,
1966:                         attempted_backends=attempted_backends,
1967:                         start_time=budget_start_time,
1968:                         is_streaming=stream,
1969:                         content_started=content_started,
1970:                         request=recovery_canonical_request,
1971:                         context=context,
1972:                         call_completion_callback=self.call_completion,
1973:                     )
1974: 
1975:                 # No failover allowed - record failure and raise the normalized error
1976:                 if stream:
1977:                     return await self._build_terminal_error_stream_envelope(
1978:                         error=normalized_exc,
1979:                         provider=current_backend,
1980:                     )
1981:                 raise normalized_exc
1982: 
1983:         except asyncio.CancelledError:
1984:             raise
1985:         except SessionCancelledError:
1986:             # Preserve SessionCancelledError - do not normalize
1987:             raise
1988:         except (
1989:             BackendError,
1990:             RateLimitExceededError,
1991:             LLMProxyError,
1992:             AuthenticationError,
1993:         ) as exc:
1994:             # Record failure in resilience coordinator (handles cooldown/backoff).
1995:             # This outer handler catches exceptions that escape the inner handler:
1996:             # 1. Exceptions from apply_failure_recovery when allow_failover=True
1997:             # 2. Exceptions from auth failure handling (which raises immediately)
1998:             # 3. Unexpected exceptions that bypass the inner handler
1999:             # We skip if already handled in inner handler (when allow_failover=False)
2000:             # to avoid double-calling record_failure for the same error.

3: This module provides lightweight helpers that mirror the behaviour of the
4: project's richer pydantic models while remaining dependency free for tests and
5: utilities. Only the pieces required by the updated graceful-degradation logic
6: are implemented here.

23: class RequestHeaders(dict[str, str]):
24:     """Case-insensitive mapping for HTTP headers."""
25: 
26:     def __init__(self, raw: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
27:         normalized: dict[str, str] = {}
28:         if raw:
29:             for key, value in raw.items():
30:                 normalized[str(key).lower()] = str(value)
31:         super().__init__(normalized)
32: 
33:     def get(self, key: str, default: str | None = None) -> str | None:  # type: ignore[override]
34:         return super().get(key.lower(), default)
35: 
36:     def contains(self, key: str) -> bool:
37:         return key.lower() in self
38: 
39:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
40:         return dict(self)
41:

60: @dataclass
61: class ContentModificationTracker:
62:     """Tracks content modifications for accurate usage calculation.
63: 
64:     This tracker monitors whether content has been modified during proxy processing,
65:     enabling accurate token recalculation for usage reporting.
66:     """
67: 
68:     # Inbound (request) modification tracking
69:     inbound_modified: bool = False
70:     inbound_original_tokens: int | None = None
71:     inbound_modified_tokens: int | None = None
72:     inbound_modification_reasons: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
73: 
74:     # Outbound (response) modification tracking
75:     outbound_modified: bool = False
76:     outbound_original_tokens: int | None = None
77:     outbound_modified_tokens: int | None = None
78:     outbound_modification_reasons: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
79: 
80:     def mark_inbound_modified(
81:         self,
82:         reason: str,
83:         original_tokens: int | None = None,
84:         modified_tokens: int | None = None,
85:     ) -> None:
86:         """Mark that inbound (request) content was modified."""
87:         self.inbound_modified = True
88:         if reason and reason not in self.inbound_modification_reasons:
89:             self.inbound_modification_reasons.append(reason)
90:         if original_tokens is not None:
91:             self.inbound_original_tokens = original_tokens
92:         if modified_tokens is not None:
93:             self.inbound_modified_tokens = modified_tokens
94: 
95:     def mark_outbound_modified(
96:         self,
97:         reason: str,
98:         original_tokens: int | None = None,
99:         modified_tokens: int | None = None,
100:     ) -> None:
101:         """Mark that outbound (response) content was modified."""
102:         self.outbound_modified = True
103:         if reason and reason not in self.outbound_modification_reasons:
104:             self.outbound_modification_reasons.append(reason)
105:         if original_tokens is not None:
106:             self.outbound_original_tokens = original_tokens
107:         if modified_tokens is not None:
108:             self.outbound_modified_tokens = modified_tokens
109: 
110:     def requires_usage_recalculation(self) -> bool:
111:         """Check if usage should be recalculated due to modifications."""
112:         return self.inbound_modified or self.outbound_modified
113: 
114:     def get_modification_summary(self) -> dict[str, JsonValue]:
115:         """Get a summary of all modifications for logging/debugging."""
116:         return {
117:             "inbound_modified": self.inbound_modified,
118:             "inbound_reasons": list[JsonValue](self.inbound_modification_reasons),
119:             "inbound_token_delta": (
120:                 (self.inbound_modified_tokens - self.inbound_original_tokens)
121:                 if self.inbound_original_tokens is not None
122:                 and self.inbound_modified_tokens is not None
123:                 else None
124:             ),
125:             "outbound_modified": self.outbound_modified,
126:             "outbound_reasons": list[JsonValue](self.outbound_modification_reasons),
127:             "outbound_token_delta": (
128:                 (self.outbound_modified_tokens - self.outbound_original_tokens)
129:                 if self.outbound_original_tokens is not None
130:                 and self.outbound_modified_tokens is not None
131:                 else None
132:             ),
133:         }
134:

139:

142:         default_factory=ContentModificationTracker
143:     )
144: 
145:     def update(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
146:         self.values.update(data)
147: 
148:     def mark_inbound_modified(
149:         self,
150:         reason: str,
151:         original_tokens: int | None = None,
152:         modified_tokens: int | None = None,
153:     ) -> None:
154:         """Convenience method to mark inbound modification."""
155:         self.modification_tracker.mark_inbound_modified(
156:             reason, original_tokens, modified_tokens
157:         )

160:         self,
161:         reason: str,
162:         original_tokens: int | None = None,
163:         modified_tokens: int | None = None,
164:     ) -> None:

171: @dataclass
172: class RequestContext(InternalDTO):
173:     """Transport-agnostic request context used by core services."""
174: 
175:     headers: RequestHeaders | Mapping[str, Any]
176:     cookies: RequestCookies | Mapping[str, Any]
177:     state: Any
178:     app_state: Any
179:     client_host: str | None = None
180:     session_id: str | None = None
181:     request_id: str | None = None
182:     agent: str | None = None
183:     original_request: Any | None = None
184:     processing_context: ProcessingContext | None = None
185:     # Explicit typed fields for cross-layer data exchange
186:     domain_request: CanonicalChatRequest | None = None
187:     raw_body: bytes | None = None
188:     backend: str | None = None
189:     effective_model: str | None = None
190:     requested_model: str | None = None
191:     extensions: dict[str, JsonValue] = field(default_factory=dict)
192:     b2bua_identity: B2buaIdentity | None = None
193:     """
194:     Extension container for vendor- and protocol-specific data.
195:     
196:     This is the single, explicitly named extension container permitted to remain
197:     open-ended for cross-layer data exchange. All values must be JSON-serializable
198:     (JsonValue: str | int | float | bool | None | list[JsonValue] | dict[str, JsonValue]).
199:     
200:     Extension fields should be used sparingly and only when:
201:     - The data is vendor/protocol-specific and not part of the canonical contract
202:     - The data is needed across layers but doesn't warrant a first-class typed field
203:     
204:     Frequently-used extension keys should be promoted to first-class typed fields
205:     in RequestContext or domain models when they become stable.
206:     """
207:     # Provenance tracking: store original contract values for debugging/accounting
208:     original_domain_request: CanonicalChatRequest | None = None
209:     """Original domain request before any mutations (copy-on-write provenance).
210:     
211:     This field stores the original contract instance before any transformations
212:     are applied. It enables debugging and accounting by providing access to
213:     the original values even after mutations occur.
214:     
215:     Requirement 5.3: When modifications occur, the LLM Proxy shall retain
216:     provenance sufficient for debugging and accounting, including the reason
217:     for modification and the ability to access the original value.
218:     """
219: 
220:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
221:         if not isinstance(self.headers, RequestHeaders):
222:             self.headers = RequestHeaders(self.headers)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
223:         if not isinstance(self.cookies, RequestCookies):
224:             self.cookies = RequestCookies(self.cookies)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
225:         if self.processing_context is not None and not isinstance(
226:             self.processing_context, ProcessingContext
227:         ):  # type: ignore[reportUnnecessaryIsInstance]
228:             self.processing_context = ProcessingContext(
229:                 values=dict(self.processing_context)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
230:             )
231: 
232:     def get_header(self, key: str, default: str | None = None) -> str | None:
233:         return self.headers.get(key, default)
234: 
235:     def get_cookie(self, key: str, default: str | None = None) -> str | None:
236:         return self.cookies.get(key, default)
237: 
238:     def with_processing_context(self, **kwargs: Any) -> RequestContext:
239:         new_context = (
240:             ProcessingContext(
241:                 values=dict(self.processing_context.values),
242:                 modification_tracker=self.processing_context.modification_tracker,
243:             )
244:             if self.processing_context
245:             else ProcessingContext(values={})
246:         )
247:         new_context.update(kwargs)
248:         return RequestContext(
249:             headers=RequestHeaders(self.headers),
250:             cookies=RequestCookies(self.cookies),
251:             state=self.state,
252:             app_state=self.app_state,
253:             client_host=self.client_host,
254:             session_id=self.session_id,
255:             request_id=self.request_id,
256:             b2bua_identity=copy.deepcopy(self.b2bua_identity),
257:             agent=self.agent,
258:             original_request=self.original_request,
259:             processing_context=new_context,
260:             domain_request=self.domain_request,
261:             raw_body=self.raw_body,
262:             backend=self.backend,
263:             effective_model=self.effective_model,
264:             requested_model=self.requested_model,
265:             extensions=copy.deepcopy(self.extensions) if self.extensions else {},
266:             original_domain_request=self.original_domain_request,
267:         )
268: 
269:     def with_b2bua_attempt_identity(
270:         self,
271:         *,
272:         b_session_id: str,
273:         b_seq: int | None,
274:     ) -> RequestContext:
275:         """Return an attempt-scoped copy with updated B-leg identity.
276: 
277:         The original context remains unchanged so per-attempt B-leg metadata can
278:         be represented without mutating shared request state.
279:         """
280:         if self.b2bua_identity is None:
281:             raise ValueError(
282:                 "b2bua_identity must be set before creating attempt-scoped context"
283:             )
284: 
285:         return RequestContext(
286:             headers=RequestHeaders(self.headers),
287:             cookies=RequestCookies(self.cookies),
288:             state=self.state,
289:             app_state=self.app_state,
290:             client_host=self.client_host,
291:             session_id=self.session_id,
292:             request_id=self.request_id,
293:             b2bua_identity=self.b2bua_identity.with_attempt(
294:                 b_session_id=b_session_id,
295:                 b_seq=b_seq,
296:             ),
297:             agent=self.agent,
298:             original_request=self.original_request,
299:             processing_context=copy.deepcopy(self.processing_context),
300:             domain_request=self.domain_request,
301:             raw_body=self.raw_body,
302:             backend=self.backend,
303:             effective_model=self.effective_model,
304:             requested_model=self.requested_model,
305:             extensions=copy.deepcopy(self.extensions) if self.extensions else {},
306:             original_domain_request=self.original_domain_request,
307:         )
308: 
309:     def ensure_processing_context(self) -> ProcessingContext:
310:         """Ensure processing context exists and return it."""
311:         if self.processing_context is None:
312:             self.processing_context = ProcessingContext()
313:         return self.processing_context

343:         if self.processing_context is None:

347:         )

37: class ConnectorInvoker:
38:     """Service for invoking connectors with canonical-first dispatch.
39: 
40:     This invoker builds canonical connector request contracts, projects
41:     RequestContext to ConnectorRequestContext, and dispatches to the canonical
42:     API when available, falling back to the legacy API with typed domain models.
43: 
44:     The invoker ensures that:
45:     - Canonical backends receive ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest contracts
46:     - Legacy backends receive canonical domain models (never dicts)
47:     - Options are expanded into kwargs only for legacy path
48:     - Legacy path usage is logged for observability
49:     """
50: 
51:     def _project_context(
52:         self, context: RequestContext | None
53:     ) -> ConnectorRequestContext | None:
54:         """Project RequestContext to ConnectorRequestContext.
55: 
56:         Performs a shallow, JSON-safe mapping of stable, transport-agnostic
57:         data needed for logging/diagnostics/correlation.
58: 
59:         Args:
60:             context: Request context to project, or None
61: 
62:         Returns:
63:             ConnectorRequestContext with projected fields, or None if context is None
64:         """
65:         if context is None:
66:             return None
67: 
68:         # Deep copy extensions dict to avoid shared mutable state
69:         extensions = copy.deepcopy(context.extensions) if context.extensions else {}
70:         for sensitive_key in ("client_session_id", "a_session_id", "auth_scope_id"):
71:             extensions.pop(sensitive_key, None)
72: 
73:         b2bua_extension = extensions.get("b2bua")
74:         if isinstance(b2bua_extension, dict):
75:             sanitized_b2bua = dict(b2bua_extension)
76:             for sensitive_key in ("client_session_id", "a_session_id", "auth_scope_id"):
77:                 sanitized_b2bua.pop(sensitive_key, None)
78:             if sanitized_b2bua:
79:                 extensions["b2bua"] = sanitized_b2bua
80:             else:
81:                 extensions.pop("b2bua", None)
82: 
83:         projected_session_id = context.session_id
84:         identity = getattr(context, "b2bua_identity", None)
85:         if isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity):
86:             # Never fall back to A-leg at connector boundary in B2BUA mode.
87:             projected_session_id = identity.b_session_id
88:             if identity.b_seq is not None:
89:                 extensions["b2bua"] = {"b_seq": identity.b_seq}
90: 
91:         return ConnectorRequestContext(
92:             request_id=context.request_id,
93:             session_id=projected_session_id,
94:             client_host=context.client_host,
95:             extensions=extensions,
96:         )
97: 
98:     def _build_canonical_request(
99:         self,
100:         domain_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
101:         processed_messages: Sequence[ChatMessage],
102:         effective_model: str,
103:         identity: IAppIdentityConfig | None,
104:         cancellation_token: SessionKey | None,
105:         cancellation_coordinator: ISessionCancellationCoordinator | None,
106:         context: ConnectorRequestContext | None,
107:         options: dict[str, JsonValue],
108:     ) -> ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest:
109:         """Build ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest from canonical inputs.
110: 
111:         Args:
112:             domain_request: Canonical chat request payload
113:             processed_messages: Typed sequence of processed messages
114:             effective_model: Model identifier after considering any overrides
115:             identity: Application identity configuration for authentication
116:             cancellation_token: Session key for cancellation scoping
117:             cancellation_coordinator: Cancellation coordinator for structural enforcement
118:             context: Connector-facing request context (from projection)
119:             options: JSON-safe container for provider-specific connector options
120: 
121:         Returns:
122:             ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest with all inputs bundled
123: 
124:         Raises:
125:             ValueError: If options dict contains reserved keys that conflict with contract fields
126:         """
127:         # Validate that options dict doesn't contain reserved keys
128:         reserved_keys = {
129:             "context",
130:             "request",
131:             "processed_messages",
132:             "effective_model",
133:             "identity",
134:             "cancellation_token",
135:             "cancellation_coordinator",
136:         }
137:         conflicting_keys = reserved_keys.intersection(options.keys())
138:         if conflicting_keys:
139:             raise ValueError(
140:                 f"Options dict contains reserved keys that conflict with ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest fields: {conflicting_keys}. "
141:                 "These keys are reserved and cannot be used in the options dict."
142:             )
143: 
144:         return ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest(
145:             request=domain_request,
146:             processed_messages=processed_messages,
147:             effective_model=effective_model,
148:             identity=identity,
149:             cancellation_token=cancellation_token,
150:             cancellation_coordinator=cancellation_coordinator,
151:             context=context,
152:             options=options,
153:         )
154: 
155:     @staticmethod
156:     def _is_canonical_request_annotation(annotation: Any) -> bool:
157:         """Return True when annotation accepts ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest."""
158:         if annotation == ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest:
159:             return True
160: 
161:         # Handle string annotations / forward refs.
162:         if (
163:             isinstance(annotation, str)
164:             and "ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest" in annotation
165:         ):
166:             return True
167: 
168:         forward_arg = getattr(annotation, "__forward_arg__", None)
169:         if (
170:             isinstance(forward_arg, str)
171:             and "ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest" in forward_arg
172:         ):
173:             return True
174: 
175:         # Handle unions like ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest | None.
176:         origin = get_origin(annotation)
177:         if origin is not None:
178:             args = get_args(annotation)
179:             if ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest in args:
180:                 return True
181: 
182:         return False
183: 
184:     def _has_canonical_request_normalizer(self, backend: LLMBackend) -> bool:
185:         """Detect strict variadic canonical adapters (e.g., Codex compatibility layer)."""
186:         normalizer = getattr(backend, "_normalize_chat_completions_request", None)
187:         if not callable(normalizer):
188:             return False
189: 
190:         try:
191:             normalizer_sig = inspect.signature(normalizer)
192:         except (TypeError, ValueError):
193:             return False
194: 
195:         normalizer_params = list(normalizer_sig.parameters.values())
196:         has_varargs = any(
197:             p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_POSITIONAL for p in normalizer_params
198:         )
199:         has_varkw = any(
200:             p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in normalizer_params
201:         )
202:         if not (has_varargs and has_varkw):
203:             return False
204: 
205:         return self._is_canonical_request_annotation(normalizer_sig.return_annotation)
206: 
207:     def _is_canonical_backend(self, backend: LLMBackend) -> bool:
208:         """Check if backend implements ICanonicalChatCompletionsBackend.
209: 
210:         Uses structural typing check: verifies that the backend has a
211:         chat_completions method with the canonical signature (first parameter
212:         named "request" with ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest type).
213: 
214:         Handles backward-compatible signatures like:
215:         - chat_completions(request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest)
216:         - chat_completions(request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest, *args, **kwargs)
217: 
218:         Args:
219:             backend: Backend instance to check
220: 
221:         Returns:
222:             True if backend implements canonical API, False otherwise
223:         """
224:         # Check if backend has chat_completions method
225:         if not hasattr(backend, "chat_completions"):
226:             return False
227: 
228:         method = backend.chat_completions
229:         if not callable(method):
230:             return False
231: 
232:         # Get method signature
233:         # Note: inspect.signature on a bound method doesn't include 'self'
234:         try:
235:             sig = inspect.signature(method)
236:         except (ValueError, TypeError):
237:             # If signature inspection fails, assume legacy
238:             return False
239: 
240:         params = list(sig.parameters.values())
241: 
242:         # Canonical path A: explicit canonical-first signature:
243:         #   chat_completions(request: ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest, ...)
244:         # with only optional compatibility parameters after request.
245:         positional_params = [
246:             p
247:             for p in params
248:             if p.kind
249:             in (
250:                 inspect.Parameter.POSITIONAL_ONLY,
251:                 inspect.Parameter.POSITIONAL_OR_KEYWORD,
252:             )
253:         ]
254: 
255:         if not positional_params:
256:             # Canonical path B: strict variadic adapter, e.g. chat_completions(*args, **kwargs)
257:             # that normalizes into ConnectorChatCompletionsRequest via a typed helper.
258:             has_varargs = any(
259:                 p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_POSITIONAL for p in params
260:             )
261:             has_varkw = any(p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in params)
262:             has_required_keyword_only = any(
263:                 p.kind == inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY
264:                 and p.default is inspect.Parameter.empty
265:                 for p in params
266:             )
267:             if has_required_keyword_only:
268:                 return False
269:             return (
270:                 has_varargs
271:                 and has_varkw
272:                 and self._has_canonical_request_normalizer(backend)
273:             )
274: 
275:         request_param = positional_params[0]
276: 
277:         # Parameter name should be "request" for canonical API.
278:         if request_param.name != "request":
279:             return False
280: 
281:         # Require explicit canonical request annotation.
282:         if not self._is_canonical_request_annotation(request_param.annotation):
283:             return False
284: 
285:         # Additional named parameters are allowed only if optional (backward-compat shims).
286:         # Required parameters after `request` indicate legacy/non-canonical shape.
287:         request_param_seen = False
288:         for param in params:
289:             if not request_param_seen and param is request_param:
290:                 request_param_seen = True
291:                 continue
292:             if not request_param_seen:
293:                 continue
294:             if param.kind in (
295:                 inspect.Parameter.VAR_POSITIONAL,
296:                 inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD,
297:             ):
298:                 continue
299:             if param.default is inspect.Parameter.empty:
300:                 return False
301: 
302:         return True
303: 
304:     async def invoke(
305:         self,
306:         backend: LLMBackend,
307:         domain_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
308:         canonical_request: CanonicalChatRequest,
309:         effective_model: str,
310:         identity: IAppIdentityConfig | None,
311:         cancellation_token: SessionKey | None,
312:         cancellation_coordinator: ISessionCancellationCoordinator | None,
313:         context: RequestContext | None,
314:         options: dict[str, JsonValue],
315:     ) -> ResponseEnvelope | StreamingResponseEnvelope:
316:         """Invoke connector with canonical-first dispatch and legacy fallback.
317: 
318:         Builds canonical connector request contract, projects RequestContext to
319:         ConnectorRequestContext, and dispatches to canonical API when available.
320:         Falls back to legacy API with typed domain models (never dicts).
321: 
322:         Args:
323:             backend: Backend instance to invoke
324:             domain_request: Canonical chat request for backend (after preparation)
325:             canonical_request: Canonical chat request (original, for processed_messages)
326:             effective_model: Model identifier after considering any overrides
327:             identity: Application identity configuration for authentication
328:             cancellation_token: Session key for cancellation scoping
329:             cancellation_coordinator: Cancellation coordinator for structural enforcement
330:             context: Request context for correlation/debugging
331:             options: JSON-safe container for provider-specific connector options
332: 
333:         Returns:
334:             Either ResponseEnvelope for non-streaming requests or
335:             StreamingResponseEnvelope for streaming requests
336: 
337:         Raises:
338:             Any exception raised by the backend connector
339:         """
340:         # Project context to connector-facing context
341:         connector_context = self._project_context(context)
342: 
343:         # Check if backend implements canonical API
344:         is_canonical = self._is_canonical_backend(backend)
345: 
346:         if is_canonical:
347:             # Build canonical request contract
348:             connector_request = self._build_canonical_request(
349:                 domain_request=domain_request,
350:                 processed_messages=list(canonical_request.messages),
351:                 effective_model=effective_model,
352:                 identity=identity,
353:                 cancellation_token=cancellation_token,
354:                 cancellation_coordinator=cancellation_coordinator,
355:                 context=connector_context,
356:                 options=options,
357:             )
358: 
359:             # Invoke canonical API
360:             return await backend.chat_completions(connector_request)  # type: ignore[call-arg]
361:         else:
362:             # Legacy path: invoke with typed domain models (never dicts)
363:             # Log legacy path usage for observability with correlation identifiers
364:             correlation_ids = extract_correlation_ids(context)
365:             backend_type = getattr(backend, "backend_type", "unknown")
366:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
367:                 logger.info(
368:                     "Using legacy connector API",
369:                     extra={
370:                         "backend_type": backend_type,
371:                         "session_id": correlation_ids["session_id"],
372:                         "request_id": correlation_ids["request_id"],
373:                     },
374:                 )
375: 
376:             # BOUNDARY EXCEPTION (Requirement 2.7, Design.md line 289):
377:             # This is the ONLY permitted location where `options` (dict[str, JsonValue])
378:             # are re-expanded into `**kwargs` for backward compatibility with legacy
379:             # connectors that expect keyword arguments.
380:             #
381:             # Rationale:
382:             # - Backward compatibility: Legacy connectors use LLMBackend.chat_completions()
383:             #   with permissive **kwargs signatures
384:             # - Constrained surface: Options expansion is confined to this single location
385:             #   in ConnectorInvoker, preventing legacy shapes from leaking into core services
386:             # - JSON-safe: Options are constrained to JsonValue types, ensuring deterministic
387:             #   logging/capture (Requirement 4.3)
388:             #
389:             # Promotion path (Design.md Phase 2/3):
390:             # - Migrate connectors to implement ICanonicalChatCompletionsBackend
391:             # - Use canonical API which receives options as typed dict[str, JsonValue]
392:             # - Deprecate legacy kwargs expansion (Phase 2: warn in logs)
393:             # - Remove legacy kwargs expansion entirely (Phase 3: optional, requires approval)
394:             #
395:             # This exception is time-bounded and documented per Requirement 2.7/3.5.
396:             kwargs: dict[str, Any] = dict(options)
397: 
398:             # Invoke legacy API with canonical domain models
399:             legacy_backend: Any = backend
400:             return cast(
401:                 ResponseEnvelope | StreamingResponseEnvelope,
402:                 await legacy_backend.chat_completions(
403:                     request_data=domain_request,  # Canonical domain model, never dict
404:                     processed_messages=list(canonical_request.messages),  # Typed values
405:                     effective_model=effective_model,
406:                     identity=identity,
407:                     cancellation_token=cancellation_token,
408:                     cancellation_coordinator=cancellation_coordinator,
409:                     **kwargs,  # Options expanded here only
410:                 ),
411:             )
