88:             if (
89:                 skip_static_route
90:                 and context is not None
91:                 and isinstance(context.extensions, dict)
92:             ):
93:                 had_previous_skip_static_route = (
94:                     _SKIP_STATIC_ROUTE_CONTEXT_KEY in context.extensions
95:                 )
96:                 if had_previous_skip_static_route:
97:                     previous_skip_static_route = cast(
98:                         JsonValue | None,
99:                         context.extensions.get(_SKIP_STATIC_ROUTE_CONTEXT_KEY),
100:                     )
101:                 context.extensions[_SKIP_STATIC_ROUTE_CONTEXT_KEY] = True
102: 
103:             try:
104:                 return await self._backend_model_resolver.resolve_target(
105:                     request_to_resolve, context
106:                 )
107:             finally:
108:                 if (
109:                     skip_static_route
110:                     and context is not None
111:                     and isinstance(context.extensions, dict)
112:                 ):
113:                     if not had_previous_skip_static_route:
114:                         context.extensions.pop(_SKIP_STATIC_ROUTE_CONTEXT_KEY, None)
115:                     else:
116:                         context.extensions[_SKIP_STATIC_ROUTE_CONTEXT_KEY] = (
117:                             previous_skip_static_route
118:                         )
119: 
120:         should_route_auxiliary = (
121:             self._auxiliary_router is not None
122:             and self._auxiliary_router.enabled
123:             and self._auxiliary_router.should_route_to_auxiliary(request)
124:         )
125: 
126:         # First, resolve the default target. When we already know this is an
127:         # auxiliary request, skip static_route to avoid clobbering the auxiliary
128:         # backend before the reroute pass.
129:         target = await _resolve_target_with_optional_static_route_skip(
130:             request, skip_static_route=should_route_auxiliary
131:         )
132: 
133:         # Check if this is an auxiliary request that should be routed differently
134:         if should_route_auxiliary and self._auxiliary_router:
135:             auxiliary_backend = self._auxiliary_router.get_auxiliary_backend()
136:             auxiliary_model = self._auxiliary_router.get_auxiliary_model()
137:             original_backend = target.backend
138:             original_model = target.model
139:             auxiliary_selector: str
140:             if auxiliary_model and has_explicit_backend_selector(auxiliary_model):
141:                 auxiliary_selector = auxiliary_model
142:             elif auxiliary_model:
143:                 auxiliary_selector = f"{auxiliary_backend}:{auxiliary_model}"
144:             else:
145:                 auxiliary_selector = f"{auxiliary_backend}:{target.model}"
146: 
147:             auxiliary_extra_body: dict[str, JsonValue] | None = None
148:             if isinstance(request.extra_body, dict):
149:                 auxiliary_extra_body = dict(request.extra_body)
150:             if auxiliary_extra_body is None:
151:                 auxiliary_extra_body = {}
152:             auxiliary_extra_body.pop("backend_type", None)
153:             auxiliary_request = request.model_copy(
154:                 update={
155:                     "model": auxiliary_selector,
156:                     "extra_body": (
157:                         auxiliary_extra_body if auxiliary_extra_body else None
158:                     ),
159:                 }
160:             )
161:             if context is not None:
162:                 context.extensions["call_purpose"] = "auxiliary"
163:                 context.extensions[COMPOSITE_ROUTING_SURFACE_KEY] = (
164:                     RoutingSurface.AUXILIARY.value
165:                 )
166:             target = await _resolve_target_with_optional_static_route_skip(
167:                 auxiliary_request,
168:                 skip_static_route=True,
169:             )
170: 
171:             if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.INFO):
172:                 logger.info(
173:                     "Routing auxiliary request via shared resolver using '%s' -> "
174:                     "'%s:%s' instead of '%s:%s'",
175:                     auxiliary_selector,
176:                     target.backend,
177:                     target.model,
178:                     original_backend,
179:                     original_model,
180:                 )
181: 
182:             if context is not None:
183:                 # IMPORTANT: Auxiliary requests (title/summary generation) must not
184:                 # interfere with the primary conversation session lifecycle (EoS
185:                 # detection, dedup status, fingerprinting).
186:                 #
187:                 # We keep the client-visible session_id unchanged, but run the
188:                 # backend call under a derived internal session id.
189:                 root_session_id = getattr(context, "session_id", None)
190:                 if isinstance(root_session_id, str) and root_session_id:
191:                     auxiliary_purpose = f"{target.backend}:{target.model}"
192:                     operation_key = derive_auxiliary_operation_key(
193:                         context=context,
194:                         request_data=request,
195:                         purpose=auxiliary_purpose,
196:                     )
197:                     auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = 1
198:                     context.extensions["auxiliary_effective_session_id"] = (
199:                         build_auxiliary_effective_session_id(
200:                             root_session_id=root_session_id,
201:                             purpose=auxiliary_purpose,
202:                             operation_key=operation_key,
203:                             attempt_ordinal=auxiliary_attempt_ordinal,
204:                         )
205:                     )
206:                     context.extensions["auxiliary_root_session_id"] = root_session_id
207:                     context.extensions["auxiliary_purpose"] = auxiliary_purpose
208:                     context.extensions["auxiliary_operation_key"] = operation_key
209:                     context.extensions["auxiliary_attempt_ordinal"] = (
210:                         auxiliary_attempt_ordinal
211:                     )
212:                 context.extensions["auxiliary_request"] = True
213:                 context.extensions["auxiliary_original_backend"] = cast(
214:                     JsonValue, original_backend
215:                 )
216:                 context.extensions["auxiliary_original_model"] = cast(
217:                     JsonValue, original_model
218:                 )
219:                 context.extensions["auxiliary_backend"] = cast(
220:                     JsonValue, target.backend
221:                 )
222:                 context.extensions["auxiliary_model"] = cast(JsonValue, target.model)
223:                 context.extensions["call_purpose"] = "auxiliary"
224: 
225:         return target
226: 
227:     def synchronize_request_with_target(
228:         self, request: CanonicalChatRequest, target: BackendTarget
229:     ) -> CanonicalChatRequest:
230:         """Ensure the request payload reflects the resolved backend and model."""
231:         result = self._backend_model_resolver.synchronize_request_with_target(
232:             request, target
233:         )
234:         return cast(CanonicalChatRequest, result)
235: 
236:     async def prepare_backend_request(
237:         self,
238:         request: CanonicalChatRequest,
239:         backend_type: str,
240:         session: ISession | None,
241:         uri_params: dict[str, JsonValue],
242:     ) -> CanonicalChatRequest:
243:         """Apply reasoning config, backend config, and URI parameters to the request."""
244:         domain_request: ChatRequest = request
245: 
246:         # Apply session reasoning configuration if available
247:         # Note: ReasoningConfigApplicator.apply handles exceptions internally
248:         if session is not None:
249:             domain_request = self._reasoning_config_applicator.apply(
250:                 domain_request, session
251:             )
252: 
253:         # Apply backend configuration
254:         if self._backend_config_service:
255:             domain_request = self._backend_config_service.apply_backend_config(
256:                 domain_request, backend_type, cast(AppConfig, self._config)
257:             )
258: 
259:         # Apply URI parameters with precedence resolution
260:         # Note: URIParameterApplicator.apply handles exceptions internally
261:         domain_request = self._uri_parameter_applicator.apply(
262:             domain_request, uri_params, backend_type, session
263:         )
264: 
265:         return cast(CanonicalChatRequest, domain_request)
266: 
267:     @staticmethod
268:     def _normalize_backend_family(backend_type: str) -> str:
269:         normalized = backend_type.strip().lower().replace("_", "-")
270:         if "." in normalized:
271:             normalized = normalized.split(".", 1)[0]
272:         return normalized
273: 
274:     @classmethod
275:     def _requires_non_empty_session_id(cls, backend_type: str) -> bool:
276:         family = cls._normalize_backend_family(backend_type)
277:         return family in _REQUIRES_NON_EMPTY_SESSION_ID_FAMILIES
278: 
279:     @classmethod
280:     def _build_opaque_surrogate_session_id(
281:         cls,
282:         *,
283:         context: RequestContext | None,
284:         backend_type: str,
285:     ) -> str:
286:         normalized_backend = cls._normalize_backend_family(backend_type) or "backend"
287:         request_id = (
288:             str(getattr(context, "request_id", "")).strip()
289:             if context is not None
290:             else ""
291:         )
292:         extensions = (
293:             getattr(context, "extensions", None) if context is not None else None
294:         )
295: 
296:         retry_attempt = 0
297:         auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = 1
298:         if isinstance(extensions, dict):
299:             raw_retry_attempt = extensions.get("retry_attempt")
300:             if isinstance(raw_retry_attempt, int):
301:                 retry_attempt = max(0, raw_retry_attempt)
302:             elif isinstance(raw_retry_attempt, str) and raw_retry_attempt.strip():
303:                 try:
304:                     retry_attempt = max(0, int(raw_retry_attempt.strip()))
305:                 except ValueError:
306:                     retry_attempt = 0
307: 
308:             raw_auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = extensions.get("auxiliary_attempt_ordinal")
309:             if isinstance(raw_auxiliary_attempt_ordinal, int):
310:                 auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = max(1, raw_auxiliary_attempt_ordinal)
311:             elif (
312:                 isinstance(raw_auxiliary_attempt_ordinal, str)
313:                 and raw_auxiliary_attempt_ordinal.strip()
314:             ):
315:                 try:
316:                     auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = max(
317:                         1, int(raw_auxiliary_attempt_ordinal.strip())
318:                     )
319:                 except ValueError:
320:                     auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = max(1, retry_attempt + 1)
321:             else:
322:                 auxiliary_attempt_ordinal = max(1, retry_attempt + 1)
323: 
324:         identity = (
325:             getattr(context, "b2bua_identity", None) if context is not None else None
326:         )
327:         a_session_id = (
328:             str(getattr(identity, "a_session_id", "")).strip()
329:             if isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity)
330:             else ""
331:         )
332:         client_session_id = (
333:             str(getattr(identity, "client_session_id", "")).strip()
334:             if isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity)
335:             else ""
336:         )
337:         auth_scope_id = (
338:             str(getattr(identity, "auth_scope_id", "")).strip()
339:             if isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity)
340:             else ""
341:         )
342: 
343:         seed = "|".join(
344:             [
345:                 normalized_backend,
346:                 request_id,
347:                 str(retry_attempt),
348:                 str(auxiliary_attempt_ordinal),
349:                 a_session_id,
350:                 client_session_id,
351:                 auth_scope_id,
352:             ]
353:         )
354:         digest = hashlib.sha256(seed.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:32]
355:         return f"sur-{normalized_backend}-{digest}"
356: 
357:     def prepare_backend_kwargs(
358:         self,
359:         session_id_for_backend: str | None,
360:         session: ISession | None,
361:         context: RequestContext | None,
362:         backend_type: str,
363:     ) -> dict[str, JsonValue]:
364:         """Prepare kwargs for backend call."""
365: 
366:         backend_call_kwargs: dict[str, JsonValue] = {}
367: 
368:         auxiliary_session_id: str | None = None
369:         extensions = getattr(context, "extensions", None) if context else None
370:         if isinstance(extensions, dict) and bool(extensions.get("auxiliary_request")):
371:             raw_auxiliary_session_id = extensions.get("auxiliary_effective_session_id")
372:             if isinstance(raw_auxiliary_session_id, str):
373:                 normalized_auxiliary_session_id = raw_auxiliary_session_id.strip()
374:                 if normalized_auxiliary_session_id:
375:                     auxiliary_session_id = normalized_auxiliary_session_id
376: 
377:         backend_session_id = session_id_for_backend
378:         identity = getattr(context, "b2bua_identity", None) if context else None
379:         if auxiliary_session_id is not None:
380:             backend_session_id = auxiliary_session_id
381:         elif isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity):
382:             # In B2BUA mode connector/provider correlation must use B-leg identity.
383:             backend_session_id = identity.b_session_id
384:         if (
385:             backend_session_id is None
386:             and isinstance(identity, B2buaIdentity)
387:             and self._requires_non_empty_session_id(backend_type)
388:         ):
389:             backend_session_id = self._build_opaque_surrogate_session_id(
390:                 context=context,
391:                 backend_type=backend_type,
392:             )
393:         if backend_session_id:
394:             backend_call_kwargs["session_id"] = backend_session_id
395: 
396:         if session is not None and hasattr(session, "state"):
397:             try:
398:                 project_value = getattr(session.state, "project", None)
399:                 if isinstance(project_value, str) and project_value:
400:                     backend_call_kwargs["project"] = project_value
401:             except (AttributeError, TypeError) as e:
402:                 # Expected exceptions from attribute access or type checking
403:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
404:                     logger.debug(
405:                         "Failed to extract project value from session state: %s",
406:                         e,
407:                         exc_info=True,
408:                     )
409:             except Exception as e:
410:                 # Unexpected exceptions should be logged at WARNING level for visibility
411:                 logger.warning(
412:                     "Unexpected error extracting project value from session state: %s",
413:                     e,
414:                     exc_info=True,
415:                 )
416:             try:
417:                 project_dir_value = getattr(session.state, "project_dir", None)
418:                 if isinstance(project_dir_value, str) and project_dir_value:
419:                     backend_call_kwargs["project_dir"] = project_dir_value
420:             except (AttributeError, TypeError) as e:
421:                 # Expected exceptions from attribute access or type checking
422:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
423:                     logger.debug(
424:                         "Failed to extract project_dir value from session state: %s",
425:                         e,
426:                         exc_info=True,
427:                     )
428:             except Exception as e:
429:                 # Unexpected exceptions should be logged at WARNING level for visibility
430:                 logger.warning(
431:                     "Unexpected error extracting project_dir value from session state: %s",
432:                     e,
433:                     exc_info=True,
434:                 )
435: 
436:         if context is not None:
437:             try:
438:                 incoming_headers = getattr(context, "headers", None)
439:                 headers_dict: dict[str, JsonValue] | None = None
440: 
441:                 to_dict = getattr(incoming_headers, "to_dict", None)
442:                 if callable(to_dict):
443:                     headers_dict = cast(dict[str, JsonValue], to_dict())
444:                 elif incoming_headers:
445:                     headers_dict = dict(incoming_headers)  # type: ignore[assignment]
446: 
447:                 if headers_dict is not None:
448:                     backend_call_kwargs["incoming_headers"] = headers_dict
449:             except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as e:
450:                 if logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG):
451:                     logger.debug(
452:                         "Failed to extract headers from context: %s",
453:                         e,
454:                         exc_info=True,
455:                     )
456:             except Exception as e:
457:                 logger.warning(
458:                     "Unexpected error extracting headers from context: %s",
459:                     e,
460:                     exc_info=True,
461:                 )
462: 
463:         return backend_call_kwargs
